##############################################################################
# HOSPI OPS - PROCUREMENT, WAREHOUSE, ACCOUNTS, ADMIN : ALL FILES IN ONE PLACE  (version 4)
##############################################################################

HOW TO USE
  1. In your replica Apps Script project, for each FILE below create (or open) a file with the NAME shown
     (type "Script" = .gs file, type "HTML" = HTML file; do NOT type the extension in the editor),
     select everything in it, and paste the text between its marker lines (not the marker lines themselves).
  2. IMPORTANT FOR EVERY HTML FILE: copy the FIRST line and the LAST line too.
        - every board file and DeptShell starts with   <script>   and ends with   </script>
        - Index.html is a full page: it starts with <!DOCTYPE html> and ends with </html>
        - DeptStyles starts with   <style>   and ends with   </style>
     If the first line is missing, the page prints the file's code as text and that tab does not appear.
  3. Files marked REPLACES overwrite the file you already have. Everything else is NEW.
  4. Do NOT copy over the old kanban script (the one with SHEET_NAME = 'Sales_Leads').
  5. Leave Client, Styles, LeadsBoard, RequirementsBoard, QuoteBoard, OrdersBoard and the other Sales .gs files as they are.
  6. In the Users tab set roles: Sales / Procurement / Warehouse / Accounts / Admin (exact spelling). Run runDiagnostics once.
  7. Deploy a new version. Open ...?dept=procurement  ...?dept=warehouse  ...?dept=accounts  ...?dept=admin
     (Accounts people now open the Accounts page, not the Warehouse page.)
  Full notes (what it does, assumptions, smoke test, troubleshooting) are in the README section at the end.

FILE LIST (paste in any order)

  1. Config                   Script REPLACES your existing Config.gs (your settings are kept; PW: lines are the additions)
  2. Main                     Script REPLACES your existing Main.gs (PW: lines are the additions)
  3. Items                    Script REPLACES Items.gs from the earlier version (adds the date-serial fix) - or NEW if you never added it
  4. Procurement              Script REPLACES Procurement.gs from the earlier version (date-serial fix) - or NEW
  5. Warehouse                Script REPLACES Warehouse.gs from the earlier version (date-serial, Done/Left, "Audit adj." fixes) - or NEW
  6. Accounts                 Script NEW (Accounts department: order receipts, purchase receipts, payments)
  7. Admin                    Script NEW (Admin department: overview, users, products, locations)
  8. Index                    HTML   REPLACES your existing Index.html (one template for all five departments)
  9. DeptShell                HTML   REPLACES DeptShell.html from the earlier version (adds Accounts/Admin icons, badges and helpers) - or NEW
 10. DeptStyles               HTML   REPLACES DeptStyles.html from the earlier version (a few more styles) - or NEW
 11. ProcQueueBoard           HTML   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
 12. PurchaseOrdersBoard      HTML   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
 13. SuppliersBoard           HTML   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
 14. ReceivingBoard           HTML   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
 15. PutAwayBoard             HTML   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
 16. DispatchBoard            HTML   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
 17. StockBoard               HTML   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
 18. AuditBoard               HTML   REPLACES AuditBoard.html from the earlier version (now also loads on the Accounts page) - or NEW
 19. OrderReceiptsBoard       HTML   NEW (Accounts)
 20. PurchaseReceiptsBoard    HTML   NEW (Accounts)
 21. PaymentsBoard            HTML   NEW (Accounts)
 22. OverviewBoard            HTML   NEW (Admin)
 23. ProductsBoard            HTML   NEW (Admin)
 24. UsersBoard               HTML   NEW (Admin)
 25. LocationsBoard           HTML   NEW (Admin)
 26. OrdersBoard-stage-fix.txt (optional: 5 small edits to your existing OrdersBoard.html - see the end)

# FILE 2 of 25 :  Main    [Script file]   REPLACES your existing Main.gs (PW: lines are the additions)
# first line of this file is:  /**
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
/**
 * Main.gs - entry points. The browser talks to ONE function: api(name, argsJson).
 * Everything returned is a JSON string, so Dates and other non-serialisable
 * values can never make google.script.run silently return null.
 *
 * CHANGED for Procurement + Warehouse (search "PW:"): doGet picks a department, api() checks the role
 * of each action, methods() merges the Procurement and Warehouse actions, boot() skips Sales-only work
 * for other departments, Auth accepts the new roles.
 */

function doGet(e) {
  var key = 'sales', user = null;
  try { user = Auth.current(); key = Main.pickDept(user, e && e.parameter ? e.parameter.dept : ''); }
  catch (err) { key = 'sales'; }     // not signed in / not in Users: the page shows the reason from boot
  var t = HtmlService.createTemplateFromFile('Index');
  t.cfg = Main.deptConfig(key, user);
  return t.evaluate()
    .setTitle(CONFIG.APP_TITLE + ' - ' + CONFIG.DEPARTMENTS[key].label)
    .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
}

/** Used inside Index.html to pull in the other HTML files. */
function include(name) {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile(name).getContent();
}

/** Single door for the browser. Returns '{"ok":true,"data":...}' or '{"ok":false,"error":"..."}'. */
function api(name, argsJson) {
  var res;
  try {
    var entry = Main.methods()[name];
    if (!entry) throw new Error('Unknown action: ' + name);
    var user = Auth.current();
    // PW: entry = [function, needsLock, roles]. No roles = a Sales action; '*' = everyone; Admin may do anything.
    var roles = entry[2] || CONFIG.SALES_ROLES;
    if (roles !== '*' && user.role !== 'Admin' && roles.indexOf(user.role) < 0) {
      throw new Error('Your role (' + (user.role || 'none') + ') cannot do this.');
    }
    var args = argsJson ? JSON.parse(argsJson) : {};
    var data = entry[1]
      ? Main.locked(function () { try { return entry[0](args, user); } finally { Items.dropCache(); } })   // PW: stock numbers change on every write
      : entry[0](args, user);
    res = { ok: true, data: data === undefined ? null : data };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(name + ' failed: ' + (e && e.stack ? e.stack : e));
    res = { ok: false, error: String(e && e.message ? e.message : e) };
  }
  return JSON.stringify(res);
}

var Main = {
  /** name -> [function(args, user), needsLock, roles?] */
  methods: function () {
    var all = {
      'boot':                 [Main.boot, false, '*'],
      'parties':              [function () { return Parties.lists(); }, false, '*'],
      'products':             [function () { return Catalog.list(); }, false, '*'],
      'photo.upload':         [Photos.upload, true],

      'leads.list':           [LeadsApi.list, false],
      'leads.create':         [LeadsApi.create, true],
      'leads.update':         [LeadsApi.update, true],

      'req.list':             [ReqApi.list, false],
      'req.create':           [ReqApi.create, true],

      'quote.nextNo':         [function () { return { no: QuoteApi.nextNo() }; }, false],
      'quote.openReqs':       [QuoteApi.openReqs, false],
      'quote.create':         [QuoteApi.create, true],
      'quote.createOrder':    [QuoteApi.createOrder, true],
      'quote.list':           [QuoteApi.list, false],
      'quote.get':            [QuoteApi.get, false],
      'quote.pdf':            [QuoteApi.pdf, true],
      'quote.lose':           [QuoteApi.lose, true],
      'quote.reopen':         [QuoteApi.reopen, true],

      'orders.list':          [OrdersApi.list, false],
      'orders.get':           [OrdersApi.get, false],
      'orders.convert':       [OrdersApi.convert, true],
      'orders.pdf':           [OrdersApi.pdf, true],
      'orders.cancel':        [OrdersApi.cancel, true]
    };
    // PW: Procurement, Warehouse, Accounts and Admin actions (Procurement.gs, Warehouse.gs, Accounts.gs, Admin.gs)
    [ProcApi.routes(), WhApi.routes(), AcctApi.routes(), AdminApi.routes()].forEach(function (m) {
      Object.keys(m).forEach(function (k) { all[k] = m[k]; });
    });
    return all;
  },

  /** One write at a time across all users, so numbers and IDs never collide. */
  locked: function (fn) {
    var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    try { lock.waitLock(25000); }
    catch (e) { throw new Error('The system is busy saving someone else\'s work. Please try again in a few seconds.'); }
    try { return fn(); } finally { lock.releaseLock(); }
  },

  // ---------- PW: departments ----------
  /** Departments this role may open. Admin may open all of them. */
  allowedDepts: function (role) {
    return Object.keys(CONFIG.DEPARTMENTS).filter(function (k) {
      return role === 'Admin' || CONFIG.DEPARTMENTS[k].roles.indexOf(role) >= 0;
    });
  },
  /** The department asked for in the URL, or the first one this role may open. */
  pickDept: function (user, want) {
    var ok = Main.allowedDepts(user.role), w = U.low(want);
    if (w && ok.indexOf(w) >= 0) return w;
    if (!ok.length) throw new Error('No department is set up for the role "' + user.role + '".');
    return ok[0];
  },
  /** What Index.html needs: titles, the board list for this role, the files to include, the department menu. */
  deptConfig: function (key, user) {
    var D = CONFIG.DEPARTMENTS, d = D[key], role = user ? user.role : '', base = '';
    try { base = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); } catch (e) { base = ''; }
    var only = d.boardsFor && d.boardsFor[role] ? d.boardsFor[role] : null;
    var menu = Object.keys(D).map(function (k) {
      return { key: k, label: D[k].label, initial: D[k].initial, sub: D[k].sub, url: base + '?dept=' + k,
               enabled: !!user && Main.allowedDepts(role).indexOf(k) >= 0 };
    });
    return {
      key: key, label: d.label, initial: d.initial,
      boards: only || d.boards,
      includes: only ? d.includes.filter(function (f) { return only.indexOf(Main.boardOf(f)) >= 0; }) : d.includes,
      menu: menu
    };
  },
  /** 'AuditBoard' -> 'audit' (the board name each file registers; kept in one place so Accounts can be limited to it) */
  boardOf: function (file) {
    return { LeadsBoard: 'leads', RequirementsBoard: 'requirements', QuoteBoard: 'quote', OrdersBoard: 'orders',
             ProcQueueBoard: 'requirements', PurchaseOrdersBoard: 'purchase', SuppliersBoard: 'suppliers',
             ReceivingBoard: 'receiving', PutAwayBoard: 'putaway', DispatchBoard: 'dispatch', StockBoard: 'stock', AuditBoard: 'audit',
             OrderReceiptsBoard: 'orderpay', PurchaseReceiptsBoard: 'billpay', PaymentsBoard: 'payments',
             OverviewBoard: 'overview', ProductsBoard: 'products', UsersBoard: 'users', LocationsBoard: 'locations' }[file];
  },

  boot: function (a, user) {
    var today = U.isoToday(), poc = '';
    CONFIG.LEAD_POCS.forEach(function (p) { if (U.low(p) === U.low(U.firstWord(user.name))) poc = p; });
    var out = {
      app: CONFIG.APP_TITLE,
      user: user,
      today: today,
      depts: Main.allowedDepts(user.role),
      defaults: {
        followUp: U.addDaysIso(today, CONFIG.FOLLOWUP_DEFAULT_DAYS),
        validUntil: U.addDaysIso(today, CONFIG.QUOTE_VALID_DAYS)
      },
      pocDefault: poc,
      lists: {
        leadSources: CONFIG.LEAD_SOURCES, leadPocs: CONFIG.LEAD_POCS, leadStatuses: CONFIG.LEAD_STATUSES,
        paymentTerms: CONFIG.PAYMENT_TERMS, deliveryTerms: CONFIG.DELIVERY_TERMS, lostReasons: CONFIG.LOST_REASONS,
        paymentModes: CONFIG.PAYMENT_MODES
      }
    };
    // PW: the next quote number is only needed on the Sales screens (it reads the Quotations and Orders tabs)
    if (user.role === 'Sales' || user.role === 'Admin') out.nextQuoteNo = QuoteApi.nextNo();
    return out;
  }
};

/** Who is using the app? Looked up in the Users tab. */
var Auth = {
  current: function () {
    var email = '';
    try { email = Session.getActiveUser().getEmail(); } catch (e) { email = ''; }
    if (!email) { try { email = Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail(); } catch (e2) { email = ''; } }
    email = U.low(email);
    if (!email) {
      throw new Error('Could not identify your Google account. Deploy the web app with "Execute as: User accessing the web app".');
    }
    var me = null;
    Db.readAll('users').forEach(function (r) { if (!me && U.low(r['Email']) === email) me = r; });
    if (!me) throw new Error('Access denied: ' + email + ' is not in the Users tab.');
    var role = U.s(me['Role']);
    if (CONFIG.ALLOWED_ROLES.indexOf(role) < 0) {
      throw new Error('Access denied: your role (' + (role || 'none') + ') cannot use this app.');
    }
    return { email: email, name: U.s(me['Name']) || email, role: role };
  }
};

/** Run this once from the editor (Run > runDiagnostics) before deploying. Read the Execution log. */
function runDiagnostics() {
  var out = [];
  Object.keys(TABLES).forEach(function (key) {
    try {
      var m = Db.meta(key);
      var missing = (REQUIRED_HEADERS[key] || []).filter(function (h) { return !m.cols[Db.norm(h)]; });
      out.push((missing.length ? 'PROBLEM  ' : 'OK       ') + m.def.sheet +
        (missing.length ? ' - missing columns: ' + missing.join(', ') : ' - ' + Db.readAll(key).length + ' rows'));
    } catch (e) {
      out.push('PROBLEM  ' + TABLES[key].sheet + ' - ' + e.message);
    }
  });
  try { var u = Auth.current(); out.push('OK       You are ' + u.email + ' (' + u.role + ')'); }
  catch (e) { out.push('PROBLEM  Sign-in check - ' + e.message); }
  try { out.push('OK       Next quote number: ' + QuoteApi.nextNo()); }
  catch (e) { out.push('PROBLEM  Quote numbering - ' + e.message); }
  try {
    var lg = PdfApi.logos_(true);
    out.push(lg.error ? 'PROBLEM  PDF logos - ' + lg.error + ' (PDFs still work, just without logos)'
      : lg.items.length ? 'OK       PDF logos - ' + lg.items.length + ' found in the template document'
      : 'OK       PDF logos - switched off (LOGO_DOC_ID is blank)');
  } catch (e) { out.push('PROBLEM  PDF logos - ' + e.message); }
  try {
    var n = Object.keys(Catalog.photoLinks()).length;
    out.push('OK       Product photos - ' + n + ' product(s) have a Photo_link (the rest can be uploaded while making a quotation)');
    out.push(CONFIG.PHOTO_FOLDER_ID ? 'OK       Photo folder - ' + DriveApp.getFolderById(CONFIG.PHOTO_FOLDER_ID).getName()
      : 'OK       Photo folder - PHOTO_FOLDER_ID is blank: uploads go to a "' + CONFIG.PHOTO_FOLDER_NAME + '" folder in the uploader\'s own Drive');
  } catch (e) { out.push('PROBLEM  Product photos - ' + e.message); }
  var text = out.join('\n');
  console.log(text);
  return text;
}
# ===== END OF Main =====

##############################################################################
# FILE 3 of 25 :  Items    [Script file]   REPLACES Items.gs from the earlier version (adds the date-serial fix) - or NEW if you never added it
# first line of this file is:  /** Items.gs - the Products tab as a lookup by item code, shared by Procurement and Warehouse. Quantities are in pieces. */
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
/** Items.gs - the Products tab as a lookup by item code, shared by Procurement and Warehouse. Quantities are in pieces. */

var Items = {
  _m: null,
  KEY: 'hospi.items.v1',
  TTL: 120,   // seconds. Names / pieces-per-set rarely change; the stock numbers on it are for display only.

  /** Forget the product map (this run and the shared cache). Called after every write and after a new item is added. */
  dropCache: function () {
    Items._m = null;
    try {
      var c = CacheService.getScriptCache(), n = parseInt(c.get(Items.KEY + '.n'), 10) || 0, keys = [Items.KEY + '.n'];
      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) keys.push(Items.KEY + '.' + i);
      c.removeAll(keys);
    } catch (e) { /* the cache is only a speed-up */ }
  },
  reset: function () { Items.dropCache(); },

  /** { code: { code, name, group, pcs (pieces per set, never below 1), gst (fraction), books, reserved, onOrder, avail, wh, lastRate } } */
  map: function () {
    if (Items._m) return Items._m;
    var rows = Items.fromCache_();
    if (rows) { Items._m = Items.build_(rows); Items._fromCache = true; return Items._m; }
    return Items.load_();
  },
  /** One item by code. If it is not in the (possibly cached) map, read the sheet once more before saying it is missing. */
  get: function (code) {
    var c = U.s(code);
    if (!c) return null;
    var m = Items.map();
    if (!m[c] && Items._fromCache) { Items.dropCache(); m = Items.load_(); }
    return m[c] || null;
  },

  load_: function () {
    var raw = Db.readCols('products', ['Item Code', 'Tally Name', 'Catalog Name', 'Stock Group', 'Pcs / Set', 'GST_Pct',
      'Books Qty (auto)', 'Warehouse Qty (auto)', 'Reserved (auto)', 'On Order (auto)', 'Available (auto)', 'Last Purchase Rate (auto)']);
    var n = function (v) { var x = U.toNum(v); return isNaN(x) ? 0 : x; }, rows = [];
    raw.forEach(function (r) {
      var code = U.s(r['Item Code']);
      if (!code) return;
      var pcs = U.toNum(r['Pcs / Set']), g = U.frac(r['GST_Pct']), last = U.toNum(r['Last Purchase Rate (auto)']);
      rows.push([code, U.s(r['Tally Name']) || U.s(r['Catalog Name']), U.s(r['Stock Group']), pcs >= 1 ? pcs : 1, isNaN(g) ? 0 : g,
                 n(r['Books Qty (auto)']), n(r['Warehouse Qty (auto)']), n(r['Reserved (auto)']), n(r['On Order (auto)']),
                 U.s(r['Available (auto)']) === '' ? '' : n(r['Available (auto)']), isNaN(last) ? '' : last]);
    });
    Items._fromCache = false;
    Items.toCache_(rows);
    Items._m = Items.build_(rows);
    return Items._m;
  },
  build_: function (rows) {
    var m = {};
    rows.forEach(function (a) {
      m[a[0]] = { code: a[0], name: a[1], group: a[2], pcs: a[3], gst: a[4], books: a[5], wh: a[6], reserved: a[7], onOrder: a[8], avail: a[9],
                  lastRate: a[10] === '' ? NaN : a[10] };
    });
    return m;
  },
  fromCache_: function () {
    try {
      var c = CacheService.getScriptCache(), n = parseInt(c.get(Items.KEY + '.n'), 10);
      if (!(n > 0)) return null;
      var keys = [], i, txt = '';
      for (i = 0; i < n; i++) keys.push(Items.KEY + '.' + i);
      var got = c.getAll(keys);
      for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { if (got[keys[i]] === undefined || got[keys[i]] === null) return null; txt += got[keys[i]]; }
      return JSON.parse(txt);
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  },
  toCache_: function (rows) {
    try {
      var all = JSON.stringify(rows), parts = {}, cnt = Math.ceil(all.length / 90000);      // one cache value holds at most 100 KB
      for (var k = 0; k < cnt; k++) parts[Items.KEY + '.' + k] = all.substr(k * 90000, 90000);
      parts[Items.KEY + '.n'] = String(cnt);
      CacheService.getScriptCache().putAll(parts, Items.TTL);
    } catch (e) { /* the cache is only a speed-up */ }
  },

  /** pieces per set for a code (fallback when the item is unknown) */
  pcs: function (code, fallback) {
    var p = code ? Items.map()[U.s(code)] : null;
    return p ? p.pcs : (fallback >= 1 ? fallback : 1);
  },
  /** display name: the product's name, or the free-text description for a new item */
  name: function (code, desc) {
    var p = code ? Items.map()[U.s(code)] : null;
    return p ? (p.name || p.code) : U.s(desc);
  },
  /** the item code as the sheet stores it (number or text); null when the item is not in Products */
  raw: function (code) { return Catalog.rawCode(code); }
};

/** Row helpers shared by Procurement and Warehouse. */
var Rows = {
  group: function (rows, header) {
    var m = {};
    rows.forEach(function (r) { var k = U.s(r[header]); (m[k] = m[k] || []).push(r); });
    return m;
  },
  by: function (rows, header) {
    var m = {};
    rows.forEach(function (r) { m[U.s(r[header])] = r; });
    return m;
  },
  /** "AE/PO/" + the next number (4 digits) after the highest one already in column header */
  nextNo: function (rows, header, prefix) {
    var max = 0;
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      var v = U.s(r[header]), m;
      if (v.indexOf(prefix) === 0 && (m = /(\d+)\s*$/.exec(v))) max = Math.max(max, parseInt(m[1], 10));
    });
    var n = String(max + 1);
    while (n.length < 4) n = '0' + n;
    return prefix + n;
  },
  /** Take a row out of use: empty every column the app may write. The key column goes blank, so readers skip it. */
  wipe: function (key, rowNum) {
    var m = Db.meta(key);
    Db.clear(key, rowNum, m.names.filter(function (n) { return n && Db.writable(m, n); }));
  },
  /** sets can be a fraction (a part-set); keep enough decimals that sets x pieces-per-set gives whole pieces back */
  sets: function (n) { return Math.round((Number(n) + 1e-12) * 1e8) / 1e8; },
  /**
   * A date for a TYPED date column (Requirements > Req Date, Sales Order Lines > Dispatch Date). The sheet refuses a number
   * format on a typed column, and writing a Date object makes it try, so write the date as its serial number: the column
   * shows it in its own date format. iso = 'yyyy-mm-dd'.
   */
  serial: function (iso) {
    var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(U.s(iso));
    return m ? Math.round((Date.UTC(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3]) - Date.UTC(1899, 11, 30)) / 86400000) : '';
  },
  /** GRN Lines > Stickered is a Done / Left drop-down (older rows may hold TRUE / FALSE) */
  stickerOut: function (on) { return on ? 'Done' : 'Left'; },
  stickerIn: function (v) { return v === true || /^(done|true)$/i.test(U.s(v)); },
  byName: function (a, b) { return a.name.toLowerCase() < b.name.toLowerCase() ? -1 : a.name.toLowerCase() > b.name.toLowerCase() ? 1 : 0; },
  matches: function (hay, q) {
    var toks = U.low(q).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean), h = U.low(hay);
    return toks.every(function (t) { return h.indexOf(t) >= 0; });
  },
  ids: function (v) { return (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []).map(U.s).filter(Boolean); }
};
# ===== END OF Items =====

##############################################################################
# FILE 4 of 25 :  Procurement    [Script file]   REPLACES Procurement.gs from the earlier version (date-serial fix) - or NEW
# first line of this file is:  /**
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
/**
 * Procurement.gs - backend for the Procurement screens: purchase queue (accept / deny requirements),
 * purchase orders, suppliers.
 *
 * Tables: Requirements (Status, Supplier, PO ID), Suppliers, Purchase, Purchase Lines.
 * Sending a PO also opens its goods receipt (GRN header + one line per PO line, "Yet to receive") so the
 * Warehouse can see what is coming. "Open Sets (auto)" on Purchase Lines is worked out by the sheet from
 * the GRN lines, so Warehouse receiving is what closes a PO line.
 */

var ProcApi = {
  routes: function () {
    var P = ['Procurement'];
    return {
      'sup.list':       [ProcApi.supList, false, P],
      'sup.save':       [ProcApi.supSave, true, P],
      'pq.list':        [ProcApi.pqList, false, P],
      'pq.counts':      [ProcApi.pqCounts, false, P],
      'pq.decide':      [ProcApi.pqDecide, true, P],
      'pq.setSupplier': [ProcApi.pqSetSupplier, true, P],
      'pq.addStock':    [ProcApi.pqAddStock, true, P],
      'po.list':        [ProcApi.poList, false, P],
      'po.get':         [ProcApi.poGet, false, P],
      'po.save':        [ProcApi.poSave, true, P],
      'po.send':        [function (a, user) { return ProcApi.send_(U.s(a.id), user); }, true, P],
      'po.delete':      [ProcApi.poDelete, true, P],
      'po.pdf':         [ProcApi.poPdf, true, P]
    };
  },

  // ---------- suppliers ----------
  suppliers_: function () {
    var list = [], by = {};
    Db.readAll('suppliers').forEach(function (r) {
      var s = {
        id: U.s(r['Supplier ID']), name: U.s(r['Supplier Company Name']), contact: U.s(r['Contact_Name']), whatsapp: U.s(r['WhatsApp']),
        email: U.s(r['Email']), gstin: U.s(r['GSTIN']), city: U.s(r['City']), address: U.s(r['Address']),
        categories: U.s(r['Product_Categories']), row: r._row
      };
      list.push(s); by[s.id] = s;
    });
    list.sort(Rows.byName);
    return { list: list, by: by };
  },

  supList: function () {
    var sup = ProcApi.suppliers_(), count = {};
    Db.readAll('purchase').forEach(function (r) { var k = U.s(r['Supplier Company Name']); count[k] = (count[k] || 0) + 1; });
    return sup.list.map(function (s) {
      return { id: s.id, name: s.name, contact: s.contact, whatsapp: s.whatsapp, email: s.email, gstin: s.gstin, city: s.city,
               address: s.address, categories: s.categories, pos: count[s.id] || 0 };
    });
  },

  /** args: { id?, name*, contact, whatsapp, email, gstin, city, address, categories } */
  supSave: function (a) {
    var name = U.s(a.name).replace(/\s+/g, ' '), sup = ProcApi.suppliers_(), id = U.s(a.id);
    if (!name) throw new Error('Enter the supplier company name.');
    sup.list.forEach(function (s) { if (s.id !== id && U.low(s.name) === U.low(name)) throw new Error('A supplier called "' + name + '" already exists.'); });
    var email = U.s(a.email), gstin = U.s(a.gstin).toUpperCase();
    if (email && !/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email)) throw new Error('That email address does not look right.');
    if (gstin && !/^[0-9A-Z]{15}$/.test(gstin)) throw new Error('GSTIN must be 15 letters and digits.');
    var obj = {
      'Supplier Company Name': name, 'Contact_Name': U.s(a.contact), 'WhatsApp': U.s(a.whatsapp), 'Email': email, 'GSTIN': gstin,
      'City': U.s(a.city), 'Address': U.s(a.address), 'Product_Categories': U.s(a.categories)
    };
    var fmt = { 'WhatsApp': '@' };
    if (id) {
      if (!sup.by[id]) throw new Error('That supplier no longer exists.');
      Db.update('suppliers', sup.by[id].row, obj, fmt);
      return { id: id };
    }
    var taken = U.idSet(Db.readAll('suppliers'), 'Supplier ID');
    obj['Supplier ID'] = U.newId(taken);
    Db.append('suppliers', [obj], fmt);
    return { id: obj['Supplier ID'] };
  },

  // ---------- purchase queue (Requirements) ----------
  reqRow_: function (r, items, sups, poNos) {
    var code = U.s(r['Item Code']), p = code ? items[code] : null, sid = U.s(r['Supplier']), poId = U.s(r['PO ID']);
    var sets = U.toNum(r['No. of Set']);
    return {
      id: U.s(r['Req ID']), date: U.iso(r['Req Date']), forType: U.s(r['Req for']) || 'Stock',
      party: U.s(r['Party (auto)']) || U.s(r['Customer / Lead Name']), salesman: U.s(r['Salesman']),
      itemCode: code, item: p ? (p.name || code) : U.s(r['Item Description']), isNew: !p,
      pcs: p ? p.pcs : 1, sets: isNaN(sets) ? 0 : sets, needBy: U.iso(r['Need By']), notes: U.s(r['Notes']),
      supplierId: sid, supplier: sups.by[sid] ? sups.by[sid].name : '', state: ReqApi.statusOf_(r),
      poId: poId, poNo: U.s(r['PO no. (auto)']) || poNos[poId] || '', quoteNo: U.s(r['Quote no. (auto)']),
      stock: p ? { wh: p.wh, books: p.books, reserved: p.reserved, onOrder: p.onOrder, avail: p.avail } : null
    };
  },

  /** args: { filter: Awaiting|Accepted|Ordered|Denied|All, search } */
  pqList: function (a) {
    var items = Items.map(), sups = ProcApi.suppliers_(), poNos = {};
    Db.readAll('purchase').forEach(function (r) { poNos[U.s(r['Purchase ID'])] = U.s(r['Purchase No']); });
    var all = Db.readAll('requirements').map(function (r) { return ProcApi.reqRow_(r, items, sups, poNos); });
    var counts = { awaiting: 0, accepted: 0, ordered: 0, denied: 0 };
    all.forEach(function (r) { counts[{ '': 'awaiting', Accepted: 'accepted', Ordered: 'ordered', Denied: 'denied' }[r.state]]++; });
    var want = { Awaiting: '', Accepted: 'Accepted', Ordered: 'Ordered', Denied: 'Denied' }, f = U.s(a.filter) || 'All', q = U.s(a.search);
    var rows = all.filter(function (r) {
      if (want[f] !== undefined && r.state !== want[f]) return false;
      return !q || Rows.matches([r.party, r.item, r.itemCode, r.notes, r.supplier, r.poNo, r.quoteNo, r.forType].join(' '), q);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) {
      if (!x.needBy !== !y.needBy) return x.needBy ? -1 : 1;
      return x.needBy < y.needBy ? -1 : x.needBy > y.needBy ? 1 : (x.date < y.date ? 1 : x.date > y.date ? -1 : 0);
    });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), counts: counts };
  },

  /** Cheap numbers for the tab badge and the bell. */
  pqCounts: function () {
    var c = { awaiting: 0, accepted: 0 };
    Db.readAll('requirements').forEach(function (r) {
      var s = ReqApi.statusOf_(r);
      if (s === '') c.awaiting++; else if (s === 'Accepted') c.accepted++;
    });
    var draft = 0;
    Db.readAll('purchase').forEach(function (r) { if ((U.s(r['Status']) || 'Draft') === 'Draft') draft++; });
    c.draft = draft;
    return c;
  },

  reqsByIds_: function (ids) {
    var by = Rows.by(Db.readAll('requirements'), 'Req ID');
    return ids.filter(function (id) { return by[id]; }).map(function (id) { return by[id]; });
  },

  /**
   * The purchase manager's call. args: { ids, decision: Accepted|Denied|'' (back to the queue), supplierId }
   * Accepting needs a supplier: choosing the supplier is what confirms the acceptance. A requirement that already
   * has a supplier keeps it when none is sent.
   */
  pqDecide: function (a) {
    var d = U.s(a.decision), ids = Rows.ids(a.ids), sid = U.s(a.supplierId);
    if (['Accepted', 'Denied', ''].indexOf(d) < 0) throw new Error('Decision must be Accepted or Denied.');
    if (!ids.length) throw new Error('Pick at least one requirement.');
    if (sid && !ProcApi.suppliers_().by[sid]) throw new Error('That supplier no longer exists.');
    var rows = ProcApi.reqsByIds_(ids).filter(function (r) { return !U.s(r['PO ID']); }), skipped = ids.length - rows.length;
    if (d === 'Accepted') {
      rows.forEach(function (r) { if (!sid && !U.s(r['Supplier'])) throw new Error('Choose the supplier to accept this requirement.'); });
    }
    rows.forEach(function (r) {                       // a requirement already on a purchase order cannot change (skipped above)
      var upd = { 'Status': d };
      if (d === 'Accepted' && sid) upd['Supplier'] = sid;
      Db.update('requirements', r._row, upd);
    });
    return { done: rows.length, skipped: skipped };
  },

  /** args: { ids, supplierId ('' clears) } */
  pqSetSupplier: function (a) {
    var ids = Rows.ids(a.ids), sid = U.s(a.supplierId), done = 0;
    if (!ids.length) throw new Error('Pick at least one requirement.');
    if (sid && !ProcApi.suppliers_().by[sid]) throw new Error('That supplier no longer exists.');
    ProcApi.reqsByIds_(ids).forEach(function (r) {
      if (U.s(r['PO ID'])) return;
      Db.update('requirements', r._row, { 'Supplier': sid }); done++;
    });
    return { done: done };
  },

  /** A stock need raised by Procurement itself (no customer or lead). It joins the queue and waits for the decision like any other. */
  pqAddStock: function (a, user) {
    var code = U.s(a.itemCode), desc = U.s(a.itemDesc), sets = U.toNum(a.sets), raw = '';
    if (code) { raw = Items.raw(code); if (raw === null) throw new Error('Item ' + code + ' is not in Products.'); desc = ''; }
    else if (!desc) throw new Error('Pick an item, or type the name of a new item.');
    if (!(sets > 0)) throw new Error('Enter the quantity.');
    var need = '';
    if (U.s(a.needBy)) { need = U.dateFromIso(a.needBy); if (!need) throw new Error('"Need by" date is not valid.'); }
    var id = U.newId(U.idSet(Db.readAll('requirements'), 'Req ID'));
    Db.append('requirements', [{
      'Req ID': id, 'Req Date': Rows.serial(U.isoToday()), 'Salesman': user.email, 'Need By': need,
      'Notes': U.s(a.notes), 'Item Code': raw, 'Item Description': desc, 'No. of Set': U.r4(sets)
    }]);
    return { id: id };
  },

  // ---------- purchase orders ----------
  lineOut_: function (r, items, reqs, draft) {
    var code = U.s(r['Item Code']), p = code ? items[code] : null, rq = reqs ? reqs[U.s(r['Req ID'])] : null;
    var pcs = U.toNum(r['Pcs / Set']); if (!(pcs >= 1)) pcs = p ? p.pcs : 1;
    var sets = U.toNum(r['No. of Set']); if (isNaN(sets)) sets = 0;
    var rate = U.toNum(r['Rate']); if (isNaN(rate)) rate = 0;
    var g = U.frac(r['GST %']); if (isNaN(g)) g = 0;
    var open = U.s(r['Open Sets (auto)']) === '' || draft ? sets : U.toNum(r['Open Sets (auto)']);
    return {
      id: U.s(r['Line ID']), reqId: U.s(r['Req ID']), itemCode: code, desc: U.s(r['Description']), name: p ? (p.name || code) : U.s(r['Description']),
      supItemCode: U.s(r['Supplier Item Code']), supItemName: U.s(r['Supplier Item Name']), pcs: pcs, sets: sets, rate: rate, gst: g, open: isNaN(open) ? sets : open,
      party: rq ? (U.s(rq['Party (auto)']) || U.s(rq['Customer / Lead Name'])) : ''
    };
  },
  value_: function (l) { var net = l.pcs * l.sets * l.rate; return { net: net, gst: net * l.gst }; },

  /** args: { filter: All|Draft|Sent|Awaiting|Received, search } */
  poList: function (a) {
    var sups = ProcApi.suppliers_(), items = Items.map(), lines = Rows.group(Db.readAll('purchaseLines'), 'Purchase ID');
    var f = U.s(a.filter) || 'All', q = U.s(a.search), today = U.isoToday(), counts = { draft: 0, awaiting: 0, received: 0 };
    var all = Db.readAll('purchase').map(function (r) {
      var id = U.s(r['Purchase ID']), status = U.s(r['Status']) || 'Draft', draft = status === 'Draft';
      var ls = (lines[id] || []).map(function (x) { return ProcApi.lineOut_(x, items, null, draft); });
      var sets = 0, open = 0, net = 0, gst = 0, recv = '';
      ls.forEach(function (l) { sets += l.sets; open += l.open; var v = ProcApi.value_(l); net += v.net; gst += v.gst; });
      var delivery = U.iso(r['Delivery Date']);
      if (!draft) recv = sets > 0 && open <= 1e-9 ? 'Received' : (open < sets - 1e-9 ? 'Part received' : 'Awaiting delivery');
      var sid = U.s(r['Supplier Company Name']);
      return {
        id: id, no: U.s(r['Purchase No']), date: U.iso(r['Purchase Date']), supplierId: sid, supplier: sups.by[sid] ? sups.by[sid].name : '',
        delivery: delivery, terms: U.s(r['Payment Terms']), status: status, recv: recv, pdf: /^https?:\/\//i.test(U.s(r['PO PDF'])) ? U.s(r['PO PDF']) : '',
        lines: ls.length, sets: U.r4(sets), open: U.r4(open), value: U.r2(net + gst),
        late: !!(recv && recv !== 'Received' && delivery && delivery < today), createdBy: U.s(r['Created By'])
      };
    });
    all.forEach(function (p) { if (p.status === 'Draft') counts.draft++; else if (p.recv === 'Received') counts.received++; else counts.awaiting++; });
    var rows = all.filter(function (p) {
      if (f === 'Draft' && p.status !== 'Draft') return false;
      if (f === 'Sent' && p.status !== 'Sent') return false;
      if (f === 'Awaiting' && !(p.status === 'Sent' && p.recv !== 'Received')) return false;
      if (f === 'Received' && p.recv !== 'Received') return false;
      return !q || Rows.matches([p.no, p.supplier, p.terms, p.status, p.recv].join(' '), q);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return x.no < y.no ? 1 : x.no > y.no ? -1 : 0; });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), counts: counts };
  },

  find_: function (id) {
    var po = Rows.by(Db.readAll('purchase'), 'Purchase ID')[U.s(id)];
    if (!po) throw new Error('That purchase order was not found.');
    return po;
  },

  /** args: { id } */
  poGet: function (a) {
    var po = ProcApi.find_(a.id), items = Items.map(), sups = ProcApi.suppliers_(), id = U.s(po['Purchase ID']);
    var reqs = Rows.by(Db.readAll('requirements'), 'Req ID'), status = U.s(po['Status']) || 'Draft';
    var lines = Db.readAll('purchaseLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Purchase ID']) === id; })
      .map(function (r) { return ProcApi.lineOut_(r, items, reqs, status === 'Draft'); });
    var grn = null;
    Db.readAll('grn').forEach(function (g) { if (U.s(g['Purchase ID']) === id) grn = { id: U.s(g['GRN ID']), no: U.s(g['GRN No']), status: U.s(g['Status']) }; });
    var sid = U.s(po['Supplier Company Name']);
    return {
      id: id, no: U.s(po['Purchase No']), date: U.iso(po['Purchase Date']), supplierId: sid, supplier: sups.by[sid] ? sups.by[sid].name : '',
      delivery: U.iso(po['Delivery Date']), terms: U.s(po['Payment Terms']), status: status,
      pdf: /^https?:\/\//i.test(U.s(po['PO PDF'])) ? U.s(po['PO PDF']) : '', lines: lines, grn: grn
    };
  },

  clean_: function (raw, items) {
    if (!Array.isArray(raw) || !raw.length) throw new Error('Add at least one item.');
    if (raw.length > 80) throw new Error('Too many lines (max 80).');
    return raw.map(function (l, i) {
      var n = 'Line ' + (i + 1) + ': ', code = U.s(l.itemCode), desc = U.s(l.desc);
      if (!code && !desc) throw new Error(n + 'pick an item, or type the name of a new item.');
      var prod = code ? Items.get(code) : null;
      if (code && !prod) throw new Error(n + 'item ' + code + ' is not in Products.');
      var pcs = U.toNum(l.pcs); if (!(pcs >= 1)) pcs = prod ? prod.pcs : 1;
      var sets = U.toNum(l.sets), rate = U.toNum(l.rate), g = U.s(l.gst) === '' ? (prod ? prod.gst : 0) : U.frac(l.gst);
      if (!(sets > 0)) throw new Error(n + 'enter the quantity in sets.');
      if (isNaN(rate)) rate = 0;
      if (rate < 0) throw new Error(n + 'the rate cannot be negative.');
      if (isNaN(g) || g < 0 || g > 1) throw new Error(n + 'GST must be between 0 and 100 %.');
      return { id: U.s(l.id), reqId: U.s(l.reqId), code: code, desc: code ? '' : desc, supCode: U.s(l.supItemCode), supName: U.s(l.supItemName),
               pcs: pcs, sets: Rows.sets(sets), rate: U.r4(rate), gst: g };
    });
  },

  /**
   * Create or change a DRAFT purchase order. args: { id?, supplierId, date, delivery, terms, lines: [{ id?, reqId?, itemCode, desc,
   * supItemCode, supItemName, pcs, sets, rate, gst (fraction) }], send }
   */
  poSave: function (a, user) {
    var sid = U.s(a.supplierId), sups = ProcApi.suppliers_(), items = Items.map();
    if (!sups.by[sid]) throw new Error('Pick the supplier from the list.');
    var clean = ProcApi.clean_(a.lines, items), poRows = Db.readAll('purchase'), po = null, id, no;
    var delivery = '', date = U.today();
    if (U.s(a.delivery)) { delivery = U.dateFromIso(a.delivery); if (!delivery) throw new Error('The delivery date is not valid.'); }
    if (U.s(a.date)) { date = U.dateFromIso(a.date); if (!date) throw new Error('The PO date is not valid.'); }
    if (a.id) {
      po = Rows.by(poRows, 'Purchase ID')[U.s(a.id)];
      if (!po) throw new Error('That purchase order was not found.');
      if ((U.s(po['Status']) || 'Draft') !== 'Draft') throw new Error('A purchase order that is already sent cannot be changed.');
      id = U.s(po['Purchase ID']); no = U.s(po['Purchase No']);
    } else { id = U.newId(U.idSet(poRows, 'Purchase ID')); no = Rows.nextNo(poRows, 'Purchase No', CONFIG.PO_PREFIX); }

    var reqRows = Db.readAll('requirements'), reqs = Rows.by(reqRows, 'Req ID'), seen = {};
    clean.forEach(function (l) {
      if (!l.reqId) return;
      var rq = reqs[l.reqId];
      if (!rq) throw new Error('A requirement on this order no longer exists. Remove that line and add it again.');
      if (seen[l.reqId]) throw new Error('The same requirement is on this order twice.');
      seen[l.reqId] = true;
      if (U.s(rq['PO ID']) && U.s(rq['PO ID']) !== id) throw new Error('A requirement on this order is already on another purchase order.');
      if (!U.s(rq['PO ID']) && U.s(rq['Status']) !== 'Accepted') throw new Error('Only accepted requirements can be ordered. Accept it in the Purchase queue first.');
    });

    var head = { 'Supplier Company Name': sid, 'Delivery Date': delivery, 'Payment Terms': U.s(a.terms), 'Purchase Date': date };
    if (po) Db.update('purchase', po._row, head);
    else {
      head['Purchase ID'] = id; head['Purchase No'] = no; head['Status'] = 'Draft'; head['Created By'] = user.email; head['Created At'] = new Date();
      Db.append('purchase', [head], { 'Created At': 'dd/mm/yyyy hh:mm:ss' });
    }

    var tL = Db.readAll('purchaseLines'), old = tL.filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Purchase ID']) === id; });
    var oldBy = Rows.by(old, 'Line ID'), taken = U.idSet(tL, 'Line ID'), keep = {}, fresh = [];
    clean.forEach(function (l) {
      var row = {
        'Req ID': l.reqId, 'Purchase ID': id, 'Item Code': l.code ? Items.raw(l.code) : '', 'Description': l.desc, 'Supplier Item Code': l.supCode,
        'Supplier Item Name': l.supName, 'Pcs / Set': l.pcs, 'No. of Set': l.sets, 'Rate': l.rate, 'GST %': l.gst
      };
      if (l.id && oldBy[l.id]) { keep[l.id] = true; Db.update('purchaseLines', oldBy[l.id]._row, row, { 'GST %': '0.00%' }); }
      else { row['Line ID'] = U.newId(taken); fresh.push(row); }
    });
    if (fresh.length) Db.append('purchaseLines', fresh, { 'GST %': '0.00%' });

    var gone = old.filter(function (r) { return !keep[U.s(r['Line ID'])]; });
    gone.forEach(function (r) {                                 // a removed line puts its requirement back in the queue
      var rq = reqs[U.s(r['Req ID'])];
      if (rq && U.s(rq['PO ID']) === id) { Db.update('requirements', rq._row, { 'PO ID': '' }); rq['PO ID'] = ''; }
    });
    clean.forEach(function (l) { if (l.reqId) Db.update('requirements', reqs[l.reqId]._row, { 'PO ID': id, 'Supplier': sid }); });
    gone.forEach(function (r) { Rows.wipe('purchaseLines', r._row); });

    var out = { id: id, no: no, sent: false, warning: '', pdf: '' };
    if (a.send) { var s = ProcApi.send_(id, user); out.sent = true; out.warning = s.warning; out.pdf = s.pdf; }
    return out;
  },

  /** Draft -> Sent. Only Sent POs count as On Order. Also opens the GRN the Warehouse will receive against. */
  send_: function (id, user) {
    var po = ProcApi.find_(id);
    if ((U.s(po['Status']) || 'Draft') !== 'Draft') throw new Error('This purchase order is already sent.');
    var lines = Db.readAll('purchaseLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Purchase ID']) === id; });
    if (!lines.length) throw new Error('Add at least one item before sending.');
    if (!ProcApi.suppliers_().by[U.s(po['Supplier Company Name'])]) throw new Error('Pick the supplier before sending.');
    var grns = Db.readAll('grn');
    if (!grns.some(function (g) { return U.s(g['Purchase ID']) === id; })) {
      var gid = U.newId(U.idSet(grns, 'GRN ID')), glRows = Db.readAll('grnLines'), taken = U.idSet(glRows, 'Line ID');
      Db.append('grn', [{ 'GRN ID': gid, 'GRN No': Rows.nextNo(grns, 'GRN No', CONFIG.GRN_PREFIX), 'Purchase ID': id,
                          'Supplier': U.s(po['Supplier Company Name']), 'Status': 'Yet to receive' }]);
      Db.append('grnLines', lines.map(function (r) {
        var code = U.s(r['Item Code']);
        return {
          'Line ID': U.newId(taken), 'GRN ID': gid, 'Purchase Line ID': U.s(r['Line ID']), 'Item Code': code ? Items.raw(code) : '',
          'Description': U.s(r['Description']), 'Supplier item code': U.s(r['Supplier Item Code']),
          'Expected pcs / set': U.toNum(r['Pcs / Set']) >= 1 ? U.toNum(r['Pcs / Set']) : Items.pcs(code, 1),
          'Expected No. of Sets': U.toNum(r['No. of Set']), 'Damaged Qty': 0, 'Stickered': Rows.stickerOut(false), 'Put Away': false
        };
      }));
    }
    Db.update('purchase', po._row, { 'Status': 'Sent' });
    var out = { id: id, no: U.s(po['Purchase No']), pdf: '', warning: '' };
    try { out.pdf = ProcApi.makePdf_(id, po._row); }
    catch (e) { out.warning = 'The order is sent, but its PDF could not be made: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e); }
    return out;
  },

  /** args: { id } - a draft only; its requirements go back to the queue */
  poDelete: function (a) {
    var po = ProcApi.find_(a.id), id = U.s(po['Purchase ID']);
    if ((U.s(po['Status']) || 'Draft') !== 'Draft') throw new Error('Only a draft purchase order can be deleted.');
    Db.readAll('requirements').forEach(function (r) { if (U.s(r['PO ID']) === id) Db.update('requirements', r._row, { 'PO ID': '' }); });
    Db.readAll('purchaseLines').forEach(function (r) { if (U.s(r['Purchase ID']) === id) Rows.wipe('purchaseLines', r._row); });
    Rows.wipe('purchase', po._row);
    return { id: id };
  },

  // ---------- PO PDF ----------
  folder_: function () {
    if (CONFIG.PO_PDF_FOLDER_ID) return DriveApp.getFolderById(CONFIG.PO_PDF_FOLDER_ID);
    var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(CONFIG.PO_PDF_FOLDER_NAME);
    return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(CONFIG.PO_PDF_FOLDER_NAME);
  },
  html_: function (po) {
    var N = PdfApi.NAVY, C = CONFIG.COMPANY, e = U.esc, sup = ProcApi.suppliers_().by[po.supplierId] || {}, f = 'font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;';
    var net = 0, gst = 0, cs = PdfApi.box_('padding:5px 4px;font-size:9px;');
    var rows = po.lines.map(function (l) {
      var v = ProcApi.value_(l); net += v.net; gst += v.gst;
      return '<tr><td valign="top" style="' + cs + '">' + (e(l.itemCode) || '-') + '</td>' +
        '<td valign="top" style="' + cs + '">' + e(l.name) + (l.supItemCode ? '<br><span style="font-size:7.5px;color:#555555">Supplier code: ' + e(l.supItemCode) + '</span>' : '') + '</td>' +
        '<td valign="top" align="center" style="' + cs + '">' + U.r4(l.pcs) + '</td><td valign="top" align="center" style="' + cs + '">' + U.r4(l.sets) + '</td>' +
        '<td valign="top" align="center" style="' + cs + '">' + U.r4(l.pcs * l.sets) + '</td><td valign="top" align="right" style="' + cs + '">' + PdfApi.money_(l.rate) + '</td>' +
        '<td valign="top" align="center" style="' + cs + '">' + PdfApi.plain_(l.gst * 100) + '</td><td valign="top" align="right" style="' + cs + '">' + U.inr(v.net + v.gst) + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('');
    var cols = [['Item Code', 11], ['Description', 31], ['Pcs / Set', 8], ['Sets', 8], ['Total Pcs', 9], ['Rate / pc', 12], ['GST %', 7], ['Amount', 14]];
    var banner = '<table width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="border-collapse:collapse"><tr><td align="center" bgcolor="' + N + '" style="background-color:' + N +
      ';color:#ffffff;padding:12px 10px;border:1px solid ' + N + '"><div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:bold;padding-bottom:6px">' + e(C.name) + '</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:9.5px;padding-bottom:5px">Address: ' + e(C.address) + '</div><div style="font-size:9.5px;padding-bottom:5px">Contact No-: ' + e(C.phone) + '</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:9.5px;padding-bottom:7px">GST NO : ' + e(C.gstin) + '</div><div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:bold">PURCHASE ORDER</div></td></tr></table>';
    var to = '<b>To:</b> ' + e(sup.name || po.supplier) + (sup.address ? '<br>' + e(sup.address) : '') + (sup.city ? '<br>' + e(sup.city) : '') +
      (sup.gstin ? '<br><b>GST NO:</b> ' + e(sup.gstin) : '') + (sup.contact ? '<br><b>Attn:</b> ' + e(sup.contact) + (sup.whatsapp ? ' (' + e(sup.whatsapp) + ')' : '') : '');
    var right = '<b>PO No :-</b> ' + e(po.no) + '<br><b>PO Date :-</b> ' + e(PdfApi.date_(po.date)) + '<br><b>Delivery by :-</b> ' + e(PdfApi.date_(po.delivery) || 'as agreed') +
      '<br><b>Payment terms :-</b> ' + e(po.terms || 'As agreed');
    var info = '<table width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="border-collapse:collapse"><tr><td width="62%" valign="top" style="' + PdfApi.box_('padding:8px 9px;font-size:10px;line-height:1.7') + '">' + to +
      '</td><td width="38%" valign="top" style="' + PdfApi.box_('padding:8px 9px;font-size:10px;line-height:1.7') + '">' + right + '</td></tr></table>';
    var head = '<tr>' + cols.map(function (c) { return PdfApi.th_(c[0], c[1]); }).join('') + '</tr>';
    var lab = PdfApi.box_('padding:6px 8px;font-size:10px;font-weight:bold;');
    var totals = '<table width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="border-collapse:collapse"><tr><td width="75%" style="' + lab + 'text-align:right">Total Amount (incl. GST)</td><td width="25%" align="right" style="' + lab + '">' + U.inr(net + gst) + '</td></tr></table>';
    var note = '<div style="font-size:9px;padding:6px 0">Rates are per piece and exclude GST unless stated. Please quote this PO number on your invoice, packing list and delivery challan.</div>';
    var gap = '<div style="height:10px;font-size:1px;line-height:1px">&nbsp;</div>';
    return '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><style>body{margin:16px;color:#1f2328;' + f + '}td,th,div,span,b{' + f + '}tr{page-break-inside:avoid}</style></head><body>' +
      banner + gap + info + gap + '<table width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="border-collapse:collapse;table-layout:fixed">' + head + rows + '</table>' + gap + totals + note + '</body></html>';
  },
  /** Make the PO PDF, store its link on the Purchase row, return the link. */
  makePdf_: function (id, rowNum) {
    var po = ProcApi.poGet({ id: id }), name = po.no.replace(/[\/\\:*?"<>|]/g, '-') + '.pdf';
    var blob = Utilities.newBlob(ProcApi.html_(po), 'text/html', 'po.html').getAs('application/pdf').setName(name);
    var folder = ProcApi.folder_(), olds = folder.getFilesByName(name);
    while (olds.hasNext()) olds.next().setTrashed(true);
    var file = folder.createFile(blob);
    if (CONFIG.PDF_SHARE_LINK) file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    var url = file.getUrl();
    Db.update('purchase', rowNum || ProcApi.find_(id)._row, { 'PO PDF': url });
    return url;
  },
  /** args: { id } - (re)make the PDF and return its link */
  poPdf: function (a) {
    var po = ProcApi.find_(a.id);
    if ((U.s(po['Status']) || 'Draft') === 'Draft') throw new Error('Send the purchase order first - the PDF is made when it is sent.');
    return { url: ProcApi.makePdf_(U.s(po['Purchase ID']), po._row) };
  }
};
# ===== END OF Procurement =====

##############################################################################
# FILE 5 of 25 :  Warehouse    [Script file]   REPLACES Warehouse.gs from the earlier version (date-serial, Done/Left, "Audit adj." fixes) - or NEW
# first line of this file is:  /**
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
/**
 * Warehouse.gs - backend for the Warehouse screens: receiving (GRN), put-away, dispatch, stock, audit.
 *
 * Stock Movement is the stock ledger and is ADD-ONLY. It is written in exactly three places:
 *   put-away      +pieces at a location  (type "Put-away", Ref ID = GRN line)
 *   dispatch      -pieces at the pick location (type "Dispatch", Ref ID = sales order line)
 *   audit adj     +/- variance when Accounts approves a count (type "Audit adj.", Ref ID = count)
 * Quantities in the ledger, Damaged Qty, Put_Qty and Counted Qty are PIECES; order and GRN line quantities are SETS.
 *
 * Sales order flow (the sheet's own Stage (auto) formula): To pick -> To pack -> Book transport -> Ready to dispatch -> Dispatched.
 *   Picked / Packed are line flags, Transport Booked is an order flag, Status = Dispatched closes the order.
 */

var Ledger = {
  /** { 'itemCode|location': pieces } from the whole ledger */
  balances: function () {
    var m = {};
    Db.readCols('movement', ['Item Code', 'Location', 'Qty']).forEach(function (r) {
      var k = U.s(r['Item Code']) + '|' + U.s(r['Location']), q = U.toNum(r['Qty']);
      m[k] = U.r4((m[k] || 0) + (isNaN(q) ? 0 : q));
    });
    return m;
  },
  at: function (bal, code, loc) { return bal[U.s(code) + '|' + U.s(loc)] || 0; },
  /** [{ loc, qty }] where the item sits now, biggest first */
  where: function (bal, code) {
    var out = [], pre = U.s(code) + '|';
    Object.keys(bal).forEach(function (k) { if (k.indexOf(pre) === 0 && bal[k] > 1e-9) out.push({ loc: k.slice(pre.length), qty: bal[k] }); });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.qty - a.qty; });
    return out;
  },
  total: function (bal, code) {
    var t = 0, pre = U.s(code) + '|';
    Object.keys(bal).forEach(function (k) { if (k.indexOf(pre) === 0) t += bal[k]; });
    return U.r4(t);
  },
  /** entries: [{ code, loc, qty (signed pieces), type, ref }] */
  add: function (entries, user) {
    if (!entries.length) return;
    var taken = U.idSet(Db.readCols('movement', ['Move ID']), 'Move ID'), now = new Date();
    Db.append('movement', entries.map(function (e) {
      var raw = Items.raw(e.code);
      if (raw === null) throw new Error('Item ' + e.code + ' is not in Products, so its stock cannot be recorded.');
      return { 'Move ID': U.newId(taken), 'Ref ID': e.ref, 'Moved At': now, 'Item Code': raw, 'Qty': U.r4(e.qty), 'Location': e.loc, 'Type': e.type, 'Moved By': user.email };
    }), { 'Moved At': 'dd/mm/yyyy hh:mm:ss' });
  }
};

var WhApi = {
  routes: function () {
    var W = ['Warehouse'], WA = ['Warehouse', 'Accounts'];
    return {
      'loc.list':        [WhApi.locList, false, '*'],
      'wh.counts':       [WhApi.counts, false, W],
      'grn.list':        [WhApi.grnList, false, W],
      'grn.get':         [WhApi.grnGet, false, W],
      'grn.receive':     [WhApi.grnReceive, true, W],
      'put.list':        [WhApi.putList, false, W],
      'put.do':          [WhApi.putDo, true, W],
      'stock.list':      [WhApi.stockList, false, W],
      'stock.history':   [WhApi.stockHistory, false, W],
      'prod.create':     [WhApi.prodCreate, true, W],
      'audit.list':      [WhApi.auditList, false, WA],
      'audit.create':    [WhApi.auditCreate, true, W],
      'audit.review':    [WhApi.auditReview, true, ['Accounts']],
      'disp.list':       [WhApi.dispList, false, W],
      'disp.get':        [WhApi.dispGet, false, W],
      'disp.save':       [WhApi.dispSave, true, W],
      'disp.book':       [WhApi.dispBook, true, W],
      'disp.dispatch':   [WhApi.dispDispatch, true, W]
    };
  },

  // ---------- locations ----------
  locList: function () {
    return Db.readAll('locations').map(function (r) {
      return { code: U.s(r['Location Code']), desc: U.s(r['Description']), floor: U.s(r['Floor']), zone: U.s(r['Zone']) };
    }).filter(function (l) { return l.code; });
  },
  locSet_: function () { var m = {}; WhApi.locList().forEach(function (l) { m[l.code] = l; }); return m; },
  stamp_: function (v) {            // a date-time cell -> 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm'
    return v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v.getTime()) ? Utilities.formatDate(v, Db.tz(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') : U.iso(v);
  },

  // ---------- receiving (GRN) ----------
  grnStatus_: function (g) { return U.s(g['Status']) === 'Received' || U.s(g['Received Date']) !== '' ? 'Received' : 'Yet to receive'; },

  lineOut_: function (r, items) {
    var code = U.s(r['Item Code']), p = code ? items[code] : null;
    var expPer = U.toNum(r['Expected pcs / set']), recPer = U.toNum(r['Received pcs / set']);
    var per = recPer >= 1 ? recPer : (expPer >= 1 ? expPer : (p ? p.pcs : 1));
    var expSets = U.toNum(r['Expected No. of Sets']), recRaw = U.s(r['Received No. of Sets']);
    var rec = recRaw === '' ? '' : U.toNum(recRaw), dmg = U.toNum(r['Damaged Qty']), sq = U.toNum(r['Stickered Qty']), pq = U.toNum(r['Put_Qty']);
    if (rec !== '' && isNaN(rec)) rec = '';
    return {
      id: U.s(r['Line ID']), grnId: U.s(r['GRN ID']), purchaseLineId: U.s(r['Purchase Line ID']), itemCode: code, desc: U.s(r['Description']),
      name: p ? (p.name || code) : U.s(r['Description']), supItemCode: U.s(r['Supplier item code']), per: per,
      expSets: isNaN(expSets) ? 0 : expSets, recSets: rec, damaged: isNaN(dmg) ? 0 : dmg,
      stickered: Rows.stickerIn(r['Stickered']), stickeredQty: isNaN(sq) ? 0 : sq, putAway: r['Put Away'] === true, putQty: isNaN(pq) ? 0 : pq,
      location: U.s(r['Location']), goodPcs: rec === '' ? 0 : Math.max(0, U.r4(rec * per - (isNaN(dmg) ? 0 : dmg))), unplanned: !U.s(r['Purchase Line ID']), row: r._row
    };
  },

  /** args: { filter: 'To receive' | 'Received' | 'All', search } */
  grnList: function (a) {
    var sups = ProcApi.suppliers_(), items = Items.map(), today = U.isoToday();
    var pos = Rows.by(Db.readAll('purchase'), 'Purchase ID'), lines = Rows.group(Db.readAll('grnLines'), 'GRN ID');
    var f = U.s(a.filter) || 'To receive', q = U.s(a.search), counts = { toReceive: 0, received: 0 };
    var all = Db.readAll('grn').map(function (g) {
      var id = U.s(g['GRN ID']), ls = (lines[id] || []).map(function (r) { return WhApi.lineOut_(r, items); });
      var expPcs = 0, recPcs = 0, pending = 0, po = pos[U.s(g['Purchase ID'])], sid = U.s(g['Supplier']) || (po ? U.s(po['Supplier Company Name']) : '');
      ls.forEach(function (l) {
        expPcs += l.expSets * l.per;
        if (l.recSets !== '') recPcs += l.recSets * l.per;
        if (l.recSets !== '' && l.recSets > 0 && !l.putAway) pending++;
      });
      var status = WhApi.grnStatus_(g), delivery = po ? U.iso(po['Delivery Date']) : '';
      return {
        id: id, no: U.s(g['GRN No']), purchaseId: U.s(g['Purchase ID']), poNo: po ? U.s(po['Purchase No']) : '', supplierId: sid,
        supplier: sups.by[sid] ? sups.by[sid].name : '', invoiceNo: U.s(g['Supplier Invoice No']), invoiceDate: U.iso(g['Invoice Date']),
        receivedDate: U.iso(g['Received Date']), cartons: U.s(g['No. of cartons received']), status: status, lines: ls.length,
        expPcs: U.r4(expPcs), recPcs: U.r4(recPcs), pending: pending, delivery: delivery, late: status !== 'Received' && !!delivery && delivery < today
      };
    });
    all.forEach(function (g) { if (g.status === 'Received') counts.received++; else counts.toReceive++; });
    var rows = all.filter(function (g) {
      if (f === 'To receive' && g.status === 'Received') return false;
      if (f === 'Received' && g.status !== 'Received') return false;
      return !q || Rows.matches([g.no, g.poNo, g.supplier, g.invoiceNo].join(' '), q);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) {
      if (f !== 'Received' && !x.delivery !== !y.delivery) return x.delivery ? -1 : 1;     // soonest expected first
      return x.no < y.no ? 1 : x.no > y.no ? -1 : 0;
    });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), counts: counts };
  },

  /** args: { id } */
  grnGet: function (a) {
    var g = Rows.by(Db.readAll('grn'), 'GRN ID')[U.s(a.id)];
    if (!g) throw new Error('That goods receipt was not found.');
    var items = Items.map(), id = U.s(g['GRN ID']), po = Rows.by(Db.readAll('purchase'), 'Purchase ID')[U.s(g['Purchase ID'])];
    var sups = ProcApi.suppliers_(), sid = U.s(g['Supplier']) || (po ? U.s(po['Supplier Company Name']) : '');
    var lines = Db.readAll('grnLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['GRN ID']) === id; }).map(function (r) { return WhApi.lineOut_(r, items); });
    return {
      id: id, no: U.s(g['GRN No']), poNo: po ? U.s(po['Purchase No']) : '', supplier: sups.by[sid] ? sups.by[sid].name : '',
      delivery: po ? U.iso(po['Delivery Date']) : '', invoiceNo: U.s(g['Supplier Invoice No']), invoiceDate: U.iso(g['Invoice Date']),
      receivedDate: U.iso(g['Received Date']), cartons: U.s(g['No. of cartons received']), status: WhApi.grnStatus_(g), lines: lines
    };
  },

  /**
   * Save the count. args: { id, invoiceNo, invoiceDate, cartons, receivedDate, markReceived,
   *   lines: [{ id, recSets ('' = not counted), per, damaged, stickered, stickeredQty, itemCode? }],
   *   newLines: [{ itemCode?, desc, supItemCode, per, recSets, damaged }] (an item that arrived but was not on the PO) }
   * "Fill Received Date only when goods arrive": markReceived sets Received Date + Status = Received.
   */
  grnReceive: function (a, user) {
    var g = Rows.by(Db.readAll('grn'), 'GRN ID')[U.s(a.id)];
    if (!g) throw new Error('That goods receipt was not found.');
    var id = U.s(g['GRN ID']), items = Items.map(), all = Db.readAll('grnLines'), mine = all.filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['GRN ID']) === id; });
    var by = Rows.by(mine, 'Line ID'), invDate = '', recDate = '';
    if (U.s(a.invoiceDate)) { invDate = U.dateFromIso(a.invoiceDate); if (!invDate) throw new Error('The invoice date is not valid.'); }
    if (a.markReceived) { recDate = U.dateFromIso(U.s(a.receivedDate) || U.isoToday()); if (!recDate) throw new Error('The received date is not valid.'); }

    // 1. check everything first, so nothing is half-saved
    var updates = {}, counted = {};
    (a.lines || []).forEach(function (l, i) {
      var r = by[U.s(l.id)], n = 'Line ' + (i + 1) + ': ';
      if (!r) throw new Error(n + 'this line is not on this goods receipt any more. Close and open it again.');
      if (r['Put Away'] === true) return;                                  // already in a location: the count is final
      var sets = U.s(l.recSets) === '' ? '' : U.toNum(l.recSets);
      if (sets !== '' && !(sets >= 0)) throw new Error(n + 'enter the received quantity.');
      var cur = WhApi.lineOut_(r, items), per = U.toNum(l.per); if (!(per >= 1)) per = cur.per;
      var dmg = U.toNum(l.damaged); if (isNaN(dmg)) dmg = 0;
      if (dmg < 0) throw new Error(n + 'damaged quantity cannot be negative.');
      if (sets !== '' && dmg > sets * per + 1e-9) throw new Error(n + 'damaged pieces are more than the pieces received.');
      var sq = U.toNum(l.stickeredQty); if (isNaN(sq) || sq < 0) sq = 0;
      var obj = {
        'Received No. of Sets': sets === '' ? '' : Rows.sets(sets), 'Received pcs / set': per, 'Damaged Qty': U.r4(dmg),
        'Stickered': Rows.stickerOut(l.stickered === true), 'Stickered Qty': l.stickered === true ? U.r4(sq) : 0
      };
      if (!U.s(r['Item Code']) && U.s(l.itemCode)) {
        var raw = Items.raw(U.s(l.itemCode));
        if (raw === null) throw new Error(n + 'item ' + U.s(l.itemCode) + ' is not in Products.');
        obj['Item Code'] = raw;
      }
      updates[U.s(l.id)] = { row: r._row, obj: obj };
      counted[U.s(l.id)] = sets === '' ? 0 : sets;
    });
    var fresh = (a.newLines || []).map(function (l, i) {
      var n = 'New item ' + (i + 1) + ': ', code = U.s(l.itemCode), desc = U.s(l.desc), sets = U.toNum(l.recSets), per = U.toNum(l.per), dmg = U.toNum(l.damaged);
      if (code && Items.raw(code) === null) throw new Error(n + 'item ' + code + ' is not in Products.');
      if (!code && !desc) throw new Error(n + 'pick the item, or type its name.');
      if (!(sets > 0)) throw new Error(n + 'enter the received quantity.');
      if (!(per >= 1)) per = code ? Items.pcs(code, 1) : 1;
      if (isNaN(dmg) || dmg < 0) dmg = 0;
      if (dmg > sets * per + 1e-9) throw new Error(n + 'damaged pieces are more than the pieces received.');
      return { code: code, desc: code ? '' : desc, sup: U.s(l.supItemCode), per: per, sets: U.r4(sets), dmg: U.r4(dmg) };
    });
    if (a.markReceived) {
      var any = fresh.length > 0;
      mine.forEach(function (r) {
        var cs = counted[U.s(r['Line ID'])];
        var sets = cs !== undefined ? cs : (U.s(r['Received No. of Sets']) === '' ? 0 : U.toNum(r['Received No. of Sets']));
        if (sets > 0) any = true;
      });
      if (!any) throw new Error('Enter the received quantity of at least one item before marking the goods as received.');
    }

    // 2. write
    var ct = U.toNum(a.cartons);
    Db.update('grn', g._row, { 'Supplier Invoice No': U.s(a.invoiceNo), 'Invoice Date': invDate, 'No. of cartons received': isNaN(ct) ? U.s(a.cartons) : ct });
    Object.keys(updates).forEach(function (k) { Db.update('grnLines', updates[k].row, updates[k].obj); });
    if (fresh.length) {
      var taken = U.idSet(all, 'Line ID');
      Db.append('grnLines', fresh.map(function (l) {
        return {
          'Line ID': U.newId(taken), 'GRN ID': id, 'Item Code': l.code ? Items.raw(l.code) : '', 'Description': l.desc, 'Supplier item code': l.sup,
          'Received pcs / set': l.per, 'Received No. of Sets': l.sets, 'Damaged Qty': l.dmg, 'Stickered': Rows.stickerOut(false), 'Put Away': false
        };
      }));
    }
    if (a.markReceived) Db.update('grn', g._row, { 'Received Date': recDate, 'Received By': user.email, 'Status': 'Received' });
    return { id: id, status: a.markReceived ? 'Received' : WhApi.grnStatus_(g) };
  },

  // ---------- put-away ----------
  /** Lines of received goods that still need a location. args: { search } */
  putList: function (a) {
    var items = Items.map(), bal = Ledger.balances(), grns = Rows.by(Db.readAll('grn'), 'GRN ID'), sups = ProcApi.suppliers_(), q = U.s(a.search);
    var rows = [];
    Db.readAll('grnLines').forEach(function (r) {
      var l = WhApi.lineOut_(r, items), g = grns[l.grnId];
      if (!g || WhApi.grnStatus_(g) !== 'Received' || l.putAway || !(l.recSets > 0) || !(l.goodPcs > 0)) return;
      var sid = U.s(g['Supplier']), out = {
        id: l.id, grnId: l.grnId, grnNo: U.s(g['GRN No']), supplier: sups.by[sid] ? sups.by[sid].name : '', receivedDate: U.iso(g['Received Date']),
        itemCode: l.itemCode, name: l.name, per: l.per, goodPcs: l.goodPcs, damaged: l.damaged, stickered: l.stickered, stickeredQty: l.stickeredQty,
        planned: l.location, where: l.itemCode ? Ledger.where(bal, l.itemCode).slice(0, 3) : []
      };
      if (!q || Rows.matches([out.grnNo, out.supplier, out.itemCode, out.name].join(' '), q)) rows.push(out);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return x.receivedDate < y.receivedDate ? -1 : x.receivedDate > y.receivedDate ? 1 : (x.grnNo < y.grnNo ? -1 : 1); });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS) };
  },

  /**
   * Put goods in a location. args: { lineId, location, pcs ('' = all), itemCode? (when the line has none), stickered? }
   * Putting away only part of a line SPLITS it: this line keeps what was put away, a new line carries the rest
   * (the received sets add up to the same total, so the PO line's Open Sets does not change).
   */
  putDo: function (a, user) {
    var tbl = Db.readAll('grnLines'), r = Rows.by(tbl, 'Line ID')[U.s(a.lineId)];
    if (!r) throw new Error('That line was not found.');
    if (r['Put Away'] === true) throw new Error('This line is already put away.');
    var g = Rows.by(Db.readAll('grn'), 'GRN ID')[U.s(r['GRN ID'])];
    if (!g || WhApi.grnStatus_(g) !== 'Received') throw new Error('Mark the goods as received first (Receiving tab).');
    var items = Items.map(), code = U.s(r['Item Code']) || U.s(a.itemCode);
    if (!code) throw new Error('This line has no item code yet. Pick the item first.');
    if (!Items.get(code)) throw new Error('Item ' + code + ' is not in Products. Add it first (Stock tab > New item).');
    var loc = U.s(a.location);
    if (!WhApi.locSet_()[loc]) throw new Error('Pick a location from the list.');
    var L = WhApi.lineOut_(r, items);
    if (!(L.recSets > 0)) throw new Error('Count the goods first (Receiving tab).');
    var good = L.goodPcs, put = U.s(a.pcs) === '' ? good : U.toNum(a.pcs);
    if (!(put > 0)) throw new Error('Enter how many pieces to put away.');
    if (put > good + 1e-6) throw new Error('Only ' + good + ' pcs can be put away on this line.');
    var per = L.per, split = good - put > 1e-6;
    var upd = { 'Put Away': true, 'Put Away By': user.email, 'Put_Qty': U.r4(put), 'Location': loc };
    if (!U.s(r['Item Code'])) upd['Item Code'] = Items.raw(code);
    if (a.stickered === true) upd['Stickered'] = Rows.stickerOut(true);
    var newRow = 0;
    if (split) {
      upd['Received No. of Sets'] = Rows.sets((put + L.damaged) / per);        // the damaged pieces stay with the part that is put away
      upd['Received pcs / set'] = per;
      newRow = Db.append('grnLines', [{
        'Line ID': U.newId(U.idSet(tbl, 'Line ID')), 'GRN ID': U.s(r['GRN ID']), 'Purchase Line ID': U.s(r['Purchase Line ID']), 'Item Code': Items.raw(code),
        'Description': U.s(r['Description']), 'Supplier item code': U.s(r['Supplier item code']), 'Expected pcs / set': r['Expected pcs / set'],
        'Received pcs / set': per, 'Received No. of Sets': Rows.sets((good - put) / per), 'Damaged Qty': 0,
        'Stickered': Rows.stickerOut(L.stickered || a.stickered === true), 'Put Away': false
      }])[0];
    }
    try {
      Ledger.add([{ code: code, loc: loc, qty: put, type: 'Put-away', ref: U.s(r['Line ID']) }], user);
      Db.update('grnLines', r._row, upd);
    } catch (e) {
      if (newRow) Rows.wipe('grnLines', newRow);                            // undo the split; nothing else has changed yet
      throw e;
    }
    return { putAway: U.r4(put), location: loc, split: split, left: split ? U.r4(good - put) : 0 };
  },

  // ---------- stock (read-only view of the ledger) ----------
  /** args: { filter: 'In warehouse' | 'Mismatch' | 'Short' | 'All', search } */
  stockList: function (a) {
    var items = Items.map(), bal = Ledger.balances(), at = {}, f = U.s(a.filter) || 'In warehouse', q = U.s(a.search), maxCode = 0;
    Object.keys(bal).forEach(function (k) {
      var i = k.indexOf('|'), code = k.slice(0, i);
      if (Math.abs(bal[k]) > 1e-9) (at[code] = at[code] || []).push({ loc: k.slice(i + 1), qty: bal[k] });
    });
    var counts = { inWh: 0, mismatch: 0, short: 0 }, rows = [];
    Object.keys(items).forEach(function (code) {
      var p = items[code], locs = (at[code] || []).sort(function (x, y) { return y.qty - x.qty; }), wh = 0;
      locs.forEach(function (l) { wh += l.qty; });
      wh = U.r4(wh);
      if (/^\d+$/.test(code)) maxCode = Math.max(maxCode, parseInt(code, 10));
      var mismatch = Math.abs(p.books - wh) > 0.5 && (p.books > 0 || wh !== 0), short = p.avail !== '' && p.avail < 0;
      if (wh !== 0) counts.inWh++;
      if (mismatch) counts.mismatch++;
      if (short) counts.short++;
      if (f === 'In warehouse' && wh === 0) return;
      if (f === 'Mismatch' && !mismatch) return;
      if (f === 'Short' && !short) return;
      var hay = [code, p.name, p.group, locs.map(function (l) { return l.loc; }).join(' ')].join(' ');
      if (q && !Rows.matches(hay, q)) return;
      rows.push({ code: code, name: p.name, group: p.group, pcs: p.pcs, books: p.books, wh: wh, reserved: p.reserved, onOrder: p.onOrder,
                  avail: p.avail, locs: locs });
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return x.code.length - y.code.length || (x.code < y.code ? -1 : x.code > y.code ? 1 : 0); });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, 200), counts: counts, nextCode: String(maxCode + 1) };
  },

  /** args: { code } - the ledger lines of one item, newest first */
  stockHistory: function (a) {
    var code = U.s(a.code), rows = [];
    Db.readAll('movement').forEach(function (r) {
      if (U.s(r['Item Code']) !== code) return;
      rows.push({ at: WhApi.stamp_(r['Moved At']), qty: U.toNum(r['Qty']), loc: U.s(r['Location']), type: U.s(r['Type']), by: U.s(r['Moved By']), ref: U.s(r['Ref ID']), row: r._row });
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return y.row - x.row; });
    return { code: code, name: Items.name(code, ''), rows: rows.slice(0, 80), total: rows.length };
  },

  /** A new item, so goods that are not in Products yet can be put away. args: { code, name, pcs, group, gst (percent) } */
  prodCreate: function (a) {
    var code = U.s(a.code), name = U.s(a.name).replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    if (!code) throw new Error('Enter the item code.');
    if (!name) throw new Error('Enter the item name.');
    if (Items.raw(code) !== null) throw new Error('Item code ' + code + ' already exists.');
    var pcs = U.toNum(a.pcs); if (!(pcs >= 1)) pcs = 1;
    var gst = U.toNum(a.gst), obj = {
      'Item Code': /^\d+$/.test(code) ? Number(code) : code, 'Tally Name': name, 'Stock Group': U.s(a.group) || 'Uncategorised', 'Pcs / Set': pcs
    };
    if (!isNaN(gst)) { if (gst < 0 || gst > 100) throw new Error('GST must be between 0 and 100 %.'); obj['GST_Pct'] = gst / 100; }
    Db.append('products', [obj], { 'GST_Pct': '0.00%' });
    Catalog._codes = null; Items.dropCache();
    return { code: code };
  },

  // ---------- audit counts ----------
  /** args: { filter: Pending|Approved|Rejected|All, search } */
  auditList: function (a) {
    var items = Items.map(), bal = Ledger.balances(), f = U.s(a.filter) || 'Pending', q = U.s(a.search), counts = { pending: 0 };
    var all = Db.readAll('audit').map(function (r) {
      var code = U.s(r['Item Code']), loc = U.s(r['Location']), st = U.s(r['Status']) || 'Pending', counted = U.toNum(r['Counted Qty']), photo = U.s(r['Photo']);
      var sys = code && st === 'Pending' ? Ledger.at(bal, code, loc) : null;
      return {
        id: U.s(r['Count ID']), at: WhApi.stamp_(r['Counted At']), itemCode: code, desc: U.s(r['Description']), name: Items.name(code, r['Description']),
        loc: loc, counted: isNaN(counted) ? 0 : counted, by: U.s(r['Counted By']), status: st,
        system: sys, variance: sys === null ? null : U.r4((isNaN(counted) ? 0 : counted) - sys),
        photo: /^https?:\/\//i.test(photo) ? photo : '', hasPhoto: !!photo, reviewedBy: U.s(r['Reviewed By']), reviewedOn: U.iso(r['Reviewed On']), row: r._row
      };
    });
    all.forEach(function (c) { if (c.status === 'Pending') counts.pending++; });
    var rows = all.filter(function (c) {
      if (f !== 'All' && c.status !== f) return false;
      return !q || Rows.matches([c.itemCode, c.name, c.loc, c.by].join(' '), q);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return y.row - x.row; });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), counts: counts };
  },

  /** args: { itemCode?, desc?, location, qty (pieces), photo: { name, data (base64 JPEG) }? } */
  auditCreate: function (a, user) {
    var code = U.s(a.itemCode), desc = U.s(a.desc), loc = U.s(a.location), qty = U.toNum(a.qty), raw = '';
    if (code) { raw = Items.raw(code); if (raw === null) throw new Error('Item ' + code + ' is not in Products.'); desc = Items.name(code, ''); }
    else if (!desc) throw new Error('Pick the item, or type what you counted.');
    if (!WhApi.locSet_()[loc]) throw new Error('Pick the location from the list.');
    if (isNaN(qty) || qty < 0) throw new Error('Enter the counted quantity in pieces (0 if the shelf is empty).');
    var id = U.newId(U.idSet(Db.readAll('audit'), 'Count ID')), photo = '';
    if (a.photo && U.s(a.photo.data)) photo = WhApi.savePhoto_(id, a.photo);
    Db.append('audit', [{
      'Count ID': id, 'Counted At': new Date(), 'Item Code': raw, 'Description': desc, 'Location': loc, 'Counted Qty': U.r4(qty),
      'Photo': photo, 'Counted By': user.email, 'Status': 'Pending'
    }], { 'Counted At': 'dd/mm/yyyy hh:mm:ss' });
    return { id: id };
  },
  savePhoto_: function (id, p) {
    var data = U.s(p.data).replace(/^data:[^,]*,/, '').replace(/\s+/g, '');
    if (data.length > 5500000) throw new Error('That picture is too large (limit about 4 MB).');
    var bytes = Utilities.base64Decode(data);
    if (Photos.sniff_(bytes) === '') throw new Error('Please use a JPG, PNG or GIF picture.');
    var folder;
    if (CONFIG.AUDIT_PHOTO_FOLDER_ID) folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(CONFIG.AUDIT_PHOTO_FOLDER_ID);
    else { var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(CONFIG.AUDIT_PHOTO_FOLDER_NAME); folder = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(CONFIG.AUDIT_PHOTO_FOLDER_NAME); }
    var name = id + '.' + U.s(p.name).replace(/[^\w.-]+/g, '_').slice(-40);
    return folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(bytes, Photos.sniff_(bytes), name)).getUrl();
  },

  /** Accounts' decision. args: { id, decision: Approved|Rejected, itemCode? (when the count has none) } */
  auditReview: function (a, user) {
    var r = Rows.by(Db.readAll('audit'), 'Count ID')[U.s(a.id)], d = U.s(a.decision);
    if (!r) throw new Error('That count was not found.');
    if (['Approved', 'Rejected'].indexOf(d) < 0) throw new Error('Decision must be Approved or Rejected.');
    if ((U.s(r['Status']) || 'Pending') !== 'Pending') throw new Error('This count has already been reviewed.');
    var code = U.s(r['Item Code']) || U.s(a.itemCode), loc = U.s(r['Location']), upd = { 'Status': d, 'Reviewed By': user.email, 'Reviewed On': U.today() };
    var out = { status: d, adjusted: 0, system: null };
    if (d === 'Approved') {
      if (!code) throw new Error('This count has no item code. Pick the item before approving.');
      if (Items.raw(code) === null) throw new Error('Item ' + code + ' is not in Products.');
      if (!WhApi.locSet_()[loc]) throw new Error('The location on this count is not in the Locations tab.');
      var sys = Ledger.at(Ledger.balances(), code, loc), counted = U.toNum(r['Counted Qty']), diff = U.r4((isNaN(counted) ? 0 : counted) - sys);
      if (!U.s(r['Item Code'])) upd['Item Code'] = Items.raw(code);
      if (Math.abs(diff) > 1e-9) Ledger.add([{ code: code, loc: loc, qty: diff, type: 'Audit adj.', ref: U.s(r['Count ID']) }], user);
      out.adjusted = diff; out.system = sys;
    }
    Db.update('audit', r._row, upd);
    return out;
  },

  // ---------- dispatch (pick, pack, book transport, dispatch) ----------
  line2_: function (r, items) {
    var code = U.s(r['Item_Code']), p = code ? items[code] : null, pcs = U.toNum(r['Pcs / Set']);
    if (!(pcs >= 1)) pcs = p ? p.pcs : 1;
    var sets = U.toNum(r['No. of set']); if (isNaN(sets)) sets = 0;
    var dq = U.toNum(r['Dispatched Qty']); if (isNaN(dq)) dq = 0;
    var done = r['Dispatched'] === true, open = done ? 0 : Math.max(0, U.r4(sets - dq));
    return {
      id: U.s(r['Line ID']), orderId: U.s(r['Order ID']), itemCode: code, desc: U.s(r['Description']), name: p ? (p.name || code) : U.s(r['Description']),
      pcs: pcs, sets: sets, dispatchedSets: done ? sets : dq, open: open, pickFrom: U.s(r['Pick From']), picked: r['Picked'] === true,
      packed: r['Packed'] === true, dispatched: done, dispatchDate: U.iso(r['Dispatch Date']), row: r._row
    };
  },
  /** the sheet's Stage (auto), worked out here too in case the cell has not recalculated yet */
  stage_: function (order, ls) {
    var st = U.s(order['Status']);
    if (st === 'Cancelled' || st === 'Dispatched') return st;
    if (!ls.length) return 'No lines';
    var picked = ls.filter(function (l) { return l.picked; }).length, packed = ls.filter(function (l) { return l.packed; }).length;
    if (picked < ls.length) return 'To pick';
    if (packed < ls.length) return 'To pack';
    return order['Transport Booked'] === true ? 'Ready to dispatch' : 'Book transport';
  },

  /** args: { filter: 'Open' | 'Ready' | 'Dispatched' | 'All', search } */
  dispList: function (a) {
    var items = {}, lines = Rows.group(Db.readAll('orderLines'), 'Order ID'), f = U.s(a.filter) || 'Open', q = U.s(a.search);   // counts only: no product read needed
    var stages = {}, all = Db.readAll('orders').map(function (o) {
      var id = U.s(o['Order ID']), ls = (lines[id] || []).map(function (r) { return WhApi.line2_(r, items); });
      var stage = U.s(o['Stage (auto)']) || WhApi.stage_(o, ls), sets = 0, open = 0;
      ls.forEach(function (l) { sets += l.sets; open += l.open; });
      return {
        id: id, no: U.s(o['Order No.']), date: U.iso(o['Order Date']), customer: U.s(o['Customer (auto)']), delivery: U.s(o['Delivery Terms']),
        status: U.s(o['Status']), stage: stage, lines: ls.length, picked: ls.filter(function (l) { return l.picked; }).length,
        packed: ls.filter(function (l) { return l.packed; }).length, done: ls.filter(function (l) { return l.dispatched || (l.sets > 0 && l.open <= 0); }).length,
        booked: o['Transport Booked'] === true, transporter: U.s(o['Transporter']), lr: U.s(o['LR No.']), dispatchDate: U.iso(o['Dispatch Date']),
