        sets: U.r4(sets), openSets: U.r4(open), row: o._row
      };
    });
    all.forEach(function (o) { if (o.stage !== 'Dispatched' && o.stage !== 'Cancelled') stages[o.stage] = (stages[o.stage] || 0) + 1; });
    var rows = all.filter(function (o) {
      var closed = o.stage === 'Dispatched' || o.stage === 'Cancelled';
      if (f === 'Open' && closed) return false;
      if (f === 'Ready' && o.stage !== 'Ready to dispatch') return false;
      if (f === 'Dispatched' && o.stage !== 'Dispatched') return false;
      return !q || Rows.matches([o.no, o.customer].join(' '), q);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return y.date > x.date ? 1 : y.date < x.date ? -1 : y.row - x.row; });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), stages: stages };
  },

  order_: function (id) {
    var o = Rows.by(Db.readAll('orders'), 'Order ID')[U.s(id)];
    if (!o) throw new Error('Order not found.');
    return o;
  },

  /** args: { orderId } */
  dispGet: function (a) {
    var o = WhApi.order_(a.orderId), items = Items.map(), id = U.s(o['Order ID']), bal = Ledger.balances();
    var ls = Db.readAll('orderLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Order ID']) === id; }).map(function (r) {
      var l = WhApi.line2_(r, items);
      l.stock = l.itemCode ? Ledger.where(bal, l.itemCode) : [];
      l.onHand = l.itemCode ? Ledger.total(bal, l.itemCode) : 0;
      l.atPick = l.pickFrom && l.itemCode ? Ledger.at(bal, l.itemCode, l.pickFrom) : 0;
      return l;
    });
    return {
      id: id, no: U.s(o['Order No.']), date: U.iso(o['Order Date']), customer: U.s(o['Customer (auto)']), delivery: U.s(o['Delivery Terms']),
      status: U.s(o['Status']), stage: U.s(o['Stage (auto)']) || WhApi.stage_(o, ls), booked: o['Transport Booked'] === true,
      transporter: U.s(o['Transporter']), lr: U.s(o['LR No.']), dispatchDate: U.iso(o['Dispatch Date']), lines: ls
    };
  },

  mustBeOpen_: function (o) {
    var st = U.s(o['Status']);
    if (st === 'Cancelled') throw new Error('This order was cancelled.');
    if (st === 'Dispatched') throw new Error('This order is already dispatched.');
  },

  /** Pick / pack. args: { orderId, lines: [{ id, pickFrom, picked, packed }] } */
  dispSave: function (a) {
    var o = WhApi.order_(a.orderId), items = Items.map(), id = U.s(o['Order ID']), locs = WhApi.locSet_(), bal = Ledger.balances();
    WhApi.mustBeOpen_(o);
    var mine = Rows.by(Db.readAll('orderLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Order ID']) === id; }), 'Line ID'), ups = [], warnings = [];
    (a.lines || []).forEach(function (x) {
      var r = mine[U.s(x.id)];
      if (!r) throw new Error('A line on this screen is not on this order any more. Close and open it again.');
      var l = WhApi.line2_(r, items), from = U.s(x.pickFrom), picked = x.picked === true, packed = x.packed === true;
      if (l.dispatched || (l.sets > 0 && l.open <= 0)) return;                // already shipped: leave it alone
      var n = (l.name || 'Item') + ': ';
      if (from && !locs[from]) throw new Error(n + 'pick-from "' + from + '" is not in the Locations tab.');
      if (picked && !from) throw new Error(n + 'choose where it is picked from.');
      if (packed && !picked) throw new Error(n + 'pick it before packing it.');
      if (!picked && packed) packed = false;
      if (picked && l.itemCode && from) {
        var have = Ledger.at(bal, l.itemCode, from), need = U.r4(l.open * l.pcs);
        if (have + 1e-9 < need) warnings.push(n + 'only ' + have + ' pcs recorded at ' + from + ' (needs ' + need + ').');
      }
      ups.push({ row: r._row, obj: { 'Pick From': from, 'Picked': picked, 'Packed': packed } });
    });
    ups.forEach(function (u) { Db.update('orderLines', u.row, u.obj); });
    return { saved: ups.length, warnings: warnings };
  },

  /** Book (or un-book) the transport. args: { orderId, booked, transporter, lrNo } */
  dispBook: function (a) {
    var o = WhApi.order_(a.orderId), items = Items.map(), id = U.s(o['Order ID']), booked = a.booked !== false, tr = U.s(a.transporter);
    WhApi.mustBeOpen_(o);
    var ls = Db.readAll('orderLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Order ID']) === id; }).map(function (r) { return WhApi.line2_(r, items); });
    if (booked) {
      if (!ls.length) throw new Error('This order has no lines.');
      if (ls.some(function (l) { return !l.packed && !(l.dispatched || (l.sets > 0 && l.open <= 0)); })) throw new Error('Pick and pack every item before booking the transport.');
      if (!tr) throw new Error('Enter the transporter you booked.');
    } else if (ls.some(function (l) { return l.dispatchedSets > 0; })) throw new Error('Part of this order has already gone out, so the booking cannot be undone.');
    var upd = { 'Transport Booked': booked, 'Transporter': booked ? tr : '' };
    if (U.s(a.lrNo) !== '' || !booked) upd['LR No.'] = booked ? U.s(a.lrNo) : '';
    Db.update('orders', o._row, upd);
    return { booked: booked };
  },

  /**
   * Hand the goods over. args: { orderId, lines: [{ id, sets }], lrNo, transporter, date }
   * Needs: every shipped line picked + packed, transport booked, enough stock at the pick location.
   * Writes a negative "Dispatch" movement per line; the line's Dispatched Qty adds up (partial shipments), and when
   * nothing is left on the order its Status becomes Dispatched.
   */
  dispDispatch: function (a, user) {
    var o = WhApi.order_(a.orderId), items = Items.map(), id = U.s(o['Order ID']);
    WhApi.mustBeOpen_(o);
    if (o['Transport Booked'] !== true) throw new Error('Book the transport first.');
    var dispIso = U.s(a.date) || U.isoToday(), date = U.dateFromIso(dispIso);
    if (!date) throw new Error('The dispatch date is not valid.');
    var allRows = Db.readAll('orderLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Order ID']) === id; }), mine = Rows.by(allRows, 'Line ID');
    var bal = Ledger.balances(), need = {}, plan = [], warnings = [];
    (a.lines || []).forEach(function (x) {
      var sets = U.toNum(x.sets);
      if (!(sets > 0)) return;
      var r = mine[U.s(x.id)];
      if (!r) throw new Error('A line on this screen is not on this order any more. Close and open it again.');
      var l = WhApi.line2_(r, items), n = (l.name || 'Item') + ': ';
      if (l.dispatched || l.open <= 0) throw new Error(n + 'already dispatched.');
      if (!l.picked || !l.packed) throw new Error(n + 'pick and pack it first.');
      if (sets > l.open + 1e-9) throw new Error(n + 'only ' + l.open + ' sets are left to dispatch.');
      var pcs = U.r4(sets * l.pcs);
      if (l.itemCode) {
        if (!l.pickFrom) throw new Error(n + 'choose where it is picked from.');
        var k = l.itemCode + '|' + l.pickFrom;
        need[k] = U.r4((need[k] || 0) + pcs);
        if (need[k] > (bal[k] || 0) + 1e-9) throw new Error(n + 'only ' + (bal[k] || 0) + ' pcs are recorded at ' + l.pickFrom + ', but ' + need[k] + ' are going out. Fix the stock first (put-away or audit).');
      } else warnings.push(n + 'no item code, so no stock was reduced.');
      plan.push({ l: l, sets: U.r4(sets), pcs: pcs });
    });
    if (!plan.length) throw new Error('Enter how many sets to dispatch on at least one line.');

    var entries = [];
    plan.forEach(function (p) { if (p.l.itemCode) entries.push({ code: p.l.itemCode, loc: p.l.pickFrom, qty: -p.pcs, type: 'Dispatch', ref: p.l.id }); });
    Ledger.add(entries, user);
    plan.forEach(function (p) {
      var total = U.r4(p.l.dispatchedSets + p.sets), full = total >= p.l.sets - 1e-9;
      Db.update('orderLines', p.l.row, { 'Dispatched Qty': total, 'Dispatched': full, 'Dispatch Date': Rows.serial(dispIso), 'Dispatched By': user.email });
      p.l.dispatchedSets = total; p.l.open = full ? 0 : U.r4(p.l.sets - total); p.l.dispatched = full;
    });
    var left = allRows.some(function (r) {
      var l = WhApi.line2_(r, items), p = plan.filter(function (x) { return x.l.id === l.id; })[0];
      return (p ? p.l.open : l.open) > 1e-9;
    });
    var head = { 'Dispatch Date': date, 'Dispatched By': user.email };
    if (U.s(a.lrNo)) head['LR No.'] = U.s(a.lrNo);
    if (U.s(a.transporter)) head['Transporter'] = U.s(a.transporter);
    if (!left) head['Status'] = 'Dispatched';
    Db.update('orders', o._row, head);
    return { dispatched: plan.length, complete: !left, warnings: warnings };
  },

  // ---------- numbers for tab badges ----------
  counts: function () {
    var toReceive = 0, pend = 0, items = {};      // counts only: no product read needed
    var grns = Db.readAll('grn'), by = Rows.by(grns, 'GRN ID');
    grns.forEach(function (g) { if (WhApi.grnStatus_(g) !== 'Received') toReceive++; });
    Db.readAll('grnLines').forEach(function (r) {
      var g = by[U.s(r['GRN ID'])];
      if (g && WhApi.grnStatus_(g) === 'Received' && r['Put Away'] !== true && U.toNum(r['Received No. of Sets']) > 0) pend++;
    });
    var lines = Rows.group(Db.readAll('orderLines'), 'Order ID'), toShip = 0, ready = 0;
    Db.readAll('orders').forEach(function (o) {
      var st = U.s(o['Stage (auto)']) || WhApi.stage_(o, (lines[U.s(o['Order ID'])] || []).map(function (r) { return WhApi.line2_(r, items); }));
      if (['To pick', 'To pack', 'Book transport', 'Ready to dispatch'].indexOf(st) >= 0) toShip++;
      if (st === 'Ready to dispatch') ready++;
    });
    var audit = 0;
    Db.readAll('audit').forEach(function (r) { if ((U.s(r['Status']) || 'Pending') === 'Pending') audit++; });
    return { receiving: toReceive, putaway: pend, dispatch: toShip, ready: ready, audit: audit };
  }
};
# ===== END OF Warehouse =====

##############################################################################
# FILE 6 of 25 :  Accounts    [Script file]   NEW (Accounts department: order receipts, purchase receipts, payments)
# first line of this file is:  /**
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
/**
 * Accounts.gs - backend for the Accounts screens: order receipts (billing + money received), purchase receipts
 * (supplier bills + money paid) and the payments ledger. Counts of stock are reviewed in the Audit tab (Warehouse.gs).
 *
 * Tables: Sales Orders (Tally Voucher No., Invoice Date, Due Date, Invoice Amount, Payment Cleared On), GRN (Bill Amount,
 * Tally Purchase Voucher No.), Payments (one row per receipt or payment; a part payment is another row).
 *
 * Money rules:
 *   - An order is worth "Invoice Amount" once billed, until then its value including GST (from the order lines).
 *   - Received / Paid / Balance are worked out here from the Payments rows (the sheet's own (auto) columns are never written).
 *   - Payments are added, never overwritten. A wrong entry is corrected in place (amount, date, mode, reference);
 *     only an Admin can delete one.
 *   - Typed date columns (Payment Date, Invoice Date, Due Date) are written as serial numbers: see Rows.serial in Items.gs.
 */

var AcctApi = {
  routes: function () {
    var A = ['Accounts'];
    return {
      'acc.counts':  [AcctApi.counts, false, A],
      'ord.list':    [AcctApi.ordList, false, A],
      'ord.get':     [AcctApi.ordGet, false, A],
      'ord.bill':    [AcctApi.ordBill, true, A],
      'bill.list':   [AcctApi.billList, false, A],
      'bill.get':    [AcctApi.billGet, false, A],
      'bill.save':   [AcctApi.billSave, true, A],
      'pay.add':     [AcctApi.payAdd, true, A],
      'pay.list':    [AcctApi.payList, false, A],
      'pay.save':    [AcctApi.paySave, true, A],
      'pay.delete':  [AcctApi.payDelete, true, []]        // Admin only
    };
  },

  // ---------- small helpers ----------
  /** a date cell -> 'yyyy-MM-dd' (also accepts a plain serial number, in case a date column was formatted as a number) */
  iso_: function (v) {
    if (typeof v === 'number' && v > 20000 && v < 80000) {
      var d = new Date(Date.UTC(1899, 11, 30) + Math.round(v) * 86400000);
      return d.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + U.pad(d.getUTCMonth() + 1) + '-' + U.pad(d.getUTCDate());
    }
    return U.iso(v);
  },
  /** 'yyyy-mm-dd' from the screen, checked. blank -> '' when optional */
  date_: function (v, label, optional) {
    var t = U.s(v);
    if (!t) { if (optional) return ''; throw new Error('Enter the ' + label + '.'); }
    var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(t);
    if (!m) throw new Error('The ' + label + ' is not a valid date.');
    var d = new Date(Date.UTC(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3]));
    if (d.getUTCMonth() !== +m[2] - 1 || d.getUTCDate() !== +m[3]) throw new Error('The ' + label + ' is not a valid date.');
    return t;
  },
  money_: function (v, label) {
    var n = U.toNum(v);
    if (isNaN(n) || !(n > 0)) throw new Error('Enter the ' + label + ' (more than 0).');
    if (n > 1e9) throw new Error('The ' + label + ' looks too large.');
    return U.r2(n);
  },
  num_: function (v) { var n = U.toNum(v); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; },
  addMonthsIso_: function (iso, n) {
    var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso);
    if (!m) return '';
    var y = +m[1], mo = +m[2] - 1 + n, day = +m[3];
    var last = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo + 1, 0)).getUTCDate(), d = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo, Math.min(day, last)));
    return d.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + U.pad(d.getUTCMonth() + 1) + '-' + U.pad(d.getUTCDate());
  },
  /** When is money due, going by the payment terms ("30 days credit", "3 months credit period", "100% advance", "After delivery")? */
  dueFrom_: function (terms, iso) {
    var t = U.low(terms), m = /(\d+)\s*(day|month)/.exec(t);
    if (!iso) return '';
    if (/credit/.test(t) && m) return m[2] === 'month' ? AcctApi.addMonthsIso_(iso, +m[1]) : U.addDaysIso(iso, +m[1]);
    if (/advance|after delivery|cash/.test(t)) return iso;
    return '';
  },
  /** Payments grouped by what they were for: { 'Order|id': { sum (signed, in the natural direction), last (iso), rows } } */
  payIndex_: function () {
    var idx = {};
    Db.readAll('payments').forEach(function (r) {
      var type = U.s(r['Ref Type']), id = U.s(r['Ref ID']), dir = U.s(r['Direction']), amt = U.toNum(r['Amount']);
      if (!type || !id || isNaN(amt)) return;
      var natural = type === 'Order' ? 'Received' : 'Paid', k = type + '|' + id;
      var e = idx[k] || (idx[k] = { sum: 0, last: '', rows: [] }), date = AcctApi.iso_(r['Payment Date']);
      e.sum = U.r2(e.sum + (dir === natural ? amt : -amt));
      if (date > e.last) e.last = date;
      e.rows.push(r);
    });
    return idx;
  },
  payOut_: function (r, refNo, party) {
    var type = U.s(r['Ref Type']);
    return {
      id: U.s(r['Payment ID']), date: AcctApi.iso_(r['Payment Date']), direction: U.s(r['Direction']), refType: type, refId: U.s(r['Ref ID']),
      refNo: refNo || '', party: party || '', amount: U.r2(AcctApi.num_(r['Amount'])), mode: U.s(r['Mode']), referenceNo: U.s(r['Reference No.']),
      by: U.s(r['Entered By']), notes: U.s(r['Notes']), row: r._row
    };
  },

  // ---------- order receipts ----------
  /** every order with its money worked out */
  orders_: function () {
    var today = U.isoToday(), pays = AcctApi.payIndex_(), lines = Rows.group(Db.readAll('orderLines'), 'Order ID');
    var cust = {};
    Db.readCols('customers', ['Customer ID', 'Company/Brand Name']).forEach(function (c) { cust[U.s(c['Customer ID'])] = U.s(c['Company/Brand Name']); });
    return Db.readAll('orders').map(function (o) {
      var id = U.s(o['Order ID']), ls = lines[id] || [], calc = 0;
      ls.forEach(function (l) {
        var pcs = U.toNum(l['Pcs / Set']), sets = U.toNum(l['No. of set']), price = U.toNum(l['Offer Price']), g = U.frac(l['GST %']);
        if (!(pcs >= 1)) pcs = 1;
        if (!isNaN(sets) && !isNaN(price)) calc += pcs * sets * price * (1 + (isNaN(g) ? 0 : g));
      });
      var auto = U.toNum(o['Value incl. GST (auto)']), value = U.r2(!isNaN(auto) && auto > 0 ? auto : calc);
      var inv = U.toNum(o['Invoice Amount']), invoiced = !isNaN(inv) && inv > 0, status = U.s(o['Status']);
      var p = pays['Order|' + id] || { sum: 0, last: '', rows: [] };
      var basis = invoiced ? U.r2(inv) : value, received = p.sum, balance = U.r2(basis - received);
      var invDate = AcctApi.iso_(o['Invoice Date']), terms = U.s(o['Payment Terms']);
      var due = AcctApi.iso_(o['Due Date']) || (invoiced ? AcctApi.dueFrom_(terms, invDate) : '');
      var state;
      if (status === 'Cancelled') state = 'Cancelled';
      else if (!invoiced) state = 'To invoice';
      else if (balance <= 0.5) state = 'Paid';
      else state = due && due < today ? 'Overdue' : 'Awaiting payment';
      var stage = U.s(o['Stage (auto)']);
      return {
        id: id, no: U.s(o['Order No.']), date: AcctApi.iso_(o['Order Date']), customerId: U.s(o['Customer']),
        customer: U.s(o['Customer (auto)']) || cust[U.s(o['Customer'])] || U.s(o['Customer']), salesman: U.s(o['Salesman']), status: status, stage: stage,
        dispatched: status === 'Dispatched' || stage === 'Dispatched', terms: terms, lines: ls.length,
        value: value, invoiced: invoiced, invoiceAmount: invoiced ? U.r2(inv) : null, voucher: U.s(o['Tally Voucher No.']),
        invoiceDate: invDate, due: due, clearedOn: AcctApi.iso_(o['Payment Cleared On']),
        received: received, balance: balance, lastPaid: p.last, payments: p.rows.length, state: state,
        overdueDays: state === 'Overdue' ? Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.parse(today + 'T00:00:00Z') - Date.parse(due + 'T00:00:00Z')) / 86400000)) : 0,
        refundDue: status === 'Cancelled' && received > 0.5, row: o._row
      };
    });
  },

  /** args: { filter: 'To invoice' | 'To collect' | 'Paid' | 'All', search } */
  ordList: function (a) {
    var all = AcctApi.orders_(), f = U.s(a.filter) || 'To invoice', q = U.s(a.search), today = U.isoToday(), month = today.slice(0, 7);
    var counts = { toInvoice: 0, toCollect: 0, paid: 0 }, sum = { toInvoiceValue: 0, outstanding: 0, overdue: 0, overdueCount: 0, receivedMonth: 0 };
    all.forEach(function (o) {
      if (o.state === 'To invoice' && o.value > 0) { counts.toInvoice++; sum.toInvoiceValue += o.value; }
      else if (o.state === 'Awaiting payment' || o.state === 'Overdue') { counts.toCollect++; sum.outstanding += o.balance; }
      else if (o.state === 'Paid') counts.paid++;
      if (o.state === 'Overdue') { sum.overdue += o.balance; sum.overdueCount++; }
    });
    Db.readAll('payments').forEach(function (r) {
      if (U.s(r['Ref Type']) === 'Order' && U.s(r['Direction']) === 'Received' && AcctApi.iso_(r['Payment Date']).slice(0, 7) === month) sum.receivedMonth += AcctApi.num_(r['Amount']);
    });
    Object.keys(sum).forEach(function (k) { sum[k] = U.r2(sum[k]); });
    var rows = all.filter(function (o) {
      if (f === 'To invoice' && !(o.state === 'To invoice' && o.value > 0)) return false;
      if (f === 'To collect' && o.state !== 'Awaiting payment' && o.state !== 'Overdue') return false;
      if (f === 'Paid' && o.state !== 'Paid') return false;
      return !q || Rows.matches([o.no, o.customer, o.voucher, o.state, o.terms, o.stage].join(' '), q);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) {
      if (f === 'To invoice') { if (x.dispatched !== y.dispatched) return x.dispatched ? -1 : 1; return x.date < y.date ? -1 : x.date > y.date ? 1 : 0; }
      if (f === 'To collect') { if ((x.state === 'Overdue') !== (y.state === 'Overdue')) return x.state === 'Overdue' ? -1 : 1; var dx = x.due || '9999', dy = y.due || '9999'; return dx < dy ? -1 : dx > dy ? 1 : 0; }
      return x.no < y.no ? 1 : x.no > y.no ? -1 : 0;
    });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), counts: counts, sum: sum };
  },

  /** args: { id } - the order, its lines and its payments */
  ordGet: function (a) {
    var o = AcctApi.orders_().filter(function (x) { return x.id === U.s(a.id); })[0];
    if (!o) throw new Error('That order was not found.');
    var items = Items.map();
    var lines = Db.readAll('orderLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Order ID']) === o.id; }).map(function (r) {
      var code = U.s(r['Item_Code']), p = code ? items[code] : null, pcs = U.toNum(r['Pcs / Set']), sets = U.toNum(r['No. of set']), price = U.toNum(r['Offer Price']), g = U.frac(r['GST %']);
      if (!(pcs >= 1)) pcs = p ? p.pcs : 1;
      sets = isNaN(sets) ? 0 : sets; price = isNaN(price) ? 0 : price; g = isNaN(g) ? 0 : g;
      return { id: U.s(r['Line ID']), itemCode: code, name: p ? (p.name || code) : U.s(r['Description']), pcs: pcs, sets: sets, price: price, gst: g,
               amount: U.r2(pcs * sets * price * (1 + g)) };
    });
    var pays = Db.readAll('payments').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Ref Type']) === 'Order' && U.s(r['Ref ID']) === o.id; })
      .map(function (r) { return AcctApi.payOut_(r, o.no, o.customer); }).sort(function (x, y) { return x.row - y.row; });
    o.suggestedDue = AcctApi.dueFrom_(o.terms, o.invoiceDate || U.isoToday());
    o.lineList = lines; o.paymentList = pays;
    return o;
  },

  /**
   * Billing, after the invoice is made in Tally. args: { id, voucherNo, invoiceDate, invoiceAmount, dueDate? }
   * Sets Status to "Billing done" unless the order is already dispatched or cancelled.
   */
  ordBill: function (a) {
    var o = AcctApi.orders_().filter(function (x) { return x.id === U.s(a.id); })[0];
    if (!o) throw new Error('That order was not found.');
    if (o.status === 'Cancelled') throw new Error('This order is cancelled.');
    var voucher = U.s(a.voucherNo);
    if (!voucher) throw new Error('Enter the Tally voucher number.');
    var amount = AcctApi.money_(a.invoiceAmount, 'invoice amount'), invDate = AcctApi.date_(a.invoiceDate, 'invoice date');
    if (invDate > U.isoToday()) throw new Error('The invoice date cannot be in the future.');
    var due = AcctApi.date_(a.dueDate, 'due date', true);
    if (due && due < invDate) throw new Error('The due date cannot be before the invoice date.');
    var upd = { 'Tally Voucher No.': voucher, 'Invoice Date': Rows.serial(invDate), 'Invoice Amount': amount, 'Due Date': due ? Rows.serial(due) : '' };
    if (o.status !== 'Dispatched') upd['Status'] = 'Billing done';
    Db.update('orders', o.row, upd, { 'Tally Voucher No.': '@' });
    AcctApi.syncCleared_(o.id);
    return { id: o.id, balance: U.r2(amount - o.received) };
  },

  /** Keep "Payment Cleared On" right: the date of the last payment once the invoice is fully paid, blank otherwise. */
  syncCleared_: function (orderId) {
    var o = AcctApi.orders_().filter(function (x) { return x.id === orderId; })[0];
    if (!o) return;
    var want = o.invoiced && o.balance <= 0.5 && o.received > 0 ? o.lastPaid : '';
    if (want === o.clearedOn) return;
    Db.update('orders', o.row, { 'Payment Cleared On': want ? U.dateFromIso(want) : '' });
  },

  // ---------- purchase receipts (supplier bills) ----------
  bills_: function () {
    var today = U.isoToday(), pays = AcctApi.payIndex_(), sups = ProcApi.suppliers_();
    var allPl = Db.readAll('purchaseLines');
    var pos = Rows.by(Db.readAll('purchase'), 'Purchase ID'), pLines = Rows.by(allPl, 'Line ID'), poLines = Rows.group(allPl, 'Purchase ID');
    var gLines = Rows.group(Db.readAll('grnLines'), 'GRN ID');
    return Db.readAll('grn').map(function (g) {
      var id = U.s(g['GRN ID']), po = pos[U.s(g['Purchase ID'])], sid = U.s(g['Supplier']) || (po ? U.s(po['Supplier Company Name']) : '');
      var poValue = 0, recValue = 0, damaged = 0;
      (po ? (poLines[U.s(po['Purchase ID'])] || []) : []).forEach(function (l) {
        var pcs = U.toNum(l['Pcs / Set']), sets = U.toNum(l['No. of Set']), rate = U.toNum(l['Rate']), gs = U.frac(l['GST %']);
        if (!(pcs >= 1)) pcs = 1;
        if (!isNaN(sets) && !isNaN(rate)) poValue += pcs * sets * rate * (1 + (isNaN(gs) ? 0 : gs));
      });
      (gLines[id] || []).forEach(function (l) {
        var pl = pLines[U.s(l['Purchase Line ID'])], rec = U.toNum(l['Received No. of Sets']), per = U.toNum(l['Received pcs / set']), dm = U.toNum(l['Damaged Qty']);
        if (!(per >= 1)) per = U.toNum(l['Expected pcs / set']);
        if (!(per >= 1)) per = 1;
        if (!isNaN(dm)) damaged += dm;
        if (pl && !isNaN(rec)) {
          var rate = U.toNum(pl['Rate']), gs = U.frac(pl['GST %']);
          if (!isNaN(rate)) recValue += rec * per * rate * (1 + (isNaN(gs) ? 0 : gs));
        }
      });
      var bill = U.toNum(g['Bill Amount']), billed = !isNaN(bill) && bill > 0, p = pays['GRN|' + id] || { sum: 0, last: '', rows: [] };
      var received = U.s(g['Status']) === 'Received' || U.s(g['Received Date']) !== '';
      var paid = p.sum, balance = billed ? U.r2(bill - paid) : null, terms = po ? U.s(po['Payment Terms']) : '';
      var invDate = AcctApi.iso_(g['Invoice Date']), recDate = AcctApi.iso_(g['Received Date']);
      var due = billed ? AcctApi.dueFrom_(terms, invDate || recDate) : '';
      var state;
      if (!received && !billed) state = 'Not received';
      else if (!billed) state = 'To bill';
      else if (balance <= 0.5) state = 'Paid';
      else state = due && due < today ? 'Overdue' : 'To pay';
      return {
        id: id, no: U.s(g['GRN No']), purchaseId: U.s(g['Purchase ID']), poNo: po ? U.s(po['Purchase No']) : '', supplierId: sid,
        supplier: sups.by[sid] ? sups.by[sid].name : '', terms: terms, received: received, receivedDate: recDate,
        invoiceNo: U.s(g['Supplier Invoice No']), invoiceDate: invDate, billAmount: billed ? U.r2(bill) : null, voucher: U.s(g['Tally Purchase Voucher No.']),
        poValue: U.r2(poValue), recValue: U.r2(recValue), damaged: U.r4(damaged), paid: paid, balance: balance, due: due, lastPaid: p.last, payments: p.rows.length,
        state: state, overdueDays: state === 'Overdue' ? Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.parse(today + 'T00:00:00Z') - Date.parse(due + 'T00:00:00Z')) / 86400000)) : 0, row: g._row
      };
    });
  },

  /** args: { filter: 'To bill' | 'To pay' | 'Paid' | 'Not received' | 'All', search } */
  billList: function (a) {
    var all = AcctApi.bills_(), f = U.s(a.filter) || 'To bill', q = U.s(a.search), month = U.isoToday().slice(0, 7);
    var counts = { toBill: 0, toPay: 0, paid: 0, notReceived: 0 }, sum = { payable: 0, overdue: 0, overdueCount: 0, paidMonth: 0 };
    all.forEach(function (b) {
      if (b.state === 'To bill') counts.toBill++;
      else if (b.state === 'To pay' || b.state === 'Overdue') { counts.toPay++; sum.payable += b.balance; }
      else if (b.state === 'Paid') counts.paid++;
      else counts.notReceived++;
      if (b.state === 'Overdue') { sum.overdue += b.balance; sum.overdueCount++; }
    });
    Db.readAll('payments').forEach(function (r) {
      if (U.s(r['Ref Type']) === 'GRN' && U.s(r['Direction']) === 'Paid' && AcctApi.iso_(r['Payment Date']).slice(0, 7) === month) sum.paidMonth += AcctApi.num_(r['Amount']);
    });
    Object.keys(sum).forEach(function (k) { sum[k] = U.r2(sum[k]); });
    var rows = all.filter(function (b) {
      if (f === 'To bill' && b.state !== 'To bill') return false;
      if (f === 'To pay' && b.state !== 'To pay' && b.state !== 'Overdue') return false;
      if (f === 'Paid' && b.state !== 'Paid') return false;
      if (f === 'Not received' && b.state !== 'Not received') return false;
      return !q || Rows.matches([b.no, b.poNo, b.supplier, b.invoiceNo, b.voucher, b.state].join(' '), q);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) {
      if (f === 'To pay') { if ((x.state === 'Overdue') !== (y.state === 'Overdue')) return x.state === 'Overdue' ? -1 : 1; var dx = x.due || '9999', dy = y.due || '9999'; return dx < dy ? -1 : dx > dy ? 1 : 0; }
      return x.no < y.no ? 1 : x.no > y.no ? -1 : 0;
    });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), counts: counts, sum: sum };
  },

  /** args: { id } - one delivery with its lines and payments */
  billGet: function (a) {
    var b = AcctApi.bills_().filter(function (x) { return x.id === U.s(a.id); })[0];
    if (!b) throw new Error('That delivery was not found.');
    var items = Items.map(), pLines = Rows.by(Db.readAll('purchaseLines'), 'Line ID');
    b.lineList = Db.readAll('grnLines').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['GRN ID']) === b.id; }).map(function (r) {
      var code = U.s(r['Item Code']), p = code ? items[code] : null, pl = pLines[U.s(r['Purchase Line ID'])];
      var per = U.toNum(r['Received pcs / set']); if (!(per >= 1)) per = U.toNum(r['Expected pcs / set']); if (!(per >= 1)) per = p ? p.pcs : 1;
      var rec = U.toNum(r['Received No. of Sets']), exp = U.toNum(r['Expected No. of Sets']), rate = pl ? U.toNum(pl['Rate']) : NaN, gs = pl ? U.frac(pl['GST %']) : NaN;
      rec = isNaN(rec) ? null : rec; rate = isNaN(rate) ? 0 : rate; gs = isNaN(gs) ? 0 : gs;
      return { id: U.s(r['Line ID']), itemCode: code, name: p ? (p.name || code) : U.s(r['Description']), per: per, expSets: isNaN(exp) ? 0 : exp, recSets: rec,
               damaged: U.r4(AcctApi.num_(r['Damaged Qty'])), rate: rate, gst: gs, amount: rec === null ? 0 : U.r2(rec * per * rate * (1 + gs)) };
    });
    b.paymentList = Db.readAll('payments').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Ref Type']) === 'GRN' && U.s(r['Ref ID']) === b.id; })
      .map(function (r) { return AcctApi.payOut_(r, b.no, b.supplier); }).sort(function (x, y) { return x.row - y.row; });
    return b;
  },

  /** The supplier's bill. args: { id, billAmount, voucherNo, invoiceNo?, invoiceDate? } */
  billSave: function (a) {
    var b = AcctApi.bills_().filter(function (x) { return x.id === U.s(a.id); })[0];
    if (!b) throw new Error('That delivery was not found.');
    var amount = AcctApi.money_(a.billAmount, 'bill amount'), voucher = U.s(a.voucherNo);
    if (!voucher) throw new Error('Enter the Tally purchase voucher number.');
    var upd = { 'Bill Amount': amount, 'Tally Purchase Voucher No.': voucher };
    if (U.s(a.invoiceNo)) upd['Supplier Invoice No'] = U.s(a.invoiceNo);
    if (U.s(a.invoiceDate)) upd['Invoice Date'] = U.dateFromIso(AcctApi.date_(a.invoiceDate, 'supplier invoice date'));
    Db.update('grn', b.row, upd, { 'Tally Purchase Voucher No.': '@' });
    return { id: b.id, balance: U.r2(amount - b.paid) };
  },

  // ---------- payments ----------
  /**
   * Record money. args: { kind: 'Order' | 'GRN', refId, date, amount, mode, referenceNo, notes, refund?, confirmOver? }
   * An order receipt is "Received", a purchase payment is "Paid"; refund = the other way round.
   */
  payAdd: function (a, user) {
    var kind = U.s(a.kind), refId = U.s(a.refId), ref, label;
    if (kind === 'Order') {
      ref = AcctApi.orders_().filter(function (x) { return x.id === refId; })[0];
      if (!ref) throw new Error('That order was not found.');
      label = ref.no;
    } else if (kind === 'GRN') {
      ref = AcctApi.bills_().filter(function (x) { return x.id === refId; })[0];
      if (!ref) throw new Error('That delivery was not found.');
      label = ref.no;
    } else throw new Error('A payment must be for an order or a delivery.');
    var refund = a.refund === true, natural = kind === 'Order' ? 'Received' : 'Paid', dir = refund ? (natural === 'Received' ? 'Paid' : 'Received') : natural;
    var amount = AcctApi.money_(a.amount, 'amount'), date = AcctApi.date_(a.date, 'payment date'), mode = U.s(a.mode), refNo = U.s(a.referenceNo);
    if (date > U.isoToday()) throw new Error('The payment date cannot be in the future.');
    if (CONFIG.PAYMENT_MODES.indexOf(mode) < 0) throw new Error('Pick how it was paid: ' + CONFIG.PAYMENT_MODES.join(', ') + '.');
    if (kind === 'Order' && ref.status === 'Cancelled' && !refund) throw new Error(label + ' is cancelled: only a refund can be recorded.');
    var basis = kind === 'Order' ? (ref.invoiced ? ref.invoiceAmount : ref.value) : ref.billAmount;
    if (!refund && basis && amount > U.r2(basis - (kind === 'Order' ? ref.received : ref.paid)) + 0.5 && a.confirmOver !== true) {
      throw new Error('That is more than the balance (Rs ' + U.inr(Math.max(0, basis - (kind === 'Order' ? ref.received : ref.paid)), 2) + ') on ' + label + '.');
    }
    var rows = Db.readAll('payments');
    if (refNo) rows.forEach(function (r) {
      if (U.s(r['Ref Type']) === kind && U.s(r['Ref ID']) === refId && U.s(r['Reference No.']) === refNo && Math.abs(AcctApi.num_(r['Amount']) - amount) < 0.005 && AcctApi.iso_(r['Payment Date']) === date)
        throw new Error('This payment (same reference, amount and date) is already recorded on ' + label + '.');
    });
    var id = U.newId(U.idSet(rows, 'Payment ID'));
    Db.append('payments', [{
      'Payment ID': id, 'Payment Date': Rows.serial(date), 'Direction': dir, 'Ref Type': kind, 'Ref ID': refId, 'Amount': amount,
      'Mode': mode, 'Reference No.': refNo, 'Entered By': user.email, 'Notes': U.s(a.notes)
    }], { 'Reference No.': '@' });
    if (kind === 'Order') AcctApi.syncCleared_(refId);
    return { id: id };
  },

  /** args: { direction: 'All' | 'Received' | 'Paid', from?, to?, search } */
  payList: function (a) {
    var f = U.s(a.direction) || 'All', q = U.s(a.search), from = AcctApi.date_(a.from, 'from date', true), to = AcctApi.date_(a.to, 'to date', true);
    var ords = {}, grns = {};
    AcctApi.orders_().forEach(function (o) { ords[o.id] = o; });
    AcctApi.bills_().forEach(function (b) { grns[b.id] = b; });
    var rec = 0, paid = 0, rows = [];
    Db.readAll('payments').forEach(function (r) {
      var type = U.s(r['Ref Type']), id = U.s(r['Ref ID']), ref = type === 'Order' ? ords[id] : grns[id];
      var p = AcctApi.payOut_(r, ref ? ref.no : '', ref ? (type === 'Order' ? ref.customer : ref.supplier) : '');
      if (f !== 'All' && p.direction !== f) return;
      if (from && p.date < from) return;
      if (to && p.date > to) return;
      if (q && !Rows.matches([p.refNo, p.party, p.mode, p.referenceNo, p.notes, p.by, p.id].join(' '), q)) return;
      if (p.direction === 'Received') rec += p.amount; else if (p.direction === 'Paid') paid += p.amount;
      rows.push(p);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return x.date < y.date ? 1 : x.date > y.date ? -1 : y.row - x.row; });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), totals: { received: U.r2(rec), paid: U.r2(paid), net: U.r2(rec - paid) }, modes: CONFIG.PAYMENT_MODES };
  },

  /** Correct a payment in place. args: { id, date, amount, mode, referenceNo, notes } (what it was for cannot change) */
  paySave: function (a) {
    var r = Rows.by(Db.readAll('payments'), 'Payment ID')[U.s(a.id)];
    if (!r) throw new Error('That payment was not found.');
    var amount = AcctApi.money_(a.amount, 'amount'), date = AcctApi.date_(a.date, 'payment date'), mode = U.s(a.mode);
    if (date > U.isoToday()) throw new Error('The payment date cannot be in the future.');
    if (CONFIG.PAYMENT_MODES.indexOf(mode) < 0) throw new Error('Pick how it was paid: ' + CONFIG.PAYMENT_MODES.join(', ') + '.');
    Db.update('payments', r._row, { 'Payment Date': Rows.serial(date), 'Amount': amount, 'Mode': mode, 'Reference No.': U.s(a.referenceNo), 'Notes': U.s(a.notes) }, { 'Reference No.': '@' });
    if (U.s(r['Ref Type']) === 'Order') AcctApi.syncCleared_(U.s(r['Ref ID']));
    return { id: U.s(a.id) };
  },

  /** Admin only. args: { id } */
  payDelete: function (a) {
    var r = Rows.by(Db.readAll('payments'), 'Payment ID')[U.s(a.id)];
    if (!r) throw new Error('That payment was not found.');
    var type = U.s(r['Ref Type']), ref = U.s(r['Ref ID']);
    Rows.wipe('payments', r._row);
    if (type === 'Order') AcctApi.syncCleared_(ref);
    return { id: U.s(a.id) };
  },

  // ---------- numbers for the tab badges ----------
  counts: function () {
    var today = U.isoToday(), toInvoice = 0, overdue = 0, toBill = 0, payOverdue = 0;
    AcctApi.orders_().forEach(function (o) { if (o.state === 'To invoice' && o.value > 0) toInvoice++; if (o.state === 'Overdue') overdue++; });
    AcctApi.bills_().forEach(function (b) { if (b.state === 'To bill') toBill++; if (b.state === 'Overdue') payOverdue++; });
    var audit = 0;
    Db.readAll('audit').forEach(function (r) { if ((U.s(r['Status']) || 'Pending') === 'Pending') audit++; });
    return { toInvoice: toInvoice, overdue: overdue, toBill: toBill, payOverdue: payOverdue, audit: audit, today: today };
  }
};
# ===== END OF Accounts =====

##############################################################################
# FILE 7 of 25 :  Admin    [Script file]   NEW (Admin department: overview, users, products, locations)
# first line of this file is:  /**
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
/**
 * Admin.gs - backend for the Admin screens: overview (what needs attention in every department), users, product master data,
 * storage locations and a sheet check. Everything here is Admin only.
 *
 * Tables: Users (Email, Name, Role), Locations, Products (attributes; the (auto) stock columns are formulas and are never written).
 * Safety rules for Users: you cannot remove or demote yourself, and there is always at least one Admin.
 */

var AdminApi = {
  routes: function () {
    var N = [];       // no role listed = only Admin passes the check in Main.api
    return {
      'adm.overview': [AdminApi.overview, false, N],
      'adm.check':    [AdminApi.check, false, N],
      'usr.list':     [AdminApi.usrList, false, N],
      'usr.save':     [AdminApi.usrSave, true, N],
      'usr.delete':   [AdminApi.usrDelete, true, N],
      'loc.admin':    [AdminApi.locAdmin, false, N],
      'loc.save':     [AdminApi.locSave, true, N],
      'prd.list':     [AdminApi.prdList, false, N],
      'prd.save':     [AdminApi.prdSave, true, N],
      'prd.verify':   [AdminApi.prdVerify, true, N]
    };
  },

  // ---------- small helpers ----------
  /** "2026-10-12" -> "12 Oct 2026" for the sentences the overview builds on the server */
  fmt_: function (iso) {
    var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso || ''), mon = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
    return m ? (+m[3]) + ' ' + mon[+m[2] - 1] + ' ' + m[1] : '';
  },
  qty_: function (n) { n = Number(n); return isFinite(n) ? String(Math.round(n * 10000) / 10000) : ''; },

  // ---------- users ----------
  deptsOf_: function (role) {
    return Main.allowedDepts(role).map(function (k) { return CONFIG.DEPARTMENTS[k].label; });
  },
  usrList: function (a, user) {
    var rows = Db.readAll('users').map(function (r) {
      var email = U.low(r['Email']), role = U.s(r['Role']);
      return { email: email, name: U.s(r['Name']), role: role, valid: CONFIG.ALLOWED_ROLES.indexOf(role) >= 0, depts: CONFIG.ALLOWED_ROLES.indexOf(role) >= 0 ? AdminApi.deptsOf_(role) : [], you: email === user.email, row: r._row };
    });
    var order = CONFIG.ALLOWED_ROLES, counts = {};
    CONFIG.ALLOWED_ROLES.forEach(function (r) { counts[r] = 0; });
    rows.forEach(function (u) { if (counts[u.role] !== undefined) counts[u.role]++; });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) {
      var ix = order.indexOf(x.role), iy = order.indexOf(y.role);
      if (ix !== iy) return (ix < 0 ? 99 : ix) - (iy < 0 ? 99 : iy);
      return x.name.toLowerCase() < y.name.toLowerCase() ? -1 : x.name.toLowerCase() > y.name.toLowerCase() ? 1 : 0;
    });
    return { rows: rows, roles: CONFIG.ALLOWED_ROLES, counts: counts };
  },
  adminCount_: function (rows, exceptEmail) {
    var n = 0;
    rows.forEach(function (r) { if (U.s(r['Role']) === 'Admin' && U.low(r['Email']) !== exceptEmail) n++; });
    return n;
  },
  /** args: { originalEmail? (when editing), email, name, role } */
  usrSave: function (a, user) {
    var email = U.low(a.email), name = U.s(a.name).replace(/\s+/g, ' '), role = U.s(a.role), orig = U.low(a.originalEmail);
    if (!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email)) throw new Error('Enter a valid Google email address.');
    if (!name) throw new Error('Enter the person\'s name.');
    if (CONFIG.ALLOWED_ROLES.indexOf(role) < 0) throw new Error('Pick a role: ' + CONFIG.ALLOWED_ROLES.join(', ') + '.');
    var rows = Db.readAll('users'), mine = null;
    rows.forEach(function (r) { if (orig && U.low(r['Email']) === orig) mine = r; });
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      if (U.low(r['Email']) === email && (!mine || r._row !== mine._row)) throw new Error(email + ' is already in the list.');
    });
    if (orig && !mine) throw new Error('That user no longer exists.');
    if (mine) {
      if (orig === user.email && email !== user.email) throw new Error('You cannot change your own email address here.');
      if (orig === user.email && role !== 'Admin') throw new Error('You cannot take the Admin role away from yourself. Ask another Admin.');
      if (U.s(mine['Role']) === 'Admin' && role !== 'Admin' && AdminApi.adminCount_(rows, orig) < 1) throw new Error('There must always be at least one Admin.');
      Db.update('users', mine._row, { 'Email': email, 'Name': name, 'Role': role });
      return { email: email };
    }
    Db.append('users', [{ 'Email': email, 'Name': name, 'Role': role }]);
    return { email: email };
  },
  /** args: { email } */
  usrDelete: function (a, user) {
    var email = U.low(a.email), rows = Db.readAll('users'), mine = null;
    rows.forEach(function (r) { if (U.low(r['Email']) === email) mine = r; });
    if (!mine) throw new Error('That user no longer exists.');
    if (email === user.email) throw new Error('You cannot remove yourself.');
    if (U.s(mine['Role']) === 'Admin' && AdminApi.adminCount_(rows, email) < 1) throw new Error('There must always be at least one Admin.');
    Rows.wipe('users', mine._row);
    return { email: email };
  },

  // ---------- locations ----------
  locAdmin: function () {
    var bal = Ledger.balances(), pcs = {}, items = {};
    Object.keys(bal).forEach(function (k) {
      var i = k.indexOf('|'), loc = k.slice(i + 1);
      if (bal[k] > 1e-9) { pcs[loc] = U.r4((pcs[loc] || 0) + bal[k]); items[loc] = (items[loc] || 0) + 1; }
    });
    var rows = Db.readAll('locations').filter(function (r) { return U.s(r['Location Code']); }).map(function (r) {
      var c = U.s(r['Location Code']);
      return { code: c, floor: U.s(r['Floor']), zone: U.s(r['Zone']), shelf: U.s(r['Shelf']), desc: U.s(r['Description']), pcs: pcs[c] || 0, items: items[c] || 0 };
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return x.code < y.code ? -1 : x.code > y.code ? 1 : 0; });
    return rows;
  },
  /** args: { code, floor, zone, shelf, desc, isNew }. A code never changes once it exists: stock and orders refer to it. */
  locSave: function (a) {
    var code = U.s(a.code).toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
    if (!/^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9._-]{0,29}$/.test(code)) throw new Error('A location code uses letters, digits, - . _ only (for example FL2-ZB-S2).');
    var rows = Db.readAll('locations'), hit = null;
    rows.forEach(function (r) { if (U.s(r['Location Code']).toUpperCase() === code) hit = r; });
    var obj = { 'Floor': U.s(a.floor), 'Zone': U.s(a.zone), 'Shelf': U.s(a.shelf), 'Description': U.s(a.desc) };
    if (a.isNew === true) {
      if (hit) throw new Error('The location ' + code + ' already exists.');
      obj['Location Code'] = code;
      Db.append('locations', [obj], { 'Location Code': '@' });
    } else {
      if (!hit) throw new Error('That location no longer exists.');
      Db.update('locations', hit._row, obj);
    }
    return { code: code };
  },

  // ---------- product master data ----------
  PRD_COLS_: ['Item Code', 'Tally Name', 'Stock Group', 'Catalog Name', 'Pcs / Set', 'List Price', 'Customisation', 'Product Type', 'Material', 'Size', 'Colour',
              'Brand', 'HSN', 'GST_Pct', 'Photo_link', 'Verified', 'Verified_By', 'Verified_On'],
  prdOut_: function (r, items) {
    var code = U.s(r['Item Code']), g = U.frac(r['GST_Pct']), lp = U.toNum(r['List Price']), pcs = U.toNum(r['Pcs / Set']), p = items[code];
    var o = {
      code: code, tallyName: U.s(r['Tally Name']), group: U.s(r['Stock Group']), catalogName: U.s(r['Catalog Name']), pcs: pcs >= 1 ? pcs : 1,
      listPrice: isNaN(lp) ? '' : lp, customisation: U.s(r['Customisation']), productType: U.s(r['Product Type']), material: U.s(r['Material']), size: U.s(r['Size']),
      colour: U.s(r['Colour']), brand: U.s(r['Brand']), hsn: U.s(r['HSN']), gst: U.s(r['GST_Pct']) === '' || isNaN(g) ? '' : U.r4(g * 100),
      photoLink: U.s(r['Photo_link']), verified: r['Verified'] === true || /^true$/i.test(U.s(r['Verified'])), verifiedBy: U.s(r['Verified_By']), verifiedOn: AcctApi.iso_(r['Verified_On']),
      wh: p ? p.wh : 0, avail: p ? p.avail : '', row: r._row
    };
    o.missing = [];
    if (!o.catalogName) o.missing.push('Catalog name');
    if (!o.hsn) o.missing.push('HSN');
    if (o.gst === '') o.missing.push('GST');
    if (o.listPrice === '') o.missing.push('List price');
    return o;
  },
  /** args: { filter: 'Needs details' | 'Unverified' | 'All', search } */
  prdList: function (a) {
    var items = Items.map(), f = U.s(a.filter) || 'Needs details', q = U.s(a.search);
    var all = Db.readCols('products', AdminApi.PRD_COLS_).map(function (r) { return AdminApi.prdOut_(r, items); });
    var counts = { total: all.length, needs: 0, unverified: 0 }, lists = { groups: {}, types: {}, materials: {}, brands: {}, colours: {}, sizes: {} };
    all.forEach(function (p) {
      if (p.missing.length) counts.needs++;
      if (!p.verified) counts.unverified++;
      if (p.group) lists.groups[p.group] = 1; if (p.productType) lists.types[p.productType] = 1; if (p.material) lists.materials[p.material] = 1;
      if (p.brand) lists.brands[p.brand] = 1; if (p.colour) lists.colours[p.colour] = 1; if (p.size) lists.sizes[p.size] = 1;
    });
    var rows = all.filter(function (p) {
      if (f === 'Needs details' && !p.missing.length) return false;
      if (f === 'Unverified' && p.verified) return false;
      return !q || Rows.matches([p.code, p.tallyName, p.catalogName, p.group, p.productType, p.material, p.brand, p.colour, p.hsn].join(' '), q);
    });
    rows.sort(function (x, y) { return x.code.length - y.code.length || (x.code < y.code ? -1 : x.code > y.code ? 1 : 0); });
    var out = {};
    Object.keys(lists).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = Object.keys(lists[k]).sort().slice(0, 300); });
    return { shown: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, CONFIG.MAX_ROWS), counts: counts, lists: out };
  },
  prdRow_: function (code) {
    var c = U.s(code), hit = null;
    if (!c) throw new Error('Pick an item.');
    Db.readCols('products', ['Item Code']).forEach(function (r) { if (U.s(r['Item Code']) === c) hit = r; });
    if (!hit) throw new Error('Item ' + c + ' was not found in Products.');
    return hit;
  },
  /**
   * Change an item's details. args: { code, catalogName, group, pcs, listPrice, customisation, productType, material, size, colour, brand,
   * hsn, gst (percent), photoLink }. The Tally name is imported from Tally and is not changed here.
   */
  prdSave: function (a) {
    var hit = AdminApi.prdRow_(a.code), pcs = U.toNum(a.pcs), lp = U.s(a.listPrice) === '' ? '' : U.toNum(a.listPrice), gst = U.s(a.gst) === '' ? '' : U.toNum(a.gst);
    if (!(pcs >= 1) || pcs !== Math.floor(pcs)) throw new Error('Pieces per set must be a whole number, 1 or more.');
    if (lp !== '' && (isNaN(lp) || lp < 0)) throw new Error('The list price must be 0 or more (Rs per piece, GST excluded).');
    if (gst !== '' && (isNaN(gst) || gst < 0 || gst > 100)) throw new Error('GST must be between 0 and 100 %.');
    var link = U.s(a.photoLink);
    if (link && !/^https?:\/\//i.test(link)) throw new Error('The photo link must start with https://');
    var hsn = U.s(a.hsn).replace(/\s+/g, '');
    if (hsn && !/^\d{4,8}$/.test(hsn)) throw new Error('HSN is 4 to 8 digits.');
    Db.update('products', hit._row, {
      'Catalog Name': U.s(a.catalogName).replace(/\s+/g, ' '), 'Stock Group': U.s(a.group) || 'Uncategorised', 'Pcs / Set': pcs, 'List Price': lp,
      'Customisation': U.s(a.customisation), 'Product Type': U.s(a.productType), 'Material': U.s(a.material), 'Size': U.s(a.size), 'Colour': U.s(a.colour),
      'Brand': U.s(a.brand), 'HSN': hsn, 'GST_Pct': gst === '' ? '' : gst / 100, 'Photo_link': link
    }, { 'HSN': '@', 'GST_Pct': '0.00%' });
    Catalog._codes = null;
    return { code: U.s(a.code) };
  },
  /** args: { codes: [..], verified: true|false } */
  prdVerify: function (a, user) {
    var codes = Rows.ids(a.codes), on = a.verified !== false, want = {}, done = 0;
    if (!codes.length) throw new Error('Pick at least one item.');
    codes.forEach(function (c) { want[c] = 1; });
    Db.readCols('products', ['Item Code']).forEach(function (r) {
      if (!want[U.s(r['Item Code'])]) return;
      Db.update('products', r._row, on ? { 'Verified': true, 'Verified_By': user.email, 'Verified_On': U.today() } : { 'Verified': false, 'Verified_By': '', 'Verified_On': '' });
      done++;
    });
    return { done: done };
  },

  // ---------- sheet check ----------
  /** The same check as runDiagnostics in the editor, for every tab the app uses. */
  check: function () {
    var out = [];
    Object.keys(TABLES).forEach(function (key) {
      try {
        var m = Db.meta(key), missing = (REQUIRED_HEADERS[key] || []).filter(function (h) { return !m.cols[Db.norm(h)]; });
        out.push({ sheet: m.def.sheet, ok: !missing.length, rows: Math.max(0, Db.lastDataRow(m) - m.def.headerRow), missing: missing, error: '' });
      } catch (e) { out.push({ sheet: TABLES[key].sheet, ok: false, rows: 0, missing: [], error: String(e.message || e) }); }
    });
    return { tables: out };
  },

  // ---------- overview ----------
  /** What needs attention, department by department, plus the things that look wrong. */
  overview: function () {
    var today = U.isoToday(), week = U.addDaysIso(today, -7), pq = ProcApi.pqCounts(), po = ProcApi.poList({ filter: 'All' }), wh = WhApi.counts();
    var ord = AcctApi.ordList({ filter: 'All' }), bills = AcctApi.billList({ filter: 'All' }), orders = AcctApi.orders_(), grns = AcctApi.bills_();
    var cards = [
      { dept: 'Procurement', title: 'Requirements waiting for a decision', value: pq.awaiting, sub: pq.accepted + ' accepted, not on a PO yet' },
      { dept: 'Procurement', title: 'Purchase orders', value: po.counts.draft, sub: 'draft · ' + po.counts.awaiting + ' sent, awaiting delivery' },
      { dept: 'Warehouse', title: 'Deliveries to receive', value: wh.receiving, sub: wh.putaway + (wh.putaway === 1 ? ' line' : ' lines') + ' to put away' },
      { dept: 'Warehouse', title: 'Orders to ship', value: wh.dispatch, sub: wh.ready + ' ready to dispatch' },
      { dept: 'Warehouse', title: 'Stock counts to review', value: wh.audit, sub: 'waiting for Accounts' },
      { dept: 'Accounts', title: 'Orders to invoice', value: ord.counts.toInvoice, sub: 'Rs ' + U.inr(ord.sum.toInvoiceValue, 0) + ' not yet billed' },
      { dept: 'Accounts', title: 'To collect from customers', value: ord.sum.outstanding, money: true, sub: ord.sum.overdueCount + ' overdue (Rs ' + U.inr(ord.sum.overdue, 0) + ')' },
      { dept: 'Accounts', title: 'To pay to suppliers', value: bills.sum.payable, money: true, sub: bills.counts.toBill + ' deliveries not billed yet' }
    ];
    var ex = [];
    function add(sev, dept, title, list) { if (list.length) ex.push({ sev: sev, dept: dept, title: list.length + ' ' + title, items: list.slice(0, 5), more: Math.max(0, list.length - 5) }); }

    add('high', 'Procurement', po.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.late; }).length === 1 ? 'purchase order is past its delivery date' : 'purchase orders are past their delivery date',
      po.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.late; }).map(function (p) { return p.no + ' · ' + (p.supplier || 'no supplier') + ' · due ' + AdminApi.fmt_(p.delivery); }));
    var accepted = ProcApi.pqList({ filter: 'Accepted' }).rows.filter(function (r) { return r.needBy && r.needBy < today; });
    add('high', 'Procurement', accepted.length === 1 ? 'accepted requirement is past its need-by date and not ordered' : 'accepted requirements are past their need-by date and not ordered',
      accepted.map(function (r) { return r.item + ' · needed by ' + AdminApi.fmt_(r.needBy); }));
    var unbilled = orders.filter(function (o) { return o.state === 'To invoice' && o.dispatched && o.value > 0; });
    add('high', 'Accounts', unbilled.length === 1 ? 'order was dispatched but not invoiced' : 'orders were dispatched but not invoiced',
      unbilled.map(function (o) { return o.no + ' · ' + o.customer + ' · Rs ' + U.inr(o.value, 0); }));
    var late = orders.filter(function (o) { return o.state === 'Overdue'; });
    add('high', 'Accounts', late.length === 1 ? 'customer payment is overdue' : 'customer payments are overdue',
      late.map(function (o) { return o.no + ' · ' + o.customer + ' · Rs ' + U.inr(o.balance, 0) + ' · ' + o.overdueDays + ' days late'; }));
    var latePay = grns.filter(function (b) { return b.state === 'Overdue'; });
    add('med', 'Accounts', latePay.length === 1 ? 'supplier bill is overdue' : 'supplier bills are overdue',
      latePay.map(function (b) { return b.no + ' · ' + b.supplier + ' · Rs ' + U.inr(b.balance, 0) + ' · ' + b.overdueDays + ' days late'; }));
    var unb = grns.filter(function (b) { return b.state === 'To bill' && b.receivedDate && b.receivedDate <= week; });
    add('med', 'Accounts', unb.length === 1 ? 'delivery was received over a week ago and is not billed' : 'deliveries were received over a week ago and are not billed',
      unb.map(function (b) { return b.no + ' · ' + b.supplier + ' · received ' + AdminApi.fmt_(b.receivedDate); }));
    var old = [];
    Db.readAll('audit').forEach(function (r) {
      var d = AcctApi.iso_(r['Counted At']);
      if ((U.s(r['Status']) || 'Pending') === 'Pending' && d && d <= week) old.push(U.s(r['Description']) || U.s(r['Item Code']) || 'Unnamed item');
    });
    add('med', 'Accounts', old.length === 1 ? 'stock count has waited over a week for review' : 'stock counts have waited over a week for review', old.map(function (n) { return n; }));
    var short = WhApi.stockList({ filter: 'Short' });
    add('high', 'Warehouse', short.rows.length === 1 ? 'item is promised to customers beyond the stock in the warehouse' : 'items are promised to customers beyond the stock in the warehouse',
      short.rows.map(function (r) { return r.name + ' · available ' + AdminApi.qty_(r.avail) + ' pcs'; }));
    var mism = WhApi.stockList({ filter: 'Mismatch' });
    add('info', 'Warehouse', mism.rows.length === 1 ? 'item where the warehouse count differs from the Tally books' : 'items where the warehouse count differs from the Tally books',
      mism.rows.map(function (r) { return r.name + ' · books ' + AdminApi.qty_(r.books) + ' vs warehouse ' + AdminApi.qty_(r.wh); }));
    var noTax = [];
    Db.readCols('products', ['Item Code', 'Tally Name', 'GST_Pct', 'HSN']).forEach(function (r) {
      var p = Items.map()[U.s(r['Item Code'])];
      if (p && p.wh !== 0 && (U.s(r['GST_Pct']) === '' || U.s(r['HSN']) === '')) noTax.push(p.name + ' · ' + p.code);
    });
    add('info', 'Admin', noTax.length === 1 ? 'item in the warehouse has no GST rate or HSN' : 'items in the warehouse have no GST rate or HSN', noTax);
    var users = AdminApi.usrList({}, { email: '' });
    var noRole = ['Sales', 'Procurement', 'Warehouse', 'Accounts'].filter(function (r) { return !users.counts[r]; });
    add('info', 'Admin', noRole.length === 1 ? 'role has nobody in it' : 'roles have nobody in them', noRole.map(function (r) { return r + ' - add people on the Users tab'; }));
    var bad = users.rows.filter(function (u) { return !u.valid; });
    add('high', 'Admin', bad.length === 1 ? 'user has a role the app does not know' : 'users have a role the app does not know', bad.map(function (u) { return u.name + ' · ' + u.email + ' · "' + u.role + '"'; }));
    var order = { high: 0, med: 1, info: 2 };
    ex.sort(function (x, y) { return order[x.sev] - order[y.sev]; });
    return { today: today, cards: cards, exceptions: ex };
  }
};
# ===== END OF Admin =====

  <? }); ?>
  <script>
    /* PW: if a board file did not load (for example its opening or closing script tag was lost when pasting), say so */
    (function () {
      var want = <?!= JSON.stringify(cfg.boards) ?>, have = App.order.map(function (o) { return o.name; });
      var miss = want.filter(function (b) { return have.indexOf(b) < 0; });
      if (miss.length) setTimeout(function () {
        App.toast('These tabs did not load: ' + miss.join(', ') + '. Open the matching board file in Apps Script: it must start with ' + '<' + 'script> and end with ' + '<' + '/script>.', 'err');
      }, 1200);
    })();
    App.start();
  </script>

# FILE 9 of 25 :  DeptShell    [HTML file]   REPLACES DeptShell.html from the earlier version (adds Accounts/Admin icons, badges and helpers) - or NEW
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* DeptShell.html - what the Procurement and Warehouse boards share, loaded right after Client.html.
   It does NOT change Client.html: it wraps a few of its public functions (App.go, App.icon, App.refreshAttn)
   and adds PW.* helpers. On the Sales page it does nothing visible. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, noop = function () {};
  var PW = window.PW = { dept: String(window.DEPT || 'sales').toLowerCase(), label: { sales: 'Sales', procurement: 'Procurement', warehouse: 'Warehouse', accounts: 'Accounts', admin: 'Admin' } };
  PW.active = function (d) { return PW.dept === d; };

  // ---------- icons for the new tabs (Client.html only knows the Sales ones) ----------
  var ICONS = {
    purchase: [['path', { d: 'M6 2 3 6v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h14a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V6l-3-4zM3 6h18M16 10a4 4 0 0 1-8 0' }]],
    suppliers: [['path', { d: 'M3 21h18M5 21V8l7-5 7 5v13M9 21v-6h6v6' }]],
    receiving: [['path', { d: 'M22 12h-6l-2 3h-4l-2-3H2M5.5 5.1 2 12v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h16a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6l-3.5-6.9A2 2 0 0 0 16.8 4H7.2a2 2 0 0 0-1.7 1.1z' }]],
    putaway: [['path', { d: 'm12 2 10 5-10 5L2 7zM2 17l10 5 10-5M2 12l10 5 10-5' }]],
    dispatch: [['path', { d: 'M1 3h15v13H1zM16 8h4l3 3v5h-7z' }], ['circle', { cx: 5.5, cy: 18.5, r: 2.5 }], ['circle', { cx: 18.5, cy: 18.5, r: 2.5 }]],
    stock: [['path', { d: 'M21 16V8a2 2 0 0 0-1-1.7l-7-4a2 2 0 0 0-2 0l-7 4A2 2 0 0 0 3 8v8a2 2 0 0 0 1 1.7l7 4a2 2 0 0 0 2 0l7-4a2 2 0 0 0 1-1.7zM3.3 7 12 12l8.7-5M12 22V12' }]],
    audit: [['path', { d: 'm9 11 3 3L22 4M21 12v7a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h11' }]],
    truck: [['path', { d: 'M1 3h15v13H1zM16 8h4l3 3v5h-7z' }]],
    pdf: [['path', { d: 'M14 3H7a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V8z' }], ['path', { d: 'M14 3v5h5' }]],
    // Accounts
    orderpay: [['rect', { x: 2, y: 5, width: 20, height: 14, rx: 2 }], ['path', { d: 'M2 10h20M6 15h4' }]],
    billpay: [['path', { d: 'M14 3H7a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V8z' }], ['path', { d: 'M14 3v5h5M9 13h6M9 17h6' }]],
    payments: [['circle', { cx: 12, cy: 12, r: 9 }], ['path', { d: 'M9 8h6M9 12h6M9 8c4 0 4 5 0 5l4 4' }]],
    // Admin
    overview: [['rect', { x: 3, y: 3, width: 7, height: 9, rx: 1.5 }], ['rect', { x: 14, y: 3, width: 7, height: 5, rx: 1.5 }], ['rect', { x: 14, y: 12, width: 7, height: 9, rx: 1.5 }], ['rect', { x: 3, y: 16, width: 7, height: 5, rx: 1.5 }]],
    users: [['circle', { cx: 9, cy: 8, r: 3.5 }], ['path', { d: 'M2.5 20c.6-3.4 3.2-5.5 6.5-5.5s5.9 2.1 6.5 5.5M16 4.8a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 0 6.4M18 14.8c2 .7 3.2 2.4 3.5 5.2' }]],
    products: [['path', { d: 'M20.6 13.4 13.4 20.6a2 2 0 0 1-2.8 0L3 13V3h10l7.6 7.6a2 2 0 0 1 0 2.8z' }], ['circle', { cx: 7.5, cy: 7.5, r: 1.2 }]],
    locations: [['path', { d: 'M20 10c0 6-8 12-8 12S4 16 4 10a8 8 0 0 1 16 0z' }], ['circle', { cx: 12, cy: 10, r: 3 }]]
  };
  var baseIcon = App.icon;
  App.icon = function (name, size) {
    var def = ICONS[name];
    if (!def) return baseIcon(name, size);
    var ns = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', svg = document.createElementNS(ns, 'svg');
    svg.setAttribute('class', 'ico'); svg.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 24 24'); svg.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    if (size) { svg.setAttribute('width', size); svg.setAttribute('height', size); }
    def.forEach(function (p) {
      var n = document.createElementNS(ns, p[0]);
      Object.keys(p[1]).forEach(function (k) { n.setAttribute(k, p[1][k]); });
      svg.appendChild(n);
    });
    return svg;
  };

  // ---------- breadcrumb: "Procurement / Purchase queue" instead of "Sales / ..." ----------
  var baseGo = App.go;
  App.go = function (name, params) {
    baseGo(name, params);
    if (PW.dept === 'sales') return;
    var path = App.byId('path'), b = path && path.querySelector('b');
    if (b) { App.clear(path).appendChild(document.createTextNode((PW.label[PW.dept] || '') + ' / ')); path.appendChild(b); }
  };

  // ---------- tab badges ----------
  /** Show a number bubble on a tab (the three Sales tabs are handled by Client.html itself). */
  PW.badge = function (name, count, title) {
    var btn = document.querySelector('#tabs [data-board="' + name + '"]');
    if (!btn) return;
    var n = btn.querySelector('.n');
    if (!n) { n = h('span', { class: 'n' }); btn.appendChild(n); }
    n.hidden = !(count > 0); n.textContent = count > 0 ? String(count) : '';
    if (title) n.setAttribute('title', title);
  };
  // What "needs attention" means outside Sales (Client.refreshAttn reads Sales tabs, which these roles cannot open)
  if (PW.dept === 'procurement') {
    App.refreshAttn = function () {
      App.api('pq.counts').then(function (d) {
        App.setAttn('requirements', { awaiting: d.awaiting });          // the existing bubble + bell entry
        PW.badge('purchase', d.draft, 'Draft purchase orders');
      }).catch(noop);
    };
  } else if (PW.dept === 'accounts') {
    App.refreshAttn = function () {
      App.api('acc.counts').then(function (d) {
        PW.badge('orderpay', d.toInvoice + d.overdue, 'Orders to invoice and overdue payments');
        PW.badge('billpay', d.toBill + d.payOverdue, 'Deliveries to bill and overdue supplier bills');
        PW.badge('audit', d.audit, 'Counts waiting for review');
      }).catch(noop);
    };
  } else if (PW.dept === 'admin') {
    App.refreshAttn = noop;        // the Overview board sets its own badge when it loads: the overview reads many tabs
  } else if (PW.dept === 'warehouse') {
    App.refreshAttn = function () {
      App.api('wh.counts').then(function (d) {
        PW.badge('receiving', d.receiving, 'Deliveries to receive');
        PW.badge('putaway', d.putaway, 'Lines waiting for a location');
        PW.badge('dispatch', d.dispatch, 'Orders to pick, pack, book or dispatch');
        PW.badge('audit', d.audit, 'Counts waiting for review');
      }).catch(noop);
    };
  }

  // ---------- shared lookups ----------
  var supP = null, locP = null;
  PW.supplierList = null; PW.locationList = null;
  PW.suppliers = function (force) {
    if (force || !supP) {
      var p = App.api('sup.list').then(function (d) { PW.supplierList = d; return d; });
      supP = p; p.catch(function () { if (supP === p) supP = null; });
    }
    return supP;
  };
  PW.locations = function () {
    if (!locP) {
      var p = App.api('loc.list').then(function (d) { PW.locationList = d; return d; });
      locP = p; p.catch(function () { if (locP === p) locP = null; });
    }
    return locP;
  };
  /** Type-ahead settings for a supplier picker */
  PW.supplierPicker = function (extra) {
    var o = { placeholder: 'Type a supplier name…', items: function () { return PW.supplierList; }, label: function (s) { return s.name; },
              sub: function (s) { return s.city || ''; }, none: 'No supplier found - add it on the Suppliers tab', max: 60, noun: 'suppliers' };
    Object.keys(extra || {}).forEach(function (k) { o[k] = extra[k]; });
    return o;
  };
  /** Type-ahead settings for a location picker. held = { code: pieces } shows what the item already has there. */
  PW.locationPicker = function (held, extra) {
    var o = { placeholder: 'Location', items: function () { return PW.locationList; }, label: function (l) { return l.code; },
              sub: function (l) { return [l.desc, held && held[l.code] ? 'has ' + held[l.code] + ' pcs of this item' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '); },
              none: 'No such location - check the Locations tab', max: 80, noun: 'locations' };
    Object.keys(extra || {}).forEach(function (k) { o[k] = extra[k]; });
    return o;
  };

  // ---------- small widgets ----------
  /** <select> from [{v,label}] or strings */
  PW.select = function (options, value, cls) {
    var sel = h('select', { class: cls || 'inp sm' });
    options.forEach(function (o) {
      var v = typeof o === 'string' ? o : o.v, l = typeof o === 'string' ? o : o.label;
      sel.appendChild(h('option', { value: v, text: l }));
    });
    sel.value = value === undefined || value === null ? '' : value;
    return sel;
  };
  /** Checkbox with a label. Returns { el, get(), set(v), input } */
  PW.check = function (label, on, onChange) {
    var cb = h('input', { type: 'checkbox' });
    cb.checked = !!on;
    if (onChange) cb.addEventListener('change', function () { onChange(cb.checked); });
    return { el: h('label', { class: 'pw-check' }, cb, ' ' + label), input: cb, get: function () { return cb.checked; }, set: function (v) { cb.checked = !!v; } };
  };
  /** Update the "N things" line + the chip labels with counts: chips = App.seg result, labels = [text, ...] in chip order, counts = [n|null, ...] */
  PW.chipCounts = function (chips, labels, counts) {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(chips.el.children, function (b, i) { b.textContent = labels[i] + (counts[i] ? ' · ' + counts[i] : ''); });
  };
  PW.pieces = function (n) { return App.fmtQty(n) + ' pcs'; };
  /** debounce a search box into load() */
  PW.search = function (placeholder, label, onChange) {
    var t = null, box = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'search', placeholder: placeholder, 'aria-label': label });
    box.addEventListener('input', function () { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(function () { onChange(box.value); }, 300); });
    return box;
  };
  /** Money as "Rs 1,23,456" (no decimals) or with paise: PW.rs(1234.5, 2) */
  PW.rs = function (n, dec) { return 'Rs ' + App.inr(n, dec === undefined ? 0 : dec); };
  /** a number card: PW.stat('To collect', 'Rs 1,200', 'note', 'c') */
  PW.stat = function (label, value, sub, tone) {
    return h('div', { class: 'stat' + (tone ? ' ' + tone : '') }, h('i', { text: tone === 'c' ? '!' : (tone === 'g' ? '✓' : '#') }), h('div', null, h('span', { text: label }), h('b', { text: String(value) }), sub ? h('span', { text: sub, style: 'font-weight:500' }) : null));
  };
  /** the link to another department's page (read from the department menu), or '' */
  PW.deptUrl = function (label) {
    var url = '';
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#dept-menu a.mi'), function (a) {
      var b = a.querySelector('b');
      if (b && b.textContent === label) url = a.getAttribute('href');
    });
    return url;
  };
  /** Put a title (and a line under it) in an App.drawer header */
  PW.drawerTitle = function (drawer, title, sub) {
    var head = drawer.el.querySelector('.drawer-h'), h2 = head.querySelector('h2'), p = head.querySelector('p');
    h2.textContent = title;
    if (!p) { p = h('p'); h2.parentNode.appendChild(p); }
    p.textContent = sub || '';
  };
  /** Download rows as a spreadsheet-friendly file. cols = [{ h, f(row) }] */
  PW.csv = function (name, cols, rows) {
    function q(v) { v = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : String(v); return /[",\n]/.test(v) ? '"' + v.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : v; }
    var lines = [cols.map(function (c) { return q(c.h); }).join(',')];
    rows.forEach(function (r) { lines.push(cols.map(function (c) { return q(c.f(r)); }).join(',')); });
    var blob = new Blob(['﻿' + lines.join('\r\n')], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' }), a = h('a', { href: URL.createObjectURL(blob), download: name });
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
  };
  /** When is money due? Same reading of the payment terms as the server ("30 days credit", "3 months credit", "100% advance", "After delivery") */
  PW.dueFrom = function (terms, iso) {
    var t = String(terms || '').toLowerCase(), m = /(\d+)\s*(day|month)/.exec(t), d = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso || '');
    if (!d) return '';
    function out(x) { return x.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (x.getUTCMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + x.getUTCDate()).slice(-2); }
    if (/credit/.test(t) && m) {
      var y = +d[1], mo = +d[2] - 1, day = +d[3];
      if (m[2] === 'day') return out(new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo, day + (+m[1]))));
      var last = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo + (+m[1]) + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();
      return out(new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo + (+m[1]), Math.min(day, last))));
    }
    return /advance|after delivery|cash/.test(t) ? iso : '';
  };
  /**
   * Record (or correct) one payment. o: { kind: 'Order' | 'GRN', id, no, party, balance (number or null), pay (an existing payment to correct), onDone() }
   * An order receipt is money Received, a purchase payment is money Paid; "refund" goes the other way.
   */
  PW.payDialog = function (o) {
    var edit = o.pay || null, isOrder = o.kind === 'Order', modes = (App.boot && App.boot.lists && App.boot.lists.paymentModes) || ['Cash', 'UPI', 'Bank Transfer', 'Cheque', 'Other'];
    var f = {
      date: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: edit ? edit.date : App.today(), max: App.today() }),
      amount: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'number', min: 0, step: '0.01', inputmode: 'decimal', value: edit ? edit.amount : (o.balance > 0.5 ? o.balance : '') }),
      mode: PW.select(modes, edit ? edit.mode : 'Bank Transfer', 'inp'),
      ref: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 60, value: edit ? edit.referenceNo : '', placeholder: 'UTR / cheque no. / receipt no.' }),
      notes: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 200, value: edit ? edit.notes : '', placeholder: 'Optional' })
    };
    var refund = edit ? null : PW.check(isOrder ? 'This is a refund to the customer' : 'This is a refund from the supplier', false);
    var verb = isOrder ? 'Receive' : 'Pay';
    var body = h('div', null,
      h('p', { class: 'mute', text: (edit ? 'Correct the payment on ' : (isOrder ? 'Money received for ' : 'Money paid for ')) + o.no + (o.party ? ' · ' + o.party : '') + (o.balance !== null && o.balance !== undefined && !edit ? ' · balance ' + PW.rs(o.balance, 2) : '') }),
      h('div', { class: 'grid' }, App.field('Date', f.date, { required: true }), App.field('Amount (Rs)', f.amount, { required: true }), App.field('How', f.mode, { required: true }), App.field('Reference', f.ref)),
      h('div', { style: 'margin-top:12px' }, App.field('Note', f.notes)), refund ? h('div', { style: 'margin-top:10px' }, refund.el) : null);
    function send(over) {
      var p = { date: f.date.value, amount: App.num(f.amount.value), mode: f.mode.value, referenceNo: f.ref.value, notes: f.notes.value };
      if (edit) { p.id = edit.id; return App.api('pay.save', p); }
      p.kind = o.kind; p.refId = o.id; p.refund = refund.get(); p.confirmOver = !!over;
      return App.api('pay.add', p);
    }
    App.modal({
      title: edit ? 'Correct payment' : (isOrder ? 'Receive money' : 'Pay supplier'), body: body,
      buttons: [{ label: 'Cancel' }, { label: edit ? 'Save changes' : verb, kind: 'primary', onclick: function (api, btn) {
        App.run(btn, function () {
          var amt = App.num(f.amount.value);
          if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { f.amount.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the amount.'); }
          if (!f.date.value) { f.date.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the date.'); }
          var over = !edit && !refund.get() && o.balance !== null && o.balance !== undefined && o.balance > 0 && amt > o.balance + 0.5;
          var go = over ? App.confirm({ title: 'More than the balance', message: PW.rs(amt, 2) + ' is more than the balance of ' + PW.rs(o.balance, 2) + ' on ' + o.no + '. Record it anyway?', ok: 'Record anyway' }) : Promise.resolve(true);
          return go.then(function (yes) {
            if (!yes) return null;
            return send(over).then(function () { api.close(); App.toast(edit ? 'Payment corrected' : (isOrder ? 'Receipt recorded' : 'Payment recorded'), 'ok'); if (o.onDone) return o.onDone(); });
          });
        });
      } }]
    });
    f.amount.focus();
  };

  PW.loadError = function (box, what, e) { App.clear(box).appendChild(h('div', { class: 'note err', text: 'Could not load ' + what + ': ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e) })); };
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF DeptShell =====
