# FILE 11 of 25 :  ProcQueueBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* ProcQueueBoard.html - the Procurement "Purchase queue": the purchase manager accepts or denies each reorder
   requirement, fills in the supplier, and turns accepted items into a purchase order.
   Registered as "requirements" on purpose: the tab bubble and the bell already know that name ("awaiting decision"). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('procurement')) return;
  var STATES = ['', 'Accepted', 'Ordered', 'Denied'];
  var GROUP_NOTE = { '': 'Waiting for your decision', 'Accepted': 'Accepted - still to be put on a purchase order', 'Ordered': 'On a purchase order', 'Denied': 'Declined' };
  var LABELS = ['Awaiting decision', 'Accepted', 'Ordered', 'Denied', 'All'];
  var B = { f: {}, filter: 'Awaiting', search: '', seq: 0, rows: [], counts: {}, sel: {}, listBox: null, countBox: null, bar: null, chips: null };

  function init(root) {
    var f = B.f;
    f.item = App.combo(App.productPicker({ placeholder: 'Search item name or code - or type a new item', onPick: itemChanged, onInput: function () { itemChanged(null); } }));
    f.info = h('div', { class: 'hint' });
    f.q = App.qty({ perSet: function () { return f._pcs; } });
    f.needBy = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date' });
    f.notes = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 300, placeholder: 'Optional' });
    var saveBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Add to queue' });
    saveBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(saveBtn, addStock); });
    [f.q.pcs, f.q.sets, f.needBy, f.notes].forEach(function (el) { el.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); saveBtn.click(); } }); });
    var form = h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'padding:20px 24px 22px' },
      h('div', { class: 'chead', style: 'margin-bottom:14px' }, h('h2', { text: 'Stock top-up' }),
        h('span', { class: 'mute', style: 'font-size:13px', text: 'An item you want in stock with no customer behind it. It joins the queue below, waiting for your decision.' })),
      h('div', { class: 'quick', style: 'grid-template-columns:minmax(220px,2fr) 96px 96px 150px minmax(140px,1fr) auto' },
        App.field('Item', f.item.el, { required: true, hint: f.info }), App.field('Pieces', f.q.pcs, { required: true }), App.field('or Sets', f.q.sets),
        App.field('Need by', f.needBy), App.field('Notes', f.notes), saveBtn));

    B.chips = App.seg({
      cls: 'chips-s', label: 'Filter requirements',
      options: [{ v: 'Awaiting', label: LABELS[0] }, { v: 'Accepted', label: LABELS[1] }, { v: 'Ordered', label: LABELS[2] }, { v: 'Denied', label: LABELS[3] }, { v: 'All', label: LABELS[4] }],
      value: B.filter, onChange: function (v) { B.filter = v; B.sel = {}; load(); }
    });
    var search = PW.search('Search party, item, supplier, notes…', 'Search requirements', function (v) { B.search = v; load(); });
    B.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); B.listBox = h('div');
    B.bar = h('div', { class: 'pw-bar', hidden: true });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Purchase queue' }),
      h('p', { text: 'Reorder requirements from Sales. Accept or deny each one, choose the supplier, then make the purchase order.' }))));
    root.appendChild(form);
    root.appendChild(h('div', null,
      h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'Requirements' }), B.chips.el, h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
        h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })),
      B.bar, B.countBox, B.listBox));
    PW.suppliers().catch(function () { return null; });
  }
  function itemChanged(it) {
    var f = B.f;
    if (it && !it.free) {
      var bits = [];
      if (it.pcs) bits.push(it.pcs + ' pcs per set');
      if (it.avail !== '' && it.avail !== undefined) bits.push('available: ' + it.avail + ' pcs');
      f.info.textContent = bits.join(' · '); f.info.classList.remove('warn'); f._pcs = Number(it.pcs) > 0 ? Number(it.pcs) : 0;
    } else {
      var t = f.item.text();
      f.info.textContent = t ? 'Not in the item list - saved as a new item.' : ''; f.info.classList.toggle('warn', !!t); f._pcs = 0;
    }
    f.q.refresh();
  }
  function addStock() {
    var f = B.f, item = f.item.get(), qty = f.q.get();
    if (!item) { f.item.focus(); throw new Error('Pick an item, or type the name of a new item.'); }
    if (!qty) { f.q.pcs.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the quantity - pieces or sets.'); }
    return App.api('pq.addStock', { itemCode: item.free ? '' : item.code, itemDesc: item.free ? item.name : '', sets: qty.sets, needBy: f.needBy.value, notes: f.notes.value }).then(function () {
      App.toast('Added to the queue - waiting for your decision', 'ok');
      f.item.clear(); f.q.clear(); f.notes.value = ''; f.info.textContent = ''; f._pcs = 0; f.item.focus();
      B.filter = 'Awaiting'; B.chips.set('Awaiting'); return load();
    });
  }

  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return Promise.all([App.api('pq.list', { filter: B.filter, search: B.search }), PW.suppliers().catch(function () { return null; }), App.products().catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (res) {
      var d = res[0];
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.rows = d.rows; B.counts = d.counts;
      Object.keys(B.sel).forEach(function (id) { if (!d.rows.some(function (r) { return r.id === id; })) delete B.sel[id]; });
      App.setAttn('requirements', { awaiting: d.counts.awaiting });
      PW.chipCounts(B.chips, LABELS, [d.counts.awaiting, d.counts.accepted, d.counts.ordered, d.counts.denied, 0]);
      B.chips.el.children[1].classList.toggle('pw-hot', d.counts.accepted > 0);     // accepted, not yet on a purchase order
      B.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' requirement' : ' requirements') + (d.shown > d.rows.length ? ' - showing the first ' + d.rows.length : '') + ' · soonest need-by first';
      renderList(); renderBar();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.listBox, 'the queue', e); } });
  }

  // ---------- actions ----------
  function decide(ids, decision, supplierId) {
    return App.api('pq.decide', { ids: ids, decision: decision, supplierId: supplierId || '' }).then(function (r) {
      App.toast((decision === 'Accepted' ? 'Accepted ' : decision === 'Denied' ? 'Denied ' : 'Moved back to the queue: ') + r.done + (r.done === 1 ? ' requirement' : ' requirements') +
        (r.skipped ? ' (' + r.skipped + ' already on a purchase order were left alone)' : ''), r.skipped ? 'warn' : 'ok');
      B.sel = {}; return load();
    });
  }
  function selIds() { return Object.keys(B.sel).filter(function (k) { return B.sel[k]; }); }
  function selRows() { var s = B.sel; return B.rows.filter(function (r) { return s[r.id]; }); }
  function makePo(rows) {
    var sups = {}; rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r.supplierId) sups[r.supplierId] = true; });
    var one = Object.keys(sups).length === 1 ? Object.keys(sups)[0] : '';
    App.go('purchase', { reqIds: rows.map(function (r) { return r.id; }), supplierId: one });
  }

  /**
   * Accepting asks for the supplier: choosing one confirms the acceptance. A supplier that is not in the list can be added right here.
   * rows = the requirements being accepted (one row, or the selected rows).
   */
  function askSupplier(rows) {
    var ids = rows.map(function (r) { return r.id; });
    var same = rows.every(function (r) { return r.supplierId && r.supplierId === rows[0].supplierId; });
    var sup = App.combo(PW.supplierPicker({ placeholder: 'Type the supplier name…', none: 'Not in the list - use "Add a new supplier" below' }));
    if (same) (PW.supplierList || []).forEach(function (s) { if (s.id === rows[0].supplierId) sup.set(s); });
    var nf = {
      name: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 120, placeholder: 'Supplier company name' }),
      contact: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 80, placeholder: 'Contact person' }),
      whatsapp: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'tel', maxlength: 30, placeholder: 'WhatsApp / phone' }),
      city: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 60, placeholder: 'City' })
    };
    var newBox = h('div', { hidden: true, style: 'margin-top:12px' }, h('div', { class: 'lbl', text: 'New supplier' }),
      h('div', { class: 'grid', style: 'grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px' }, App.field('Company name', nf.name, { required: true }), App.field('Contact person', nf.contact), App.field('WhatsApp / phone', nf.whatsapp), App.field('City', nf.city)));
    var toggle = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm link', style: 'margin-top:8px', text: 'Not in the list? Add a new supplier' });
    toggle.addEventListener('click', function () { newBox.hidden = false; toggle.hidden = true; if (!nf.name.value) nf.name.value = sup.text(); nf.name.focus(); });
    var what = rows.length === 1 ? '“' + rows[0].item + '”' : rows.length + ' items';
    App.modal({
      title: 'Accept ' + (rows.length === 1 ? 'requirement' : rows.length + ' requirements'),
      body: h('div', null, h('p', { text: 'Who will you order ' + what + ' from? Choosing the supplier accepts it.' }), App.field('Supplier', sup.el, { required: true }), toggle, newBox),
      buttons: [{ label: 'Cancel' }, { label: 'Accept', kind: 'primary', onclick: function (api, btn) {
        App.run(btn, function () {
          function accept(id) { return decide(ids, 'Accepted', id).then(function () { api.close(); }); }
          if (!newBox.hidden) {
            if (!nf.name.value.trim()) { nf.name.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the new supplier’s company name.'); }
            return App.api('sup.save', { name: nf.name.value, contact: nf.contact.value, whatsapp: nf.whatsapp.value, city: nf.city.value }).then(function (r) {
              App.toast('Supplier added: ' + nf.name.value.trim(), 'ok');
              return PW.suppliers(true).catch(function () { return null; }).then(function () { return accept(r.id); });
            });
          }
          var chosen = sup.get();
          if (!chosen) { sup.focus(); throw new Error('Pick the supplier from the list, or add a new one.'); }
          return accept(chosen.id);
        });
      } }]
    });
  }

  function renderBar() {
    var ids = selIds(), rows = selRows();
    B.bar.hidden = !ids.length;
    if (!ids.length) return;
    var undecided = rows.every(function (r) { return r.state === ''; }), allAccepted = rows.every(function (r) { return r.state === 'Accepted'; });
    App.clear(B.bar).appendChild(h('b', { text: ids.length + ' selected' }));
    B.bar.appendChild(h('span', { class: 'sp' }));
    if (undecided) {
      B.bar.appendChild(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Accept', onclick: function () { askSupplier(rows); } }));
      B.bar.appendChild(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn danger', text: 'Deny', onclick: function () { decide(ids, 'Denied').catch(function (e) { App.toast(e.message, 'err'); }); } }));
    }
    if (allAccepted) B.bar.appendChild(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Make purchase order', onclick: function () { makePo(rows); } }));
    B.bar.appendChild(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Clear', onclick: function () { B.sel = {}; renderList(); renderBar(); } }));
  }

  // ---------- table ----------
  function stateBadge(s) {
    if (!s) return App.badge('Awaiting decision', 'amber');
    return App.badge(s, s === 'Denied' ? 'red' : (s === 'Accepted' ? 'green' : 'blue'));
  }
  function stockLine(r) {
    if (!r.stock) return null;
    var s = r.stock, bits = ['in warehouse ' + App.fmtQty(s.wh)];
    if (s.reserved) bits.push('reserved ' + App.fmtQty(s.reserved));
    if (s.onOrder) bits.push('on order ' + App.fmtQty(s.onOrder));
    return h('span', { class: 'sub' + (s.avail !== '' && s.avail < 0 ? ' late' : ''), text: bits.join(' · ') + ' pcs' });
  }
  function showSup() { return B.filter !== 'Awaiting'; }      // while reviewing there is no supplier yet, so no supplier column
  function rowEl(r) {
    var q = App.qtyOf(r.sets, r.itemCode), today = App.today(), late = r.needBy && r.needBy < today && (r.state === '' || r.state === 'Accepted');
    var cb = h('input', { type: 'checkbox', 'aria-label': 'Select ' + r.item, disabled: r.state === 'Ordered' });
    cb.checked = !!B.sel[r.id];
    cb.addEventListener('change', function () { B.sel[r.id] = cb.checked; renderBar(); });
    var supCell = r.supplier ? [r.supplier] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' });
    if (r.state === 'Accepted') supCell = [r.supplier || h('span', { class: 'late', text: 'No supplier' }), h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm link', text: 'Change', onclick: function () { askSupplier([r]); } })];
    var act = [];
    if (r.state === '') {
      act.push(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm primary', text: 'Accept', onclick: function () { askSupplier([r]); } }));
      act.push(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm danger', text: 'Deny', onclick: function (e) { App.run(e.currentTarget, function () { return decide([r.id], 'Denied'); }); } }));
    } else if (r.state === 'Accepted') {
      act.push(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm primary', text: 'Make PO', onclick: function () { makePo([r]); } }));
      act.push(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: 'Deny', onclick: function (e) { App.run(e.currentTarget, function () { return decide([r.id], 'Denied'); }); } }));
    } else if (r.state === 'Denied') {
      act.push(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: 'Reopen', onclick: function (e) { App.run(e.currentTarget, function () { return decide([r.id], ''); }); } }));
    } else act.push(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm link', text: r.poNo || 'Open PO', onclick: function () { App.go('purchase', { open: r.poId }); } }));
    return h('tr', { class: (B.sel[r.id] ? 'sel' : '') + (r.state === 'Accepted' ? ' pw-ready' : '') },
      h('td', { class: 'pw-c' }, cb),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Date', text: App.fmtDate(r.date) }),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'For' }, r.party ? [h('b', { text: r.party }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: r.forType })] : [h('b', { text: 'Stock' }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: r.salesman })]),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Item' }, r.item, h('span', { class: 'sub', text: r.isNew ? 'New item - not in Products yet' : r.itemCode }), stockLine(r)),
      h('td', { class: 'num', 'data-l': 'Qty' }, q.main, q.sub ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: q.sub }) : null),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Need by' }, r.needBy ? [App.fmtDate(r.needBy), late ? h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: 'Overdue' }) : null] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' })),
      showSup() ? h('td', { 'data-l': 'Supplier' }, supCell) : null,
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Status' }, stateBadge(r.state), r.state === 'Accepted' ? h('span', { class: 'sub pw-todo', text: 'Ready to order' }) : null, r.poNo ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'PO ' + r.poNo }) : null, r.quoteNo ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'Quote ' + r.quoteNo }) : null),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Notes' }, r.notes ? h('div', { class: 'clip', title: r.notes, text: r.notes }) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' })),
      h('td', { class: 'acts' }, act));
  }
  function renderList() {
    App.clear(B.listBox);
    if (B.filter === 'Accepted' && B.rows.length) B.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'note pw-banner' }, h('b', { text: B.rows.length + (B.rows.length === 1 ? ' item is' : ' items are') + ' accepted but not ordered yet. ' }), 'Select them and press Make purchase order.'));
    if (!B.rows.length) { B.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('div', { class: 'empty', text: B.filter === 'Awaiting' ? 'Nothing is waiting for a decision.' : 'No requirements found.' }))); return; }
    var all = h('input', { type: 'checkbox', 'aria-label': 'Select all' });
    var selectable = B.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.state !== 'Ordered'; });
    all.checked = selectable.length > 0 && selectable.every(function (r) { return B.sel[r.id]; });
    all.addEventListener('change', function () { selectable.forEach(function (r) { B.sel[r.id] = all.checked; }); renderList(); renderBar(); });
    var head = h('tr', null, h('th', { class: 'pw-c' }, all), h('th', { text: 'Date' }), h('th', { text: 'For' }), h('th', { text: 'Item' }), h('th', { class: 'num', text: 'Qty' }),
      h('th', { text: 'Need by' }), showSup() ? h('th', { text: 'Supplier' }) : null, h('th', { text: 'Status' }), h('th', { text: 'Notes' }), h('th', null, h('span', { class: 'sr', text: 'Actions' })));
    var body = h('tbody');
    if (B.filter === 'All') {
      STATES.forEach(function (s) {
        var g = B.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.state === s; });
        if (!g.length) return;
        body.appendChild(h('tr', { class: 'grp' }, h('td', { colspan: showSup() ? '10' : '9' }, h('div', { class: 'gh' }, stateBadge(s), h('span', { class: 'gc', text: String(g.length) }), h('span', { class: 'm', text: GROUP_NOTE[s] })))));
        g.forEach(function (r) { body.appendChild(rowEl(r)); });
      });
    } else B.rows.forEach(function (r) { body.appendChild(rowEl(r)); });
    B.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('table', { class: 't' }, h('thead', null, head), body)));
  }

  App.register('requirements', 'Purchase queue', { init: init, show: function () { App.refreshLookups(); PW.suppliers(true).catch(function () { return null; }); load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF ProcQueueBoard =====

# FILE 12 of 25 :  PurchaseOrdersBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* PurchaseOrdersBoard.html - Procurement: make a purchase order from accepted requirements (or any item), keep it as a
   draft, then send it. Sending marks it On Order, makes the PDF and opens the goods receipt for the Warehouse.
   Prices are Rs per piece, GST-exclusive. Quantities are in SETS (pieces per set x sets = pieces). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('procurement')) return;
  var LABELS = ['All', 'Draft', 'Awaiting delivery', 'Received'];
  var P = { filter: 'All', search: '', seq: 0, rows: [], counts: {}, listBox: null, countBox: null, chips: null, stats: null, drawer: null, body: null, btn: {}, F: null, cands: [] };

  function init(root) {
    P.chips = App.seg({
      cls: 'chips-s', label: 'Filter purchase orders',
      options: [{ v: 'All', label: LABELS[0] }, { v: 'Draft', label: LABELS[1] }, { v: 'Awaiting', label: LABELS[2] }, { v: 'Received', label: LABELS[3] }],
      value: P.filter, onChange: function (v) { P.filter = v; load(); }
    });
    var search = PW.search('Search PO number, supplier…', 'Search purchase orders', function (v) { P.search = v; load(); });
    P.stats = h('div', { class: 'pw-stats' });
    P.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); P.listBox = h('div');
    var newBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary' }, App.icon('plus', 16), 'New purchase order');
    newBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { openNew([], ''); });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Purchase orders' }),
      h('p', { text: 'Order from a supplier. A draft can be changed; once sent it counts as On Order and the Warehouse sees the delivery coming.' })),
      h('div', { class: 'acts' }, newBtn)));
    root.appendChild(P.stats);
    root.appendChild(h('div', null,
      h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'All purchase orders' }), P.chips.el, h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
        h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })),
      P.countBox, P.listBox));
    buildDrawer();
  }

  // ---------- list ----------
  function load() {
    var seq = ++P.seq;
    P.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return Promise.all([App.api('po.list', { filter: P.filter, search: P.search }), PW.suppliers().catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (res) {
      var d = res[0];
      if (seq !== P.seq) return;
      P.rows = d.rows; P.counts = d.counts;
      PW.badge('purchase', d.counts.draft, 'Draft purchase orders');
      PW.chipCounts(P.chips, LABELS, [0, d.counts.draft, d.counts.awaiting, d.counts.received]);
      P.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' purchase order' : ' purchase orders');
      renderStats(); renderList();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === P.seq) { P.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(P.listBox, 'purchase orders', e); } });
  }
  function renderStats() {
    function stat(cls, icon, label, val) { return h('div', { class: 'stat ' + cls }, h('i', null, icon), h('div', null, h('span', { text: label }), h('b', { text: val }))); }
    App.clear(P.stats);
    P.stats.appendChild(stat('c', App.icon('clock', 22), 'Drafts to send', String(P.counts.draft || 0)));
    P.stats.appendChild(stat('', App.icon('purchase', 22), 'Awaiting delivery', String(P.counts.awaiting || 0)));
    P.stats.appendChild(stat('g', App.icon('check', 22), 'Received', String(P.counts.received || 0)));
  }
  function recvBadge(p) {
    if (p.status === 'Draft') return App.badge('Draft', 'amber');
    return App.badge(p.recv, p.recv === 'Received' ? 'green' : (p.recv === 'Part received' ? 'blue' : 'amber'));
  }
  function rowEl(p) {
    var open = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'ordno', text: p.no, 'aria-label': 'Open ' + p.no });
    open.addEventListener('click', function () { openPo(p.id); });
    var act = [h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: p.status === 'Draft' ? 'Edit' : 'View', onclick: function () { openPo(p.id); } })];
    if (p.status === 'Draft') {
      act.push(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm primary', text: 'Send', onclick: function (e) { App.run(e.currentTarget, function () { return sendNow(p); }); } }));
    } else act.push(h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm soft', text: 'PDF', onclick: function (e) { App.run(e.currentTarget, function () { return showPdf(p.id, p.no); }); } }));
    return h('tr', null,
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'PO' }, open, h('span', { class: 'sub', text: App.fmtDate(p.date) })),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Supplier', text: p.supplier || '–' }),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Items' }, p.lines + (p.lines === 1 ? ' item' : ' items'), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: App.fmtQty(p.sets) + ' sets' + (p.status === 'Sent' && p.open ? ' · ' + App.fmtQty(p.open) + ' open' : '') })),
      h('td', { class: 'num', 'data-l': 'Value', text: 'Rs ' + App.inr(p.value) }),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Delivery' }, p.delivery ? [App.fmtDate(p.delivery), p.late ? h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: 'Overdue' }) : null] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' })),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Status' }, recvBadge(p), p.terms ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: p.terms }) : null),
      h('td', { class: 'acts' }, act));
  }
  function renderList() {
    App.clear(P.listBox);
    if (!P.rows.length) { P.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('div', { class: 'empty', text: 'No purchase orders yet. Use New purchase order, or Make PO on the Purchase queue.' }))); return; }
    var head = h('tr', null, h('th', { text: 'PO' }), h('th', { text: 'Supplier' }), h('th', { text: 'Items' }), h('th', { class: 'num', text: 'Value (incl. GST)' }), h('th', { text: 'Delivery' }), h('th', { text: 'Status' }), h('th', null, h('span', { class: 'sr', text: 'Actions' })));
    var body = h('tbody');
    P.rows.forEach(function (p) { body.appendChild(rowEl(p)); });
    P.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('table', { class: 't' }, h('thead', null, head), body)));
  }
  function sendNow(p) {
    return App.confirm({ title: 'Send purchase order', message: 'Send ' + p.no + ' to ' + p.supplier + ' (Rs ' + App.inr(p.value) + ')? It will count as On Order, a PDF is made, and the Warehouse gets a goods receipt to receive against. It cannot be changed after this.', ok: 'Send' }).then(function (yes) {
      if (!yes) return;
      return App.api('po.send', { id: p.id }).then(function (r) { sentToast(r); return load(); });
    });
  }
  function sentToast(r) {
    App.toast('Purchase order ' + r.no + ' sent', 'ok');
    if (r.warning) App.toast(r.warning, 'warn');
  }
  function showPdf(id, no) {
    return App.api('po.pdf', { id: id }).then(function (d) {
      App.modal({ title: 'Purchase order ' + no, body: h('div', null, h('p', { text: 'The PDF is ready.' }), h('a', { class: 'btn primary', href: d.url, target: '_blank', rel: 'noopener', text: 'Open PDF' })), buttons: [{ label: 'Close' }] });
    });
  }

  // ---------- the order form (drawer) ----------
  function buildDrawer() {
    var b = P.btn;
    b.cancel = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Cancel', onclick: function () { P.drawer.close(); } });
    b.del = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn danger', text: 'Delete draft' });
    b.save = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Save draft' });
    b.send = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Save & send' });
    b.pdf = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn soft', text: 'Open PDF' });
    b.close = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Close', onclick: function () { P.drawer.close(); } });
    b.del.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(b.del, removeDraft); });
    b.save.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(b.save, function () { return save(false); }); });
    b.send.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(b.send, function () { return save(true); }); });
    b.pdf.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(b.pdf, function () { return showPdf(P.F.id, P.F.no); }); });
    P.body = h('div');
    P.drawer = App.drawer({ title: 'Purchase order', wide: true, body: P.body, footer: [b.cancel, b.del, b.save, b.send, b.pdf, b.close] });
  }
  function setTitle(t, sub) {
    var h2 = P.drawer.el.querySelector('.drawer-h h2'), p = P.drawer.el.querySelector('.drawer-h p');
    h2.textContent = t;
    if (!p && sub) { p = h('p'); h2.parentNode.appendChild(p); }
    if (p) p.textContent = sub || '';
  }
  function blankForm() { return { id: '', no: '', status: 'Draft', supplier: null, date: App.today(), delivery: '', terms: '', lines: [], uid: 0, grn: null, ui: { lines: {} } }; }

  /** reqIds: accepted requirements to start from (from the Purchase queue); supplierId: their common supplier.
      The form opens at once; the supplier list, items and accepted requirements fill in as they arrive. */
  function openNew(reqIds, supplierId) {
    P.F = blankForm();
    setTitle('New purchase order', 'Add accepted requirements, or any item you want to order.');
    App.clear(P.body).appendChild(h('div', { class: 'dp-b', text: 'Loading suppliers and items…' }));
    hideButtons(); P.drawer.open();
    Promise.all([PW.suppliers(), App.products(), loadCands()]).then(function () {
      if (!P.drawer.isOpen()) return;
      if (supplierId) (PW.supplierList || []).forEach(function (s) { if (s.id === supplierId) P.F.supplier = s; });
      var picked = P.cands.filter(function (r) { return reqIds.indexOf(r.id) >= 0; });
      picked.forEach(function (r) { P.F.lines.push(lineFromReq(r)); });
      if (!P.F.lines.length) P.F.lines.push(blankLine());
      renderForm();
    }).catch(function (e) { P.drawer.close(); App.toast('Could not open the purchase order form: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e), 'err'); });
  }
  function hideButtons() { Object.keys(P.btn).forEach(function (k) { P.btn[k].hidden = k !== 'cancel'; }); }
  function openPo(id) {
    setTitle('Purchase order', '');
    App.clear(P.body).appendChild(h('div', { class: 'dp-b', text: 'Loading…' }));
    hideButtons(); P.drawer.open();
    return Promise.all([App.api('po.get', { id: id }), PW.suppliers(), App.products(), loadCands()]).then(function (res) {
      if (!P.drawer.isOpen()) return;
      var d = res[0], F = blankForm();
      F.id = d.id; F.no = d.no; F.status = d.status; F.date = d.date; F.delivery = d.delivery; F.terms = d.terms; F.grn = d.grn; F.pdf = d.pdf;
      (PW.supplierList || []).forEach(function (s) { if (s.id === d.supplierId) F.supplier = s; });
      if (!F.supplier) F.supplier = { id: d.supplierId, name: d.supplier };
      P.F = F;
      d.lines.forEach(function (l) {
        F.lines.push({ uid: ++F.uid, id: l.id, reqId: l.reqId, itemCode: l.itemCode, desc: l.desc, name: l.name, pcs: l.pcs, sets: l.sets, rate: l.rate, gst: Math.round(l.gst * 10000) / 100,
                       supItemCode: l.supItemCode, supItemName: l.supItemName, party: l.party, open: l.open });
      });
      if (!F.lines.length && d.status === 'Draft') F.lines.push(blankLine());
      setTitle(d.status === 'Draft' ? 'Edit ' + d.no : d.no, d.status === 'Draft' ? 'Draft - not sent yet' : 'Sent - this order can no longer be changed');
      renderForm();
    }).catch(function (e) { P.drawer.close(); App.toast('Could not open the purchase order: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e), 'err'); });
  }
  function loadCands() {
    return App.api('pq.list', { filter: 'Accepted' }).then(function (d) { P.cands = d.rows; return d; });
  }
  function blankLine() { return { uid: ++P.F.uid, id: '', reqId: '', itemCode: '', desc: '', name: '', pcs: 1, sets: '', rate: '', gst: '', supItemCode: '', supItemName: '', party: '', open: 0 }; }
  function lineFromReq(r) {
    var p = r.itemCode ? App.findProduct(r.itemCode) : null;
    return { uid: ++P.F.uid, id: '', reqId: r.id, itemCode: r.itemCode, desc: r.itemCode ? '' : r.item, name: r.item, pcs: r.pcs || 1, sets: r.sets, rate: '', gst: p && p.gst !== '' ? p.gst : '',
             supItemCode: '', supItemName: '', party: r.party, open: 0 };
  }
  function used(reqId) { return P.F.lines.some(function (l) { return l.reqId === reqId; }); }

  function renderForm() {
    var F = P.F, b = P.btn, edit = F.status === 'Draft';
    b.cancel.hidden = !edit; b.save.hidden = !edit; b.send.hidden = !edit; b.del.hidden = !(edit && F.id); b.pdf.hidden = edit || !F.id; b.close.hidden = edit;
    App.clear(P.body);
    if (!edit) { renderReadOnly(); return; }
    var sup = App.combo(PW.supplierPicker({ onPick: function (s) { F.supplier = s; renderCands(); }, onInput: function () { F.supplier = null; renderCands(); } }));
    if (F.supplier) sup.set(F.supplier);
    var date = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: F.date || '' }), delivery = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: F.delivery || '' });
    var terms = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 80, value: F.terms || '', list: 'pw-terms', placeholder: 'e.g. 40% advance' });
    var dl = h('datalist', { id: 'pw-terms' }, (App.boot.lists.paymentTerms || []).map(function (t) { return h('option', { value: t }); }));
    date.addEventListener('input', function () { F.date = date.value; }); delivery.addEventListener('input', function () { F.delivery = delivery.value; }); terms.addEventListener('input', function () { F.terms = terms.value; });
    F.ui.sup = sup;
    P.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '1' }), h('h2', { text: 'Supplier' }))),
      h('div', { class: 'grid' }, h('div', { class: 'field span2' }, h('label', null, 'Supplier', h('span', { class: 'req', text: ' *' })), sup.el),
        App.field('PO date', date), App.field('Delivery by', delivery), App.field('Payment terms', terms)), dl));
    F.ui.candBox = h('div'); P.body.appendChild(F.ui.candBox);
    F.ui.linesBox = h('div', { class: 'lines' });
    var add = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn soft', text: '+ Add item', onclick: function () { var l = blankLine(); F.lines.push(l); renderLines(); focusLast(); } });
    P.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '2' }), h('h2', { text: 'Items' })),
      h('span', { class: 'mute', style: 'font-size:13px', text: 'Rate is per piece, excluding GST. Quantity is in sets.' })), F.ui.linesBox, h('div', { style: 'margin-top:14px' }, add)));
    var tx = function (label, cls) { var bb = h('b', { text: 'Rs 0.00' }); return { el: h('div', { class: 't1' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') }, h('span', { text: label }), bb), b: bb }; };
    F.ui.tTax = tx('Taxable value'); F.ui.tGst = tx('GST'); F.ui.tAll = tx('Total', 'g');
    P.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'dock', role: 'region', 'aria-label': 'Order total', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'sp' }), F.ui.tTax.el, F.ui.tGst.el, F.ui.tAll.el));
    renderCands(); renderLines();
  }
  function focusLast() { var l = P.F.lines[P.F.lines.length - 1]; if (l && P.F.ui.lines[l.uid]) P.F.ui.lines[l.uid].item.focus(); }

  function renderCands() {
    var F = P.F, box = F.ui.candBox;
    if (!box) return;
    App.clear(box);
    var list = P.cands.filter(function (r) { return !used(r.id) && (!F.supplier || !r.supplierId || r.supplierId === F.supplier.id); });
    if (!list.length) return;
    var addAll = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm soft', text: 'Add all', onclick: function () { addReqs(list); } });
    var grid = h('div', { class: 'reqs' }, list.map(function (r) {
      var q = App.qtyOf(r.sets, r.itemCode);
      return h('div', { class: 'reqrow' }, h('div', null, h('b', { text: r.item }),
        h('small', { text: [q.main, r.party || 'Stock', r.supplier && !F.supplier ? r.supplier : '', r.needBy ? 'need by ' + App.fmtDate(r.needBy) : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') })),
        h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm primary', text: 'Add', onclick: function () { addReqs([r]); } }));
    }));
    box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card reqbox', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '+' }), h('h2', { text: 'Accepted requirements waiting for an order (' + list.length + ')' })), addAll), grid));
  }
  function addReqs(rs) {
    var F = P.F;
    F.lines = F.lines.filter(function (l) { return !isBlank(l); });
    rs.forEach(function (r) { if (!used(r.id)) F.lines.push(lineFromReq(r)); });
    if (!F.lines.length) F.lines.push(blankLine());
    if (!F.supplier) {
      var sid = rs[0].supplierId;
      if (sid && rs.every(function (r) { return r.supplierId === sid; })) (PW.supplierList || []).forEach(function (s) { if (s.id === sid) { F.supplier = s; F.ui.sup.set(s); } });
    }
    renderCands(); renderLines();
  }
  function isBlank(l) { return !l.itemCode && !l.desc && !l.reqId && l.sets === '' && l.rate === ''; }

  function renderLines() {
    var F = P.F;
    App.clear(F.ui.linesBox); F.ui.lines = {};
    F.lines.forEach(function (l, i) { F.ui.linesBox.appendChild(lineRow(l, i)); });
    recalc();
  }
  function num(v) { var n = App.num(v); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; }
  function lineRow(l, i) {
    var F = P.F, u = {};
    F.ui.lines[l.uid] = u;
    u.item = App.combo(App.productPicker({
      placeholder: 'Item name or code - or type a new item',
      onPick: function (it) {
        if (it.free) { l.itemCode = ''; l.desc = it.name; l.name = it.name; }
        else { l.itemCode = it.code; l.desc = ''; l.name = it.name; l.pcs = it.pcs > 0 ? it.pcs : 1; u.pcs.value = l.pcs; if (it.gst !== '') { l.gst = it.gst; u.gst.value = it.gst; } }
        recalc();
      },
      onInput: function (t) { l.itemCode = ''; l.desc = t; l.name = t; }
    }));
    var p = l.itemCode ? App.findProduct(l.itemCode) : null;
    if (p) u.item.set(p); else if (l.name) u.item.setText(l.name);
    if (l.reqId) u.item.disable(true);
    function inp(key, extra) { var el = h('input', { class: 'inp sm', type: 'number', step: 'any', min: 0, inputmode: 'decimal', value: l[key] === '' ? '' : l[key] }); Object.keys(extra || {}).forEach(function (k) { el.setAttribute(k, extra[k]); }); el.addEventListener('input', function () { l[key] = el.value; recalc(); }); return el; }
    u.pcs = inp('pcs', { min: 1 }); u.sets = inp('sets'); u.rate = inp('rate'); u.gst = inp('gst', { max: 100 });
    u.tot = h('div', { class: 'amt', style: 'font-size:15px' }); u.amt = h('div', { class: 'amt' });
    var rm = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'x rm', title: 'Remove this item', 'aria-label': 'Remove item', text: '×' });
    rm.addEventListener('click', function () { F.lines.splice(F.lines.indexOf(l), 1); if (!F.lines.length) F.lines.push(blankLine()); renderCands(); renderLines(); });
    function cell(label, ctl) { return h('div', null, h('label', { text: label }), ctl); }
    var sup = h('input', { class: 'inp sm', type: 'text', maxlength: 60, placeholder: 'Optional', value: l.supItemCode || '' });
    sup.addEventListener('input', function () { l.supItemCode = sup.value; });
    return h('div', { class: 'line' },
      h('div', { class: 'line-top' }, h('span', { class: 'no', 'aria-hidden': 'true', text: String(i + 1) }),
        h('div', { class: 'item' }, h('label', { text: 'Item' }), u.item.el, h('div', { class: 'hint' + (l.itemCode ? '' : ' warn'), text: l.reqId ? 'For ' + (l.party || 'stock') + (l.itemCode ? ' · ' + l.itemCode : '') : (l.itemCode ? l.itemCode : (l.desc ? 'New item - add it to Products before it is put away' : '')) })), rm),
      h('div', { class: 'line-nums' }, cell('Pcs / set', u.pcs), cell('Sets', u.sets), cell('Rate Rs/pc', u.rate), cell('GST %', u.gst), cell('Supplier code', sup),
        h('div', null, h('label', { text: 'Total pcs' }), u.tot), h('div', null, h('label', { text: 'Amount (incl. GST)' }), u.amt)));
  }
  function recalc() {
    var F = P.F, tax = 0, gst = 0;
    F.lines.forEach(function (l) {
      var u = F.ui.lines[l.uid], pcs = Math.max(1, num(l.pcs)), net = App.r2(pcs * num(l.sets) * num(l.rate)), g = App.r2(net * num(l.gst) / 100);
      tax += net; gst += g;
      if (u) { u.tot.textContent = App.fmtQty(pcs * num(l.sets)) + ' pcs'; App.clear(u.amt).appendChild(document.createTextNode('Rs ' + App.inr(net + g))); u.amt.appendChild(h('small', { text: 'GST ' + App.inr(g) })); }
    });
    F.ui.tTax.b.textContent = 'Rs ' + App.inr(tax); F.ui.tGst.b.textContent = 'Rs ' + App.inr(gst); F.ui.tAll.b.textContent = 'Rs ' + App.inr(App.r2(tax + gst));
  }

  function renderReadOnly() {
    var F = P.F, net = 0, gst = 0;
    P.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card' }, h('dl', { class: 'kv' },
      h('dt', { text: 'Supplier' }), h('dd', null, h('b', { text: F.supplier ? F.supplier.name : '' })),
      h('dt', { text: 'PO date' }), h('dd', { text: App.fmtDate(F.date) }), h('dt', { text: 'Delivery by' }), h('dd', { text: F.delivery ? App.fmtDate(F.delivery) : 'as agreed' }),
      h('dt', { text: 'Payment' }), h('dd', { text: F.terms || 'as agreed' }),
      F.grn ? h('dt', { text: 'Goods receipt' }) : null, F.grn ? h('dd', { text: F.grn.no + ' · ' + F.grn.status }) : null)));
    var cols = [
      { h: 'Item', f: function (l) { return [l.name, l.itemCode ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: l.itemCode }) : h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: 'New item' }), l.party ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'For ' + l.party }) : null]; } },
      { h: 'Pcs × sets', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return l.pcs + ' × ' + App.fmtQty(l.sets) + ' = ' + App.fmtQty(l.pcs * l.sets); } },
      { h: 'Open sets', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return l.open > 0 ? App.fmtQty(l.open) : h('span', { class: 'pw-pos', text: 'Received' }); } },
      { h: 'Rate Rs/pc', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return App.inr(l.rate); } },
      { h: 'GST', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return Math.round(l.gst * 10000) / 100 + '%'; } },
      { h: 'Amount', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { var n = l.pcs * l.sets * l.rate; net += n; gst += n * l.gst; return App.inr(n * (1 + l.gst)); } }
    ];
    P.body.appendChild(h('div', { style: 'margin-top:16px' }, App.table(cols, F.lines, 'No lines.')));
    P.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'totals' }, h('span', null, 'Taxable', h('b', { text: 'Rs ' + App.inr(net) })), h('span', null, 'GST', h('b', { text: 'Rs ' + App.inr(gst) })), h('span', null, 'Total', h('b', { text: 'Rs ' + App.inr(net + gst) }))));
  }

  // ---------- save / send / delete ----------
  function payload(send) {
    var F = P.F;
    if (!F.supplier || !F.supplier.id) { F.ui.sup.focus(); throw new Error('Pick the supplier from the list.'); }
    var real = F.lines.filter(function (l) { return !isBlank(l); });
    if (!real.length) throw new Error('Add at least one item.');
    var lines = real.map(function (l, i) {
      var n = 'Item ' + (i + 1) + ': ';
      if (!l.itemCode && !U_s(l.desc)) throw new Error(n + 'pick an item, or type the name of a new item.');
      if (!(num(l.sets) > 0)) throw new Error(n + 'enter the quantity in sets.');
      if (send && !(num(l.rate) > 0)) throw new Error(n + 'enter the rate before sending.');
      return { id: l.id, reqId: l.reqId, itemCode: l.itemCode, desc: l.desc, supItemCode: l.supItemCode, supItemName: l.supItemName, pcs: Math.max(1, num(l.pcs)), sets: num(l.sets), rate: num(l.rate), gst: num(l.gst) / 100 };
    });
    return { id: F.id, supplierId: F.supplier.id, date: F.date, delivery: F.delivery, terms: F.terms, lines: lines, send: !!send };
  }
  function U_s(v) { return String(v === null || v === undefined ? '' : v).trim(); }
  function save(send) {
    var body = payload(send);
    var go = function () {
      return App.api('po.save', body).then(function (r) {
        if (r.sent) sentToast(r); else App.toast('Draft ' + r.no + ' saved', 'ok');
        P.drawer.close(); return Promise.all([load(), loadCands()]);
      });
    };
    if (!send) return go();
    var total = P.F.ui.tAll.b.textContent;
    return App.confirm({ title: 'Send purchase order', message: 'Save and send this order to ' + P.F.supplier.name + ' (' + total + ')? It will count as On Order, a PDF is made, and the Warehouse gets a goods receipt to receive against. It cannot be changed after this.', ok: 'Save & send' }).then(function (yes) { if (yes) return go(); });
  }
  function removeDraft() {
    return App.confirm({ title: 'Delete draft', danger: true, ok: 'Delete', cancel: 'Keep it', message: 'Delete draft ' + P.F.no + '? Its requirements go back to the Purchase queue.' }).then(function (yes) {
      if (!yes) return;
      return App.api('po.delete', { id: P.F.id }).then(function () { App.toast('Draft deleted', 'ok'); P.drawer.close(); return load(); });
    });
  }

  App.register('purchase', 'Purchase orders', {
    init: init,
    show: function (p) {
      App.refreshLookups(); PW.suppliers(true).catch(function () { return null; });
      load();
      if (p && p.reqIds && p.reqIds.length) openNew(p.reqIds, p.supplierId || '');
      else if (p && p.open) openPo(p.open);
    }
  });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF PurchaseOrdersBoard =====

# FILE 13 of 25 :  SuppliersBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* SuppliersBoard.html - Procurement: the supplier list. Other tables store the Supplier ID, never the name. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('procurement')) return;
  var B = { f: {}, rows: [], search: '', seq: 0, editId: '', listBox: null, countBox: null, title: null, saveBtn: null, cancelBtn: null };
  var FIELDS = [
    ['name', 'Company name', 'text', 120, true], ['contact', 'Contact person', 'text', 80], ['whatsapp', 'WhatsApp / phone', 'tel', 30], ['email', 'Email', 'email', 120],
    ['gstin', 'GSTIN', 'text', 15], ['city', 'City', 'text', 60], ['address', 'Address', 'text', 200], ['categories', 'Products they supply', 'text', 200]
  ];

  function init(root) {
    var f = B.f;
    FIELDS.forEach(function (d) { f[d[0]] = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: d[2], maxlength: d[3] }); });
    B.saveBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Save supplier' });
    B.cancelBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Cancel edit', hidden: true, onclick: reset });
    B.saveBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(B.saveBtn, save); });
    B.title = h('h2', { text: 'Add a supplier' });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Suppliers' }), h('p', { text: 'The companies you buy from. Pick one on a purchase order.' }))));
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, B.title),
      h('div', { class: 'grid' }, FIELDS.map(function (d) { return App.field(d[1], f[d[0]], { required: !!d[4], cls: d[0] === 'address' || d[0] === 'categories' ? 'span2' : '' }); })),
      h('div', { class: 'actions' }, B.saveBtn, B.cancelBtn)));
    var search = PW.search('Search name, city, contact…', 'Search suppliers', function (v) { B.search = v; render(); });
    B.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); B.listBox = h('div');
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'All suppliers' }), h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), B.countBox, B.listBox));
  }
  function reset() {
    B.editId = ''; Object.keys(B.f).forEach(function (k) { B.f[k].value = ''; });
    B.title.textContent = 'Add a supplier'; B.saveBtn.textContent = 'Save supplier'; B.cancelBtn.hidden = true;
  }
  function edit(s) {
    B.editId = s.id; Object.keys(B.f).forEach(function (k) { B.f[k].value = s[k] || ''; });
    B.title.textContent = 'Edit ' + s.name; B.saveBtn.textContent = 'Save changes'; B.cancelBtn.hidden = false;
    B.f.name.focus(); window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }
  function save() {
    var f = B.f, p = { id: B.editId };
    if (!f.name.value.trim()) { f.name.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the supplier company name.'); }
    Object.keys(f).forEach(function (k) { p[k] = f[k].value; });
    return App.api('sup.save', p).then(function () {
      App.toast('Supplier saved: ' + f.name.value.trim(), 'ok'); reset(); PW.suppliers(true).catch(function () { return null; }); return load();
    });
  }
  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return App.api('sup.list').then(function (rows) {
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.rows = rows; render();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.listBox, 'suppliers', e); } });
  }
  function render() {
    var rows = B.rows.filter(function (s) { var q = B.search.toLowerCase(); return !q || [s.name, s.city, s.contact, s.categories].join(' ').toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0; });
    B.countBox.textContent = rows.length + (rows.length === 1 ? ' supplier' : ' suppliers');
    var cols = [
      { h: 'Supplier', f: function (s) { return h('div', { class: 'who2' }, h('i', { text: App.initials(s.name) }), h('div', null, h('b', { text: s.name }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: [s.contact, s.city].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') }))); } },
      { h: 'Phone', f: function (s) { return s.whatsapp ? h('a', { href: 'tel:' + String(s.whatsapp).replace(/[^0-9+]/g, ''), text: s.whatsapp }) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Email', f: function (s) { return s.email ? h('a', { href: 'mailto:' + s.email, text: s.email }) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'GSTIN', f: function (s) { return s.gstin || h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Supplies', f: function (s) { return h('div', { class: 'clip', title: s.categories, text: s.categories }); } },
      { h: 'POs', cls: 'num', f: function (s) { return s.pos ? String(s.pos) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '0' }); } },
      { h: '', cls: 'acts', f: function (s) { return h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: 'Edit', onclick: function () { edit(s); } }); } }
    ];
    App.clear(B.listBox).appendChild(App.table(cols, rows, 'No suppliers yet. Add one above.'));
  }

  App.register('suppliers', 'Suppliers', { init: init, show: function () { load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF SuppliersBoard =====

# FILE 14 of 25 :  ReceivingBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
# first line of this file is:  <script>

<script>
/* ReceivingBoard.html - Warehouse: goods arrive. Count them, note the damaged pieces, sticker them, then mark them received.
   A purchase order that was sent already has its goods receipt (GRN) here, "Yet to receive".
   Received quantity is in SETS in the sheet; type pieces or sets, either one. Damaged is in PIECES.
   "Fill Received Date only when goods arrive": Mark as received sets the date; Save count keeps a half-finished count. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('warehouse')) return;
  var LABELS = ['To receive', 'Received', 'All'];
  var R = { filter: 'To receive', search: '', seq: 0, rows: [], counts: {}, listBox: null, countBox: null, chips: null, drawer: null, body: null, btn: {}, G: null };

  function init(root) {
    R.chips = App.seg({
      cls: 'chips-s', label: 'Filter deliveries',
      options: [{ v: 'To receive', label: LABELS[0] }, { v: 'Received', label: LABELS[1] }, { v: 'All', label: LABELS[2] }], value: R.filter,
      onChange: function (v) { R.filter = v; load(); }
    });
    var search = PW.search('Search GRN, PO, supplier, invoice…', 'Search deliveries', function (v) { R.search = v; load(); });
    R.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); R.listBox = h('div');
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Receiving' }),
      h('p', { text: 'Open the delivery, count what arrived, note anything damaged and sticker it. Then mark it received and put it away.' }))));
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'Deliveries' }), R.chips.el, h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), R.countBox, R.listBox));
    var b = R.btn;
    b.cancel = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Close', onclick: function () { R.drawer.close(); } });
    b.save = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Save count' });
    b.recv = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Mark as received' });
    b.save.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(b.save, function () { return save(false); }); });
    b.recv.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(b.recv, function () { return save(true); }); });
    R.body = h('div');
    R.drawer = App.drawer({ title: 'Delivery', wide: true, body: R.body, footer: [b.cancel, b.save, b.recv] });
  }

  // ---------- list ----------
  function load() {
    var seq = ++R.seq;
    R.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return Promise.all([App.api('grn.list', { filter: R.filter, search: R.search }), App.products().catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (res) {
      var d = res[0];
      if (seq !== R.seq) return;
      R.rows = d.rows; R.counts = d.counts;
      PW.badge('receiving', d.counts.toReceive, 'Deliveries to receive');
      PW.chipCounts(R.chips, LABELS, [d.counts.toReceive, 0, 0]);
      R.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' delivery' : ' deliveries') + (R.filter === 'To receive' ? ' expected, soonest first' : '');
      renderList();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === R.seq) { R.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(R.listBox, 'deliveries', e); } });
  }
  function renderList() {
    App.clear(R.listBox);
    if (!R.rows.length) { R.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('div', { class: 'empty', text: R.filter === 'To receive' ? 'No deliveries are expected right now.' : 'No deliveries found.' }))); return; }
    var head = h('tr', null, h('th', { text: 'Delivery' }), h('th', { text: 'Supplier' }), h('th', { text: 'Expected by' }), h('th', { class: 'num', text: 'Items' }), h('th', { class: 'num', text: 'Pieces' }),
      h('th', { text: 'Supplier invoice' }), h('th', { text: 'Status' }), h('th', null, h('span', { class: 'sr', text: 'Actions' })));
    var body = h('tbody');
    R.rows.forEach(function (g) {
      var open = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'ordno', text: g.no, 'aria-label': 'Open ' + g.no });
      open.addEventListener('click', function () { openGrn(g.id); });
      body.appendChild(h('tr', null,
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Delivery' }, open, h('span', { class: 'sub', text: g.poNo ? 'PO ' + g.poNo : 'No PO' })),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Supplier', text: g.supplier || '–' }),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Expected by' }, g.delivery ? [App.fmtDate(g.delivery), g.late ? h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: 'Overdue' }) : null] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' })),
        h('td', { class: 'num', 'data-l': 'Items', text: String(g.lines) }),
        h('td', { class: 'num', 'data-l': 'Pieces' }, g.status === 'Received' ? App.fmtQty(g.recPcs) : App.fmtQty(g.expPcs), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: g.status === 'Received' ? 'of ' + App.fmtQty(g.expPcs) + ' ordered' : 'ordered' })),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Invoice' }, g.invoiceNo || h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }), g.invoiceDate ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: App.fmtDate(g.invoiceDate) }) : null),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Status' }, g.status === 'Received' ? App.badge('Received', 'green') : App.badge('Yet to receive', 'amber'),
          g.status === 'Received' ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: App.fmtDate(g.receivedDate) + (g.pending ? ' · ' + g.pending + (g.pending === 1 ? ' line' : ' lines') + ' to put away' : ' · all put away') }) : null),
        h('td', { class: 'acts' }, h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm' + (g.status === 'Received' ? '' : ' primary'), text: g.status === 'Received' ? 'View / edit' : 'Receive', onclick: function () { openGrn(g.id); } }))));
    });
    R.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('table', { class: 't' }, h('thead', null, head), body)));
  }

  // ---------- the count (drawer) ----------
  function openGrn(id) {
    return Promise.all([App.api('grn.get', { id: id }), App.products()]).then(function (res) {
      R.G = { d: res[0], lines: [], extra: [], uid: 0 };
      render(); R.drawer.open();
    }).catch(function (e) { App.toast(e.message, 'err'); });
  }
  function num(v) { var n = App.num(v); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; }
  function render() {
    var G = R.G, d = G.d, received = d.status === 'Received';
    var h2 = R.drawer.el.querySelector('.drawer-h h2'), p = R.drawer.el.querySelector('.drawer-h p');
    h2.textContent = d.no + (d.poNo ? ' · PO ' + d.poNo : '');
    if (!p) { p = h('p'); h2.parentNode.appendChild(p); }
    p.textContent = d.supplier + (d.delivery ? ' · expected ' + App.fmtDate(d.delivery) : '');
    R.btn.recv.hidden = received; R.btn.save.textContent = received ? 'Save changes' : 'Save count';
    App.clear(R.body);
    var f = G.f = {
      inv: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 60, value: d.invoiceNo || '', placeholder: 'Number on the supplier’s invoice' }),
      invDate: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: d.invoiceDate || '' }),
      cartons: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'number', min: 0, step: 1, value: d.cartons || '' }),
      recDate: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: d.receivedDate || App.today() })
    };
    R.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '1' }), h('h2', { text: 'Delivery details' })),
      received ? App.badge('Received ' + App.fmtDate(d.receivedDate), 'green') : App.badge('Yet to receive', 'amber')),
      h('div', { class: 'grid' }, App.field('Supplier invoice no.', f.inv), App.field('Invoice date', f.invDate), App.field('Cartons received', f.cartons), received ? null : App.field('Received on', f.recDate))));
    var fill = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm soft', text: 'Fill all as ordered', onclick: function () { G.lines.forEach(function (u) { if (!u.L.putAway && !u.q.get() && u.L.expSets > 0) u.q.setSets(u.L.expSets); }); } });
    G.box = h('div', { class: 'pw-lines' });
    R.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '2' }), h('h2', { text: 'Count the goods' })),
      received ? null : fill), G.box));
    d.lines.forEach(function (L) { G.box.appendChild(lineCard(L)); });
    G.extraBox = h('div', { class: 'pw-lines', style: 'margin-top:12px' });
    R.body.appendChild(h('div', { style: 'margin-top:14px' }, G.extraBox, h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn soft', text: '+ Item that was not on the order', onclick: function () { G.extraBox.appendChild(extraCard()); } })));
  }

  function qtyBits(L, per) {
    return L.expSets > 0 ? 'Ordered ' + App.fmtQty(L.expSets) + ' sets × ' + App.fmtQty(per) + ' = ' + App.fmtQty(L.expSets * per) + ' pcs' : 'Not on the purchase order';
  }
  function lineCard(L) {
    var G = R.G, u = { L: L };
    G.lines.push(u);
    var locked = L.putAway;
    u.per = h('input', { class: 'inp sm', type: 'number', min: 1, step: 'any', value: L.per, disabled: locked });
    u.q = App.qty({ cls: 'inp sm', perSet: function () { return num(u.per.value) >= 1 ? num(u.per.value) : 1; } });
    u.dmg = h('input', { class: 'inp sm', type: 'number', min: 0, step: 'any', value: L.damaged || '', placeholder: '0', disabled: locked });
    u.stk = PW.check('Stickered', L.stickered, function (on) { u.sq.hidden = !on; if (on && !u.sq.value) u.sq.value = good(u); });
    u.sq = h('input', { class: 'inp sm pw-n', type: 'number', min: 0, step: 'any', value: L.stickeredQty || '', placeholder: 'Pcs stickered', hidden: !L.stickered, disabled: locked });
    u.stk.input.disabled = locked;
    u.per.addEventListener('input', function () { u.q.refresh(); });
    if (L.recSets !== '' && L.recSets > 0) u.q.setSets(L.recSets);
    u.q.pcs.disabled = locked; u.q.sets.disabled = locked;
    if (!L.itemCode) {
      u.item = App.combo(App.productPicker({ placeholder: 'Pick the item (needed before put-away)', onPick: function (it) { if (!it.free) { u.itemCode = it.code; u.per.value = it.pcs > 0 ? it.pcs : 1; u.q.refresh(); } }, onInput: function () { u.itemCode = ''; } }));
      if (L.desc) u.item.setText(L.desc);
    }
    function cell(label, ctl) { return h('div', null, h('label', { text: label }), ctl); }
    return h('div', { class: 'line' },
      h('div', { class: 'pw-head' }, h('div', null, h('b', { text: L.name || 'New item' }), h('div', { class: 'pw-exp', text: (L.itemCode ? L.itemCode + ' · ' : '') + qtyBits(L, L.per) + (L.supItemCode ? ' · supplier code ' + L.supItemCode : '') })),
        locked ? h('span', { class: 'pw-lock', text: 'Put away to ' + L.location + ' · ' + App.fmtQty(L.putQty) + ' pcs' }) : null),
      u.item ? h('div', { style: 'margin-top:10px' }, u.item.el) : null,
      h('div', { class: 'line-nums', style: 'grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));margin-top:12px' }, cell('Pcs / set', u.per), cell('Received pcs', u.q.pcs), cell('or sets', u.q.sets), cell('Damaged pcs', u.dmg)),
      h('div', { class: 'pw-checks', style: 'padding-top:12px' }, u.stk.el, u.sq));
  }
  function good(u) { var q = u.q.get(); return q ? Math.max(0, App.r4(q.pcs - num(u.dmg.value))) : ''; }

  function extraCard() {
    var u = {}, G = R.G;
    u.item = App.combo(App.productPicker({ placeholder: 'Item name or code - or type a new item', onPick: function (it) { if (!it.free) { u.itemCode = it.code; u.per.value = it.pcs > 0 ? it.pcs : 1; u.q.refresh(); } else u.itemCode = ''; }, onInput: function () { u.itemCode = ''; } }));
    u.per = h('input', { class: 'inp sm', type: 'number', min: 1, step: 'any', value: 1 });
    u.q = App.qty({ cls: 'inp sm', perSet: function () { return num(u.per.value) >= 1 ? num(u.per.value) : 1; } });
    u.dmg = h('input', { class: 'inp sm', type: 'number', min: 0, step: 'any', placeholder: '0' });
    u.sup = h('input', { class: 'inp sm', type: 'text', maxlength: 60, placeholder: 'Optional' });
    u.per.addEventListener('input', function () { u.q.refresh(); });
    G.extra.push(u);
    function cell(label, ctl) { return h('div', null, h('label', { text: label }), ctl); }
    var el = h('div', { class: 'line' },
      h('div', { class: 'line-top' }, h('div', { class: 'item' }, h('label', { text: 'Item that arrived but was not ordered' }), u.item.el),
        h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'x rm', 'aria-label': 'Remove', text: '×', onclick: function () { G.extra.splice(G.extra.indexOf(u), 1); el.parentNode.removeChild(el); } })),
      h('div', { class: 'line-nums', style: 'grid-template-columns:repeat(5,minmax(0,1fr));margin-top:12px' }, cell('Pcs / set', u.per), cell('Received pcs', u.q.pcs), cell('or sets', u.q.sets), cell('Damaged pcs', u.dmg), cell('Supplier code', u.sup)));
    return el;
  }

  function save(mark) {
    var G = R.G, d = G.d, f = G.f;
    var lines = G.lines.map(function (u) {
      if (u.L.putAway) return null;
      var q = u.q.get();
      return { id: u.L.id, recSets: q ? q.sets : '', per: num(u.per.value), damaged: num(u.dmg.value), stickered: u.stk.get(), stickeredQty: num(u.sq.value), itemCode: u.itemCode || '' };
    }).filter(Boolean);
    var extra = G.extra.map(function (u) {
      var item = u.item.get(), q = u.q.get();
      if (!item) throw new Error('Pick the extra item, or remove it.');
      if (!q) throw new Error('Enter the quantity of the extra item ' + item.name + '.');
      return { itemCode: item.free ? '' : item.code, desc: item.free ? item.name : '', supItemCode: u.sup.value, per: num(u.per.value), recSets: q.sets, damaged: num(u.dmg.value) };
    });
    var run = function () {
      return App.api('grn.receive', { id: d.id, invoiceNo: f.inv.value, invoiceDate: f.invDate.value, cartons: f.cartons.value, receivedDate: f.recDate.value, markReceived: !!mark, lines: lines, newLines: extra }).then(function () {
        App.toast(mark ? d.no + ' marked as received - now put it away' : 'Saved', 'ok');
        R.drawer.close(); return load();
      });
    };
    if (!mark) return run();
    var n = lines.filter(function (l) { return l.recSets !== ''; }).length + extra.length;
    return App.confirm({ title: 'Mark as received', message: 'The goods have arrived. Mark ' + d.no + ' as received on ' + App.fmtDate(f.recDate.value) + ' (' + n + (n === 1 ? ' item counted' : ' items counted') + ')? It then appears under Put-away.', ok: 'Mark as received' })
      .then(function (yes) { if (yes) return run(); });
  }

  App.register('receiving', 'Receiving', { init: init, show: function () { App.refreshLookups(); load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF ReceivingBoard =====

# FILE 15 of 25 :  PutAwayBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* PutAwayBoard.html - Warehouse: received goods that still need a shelf. Choose the location and the pieces; the stock
   ledger (Stock Movement) gets a "Put-away" line automatically. Putting away only part of a line is fine: the rest stays here. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('warehouse')) return;
  var B = { search: '', seq: 0, rows: [], listBox: null, countBox: null };

  function init(root) {
    var search = PW.search('Search delivery, supplier, item…', 'Search put-away', function (v) { B.search = v; load(); });
    B.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); B.listBox = h('div');
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Put-away' }),
      h('p', { text: 'Goods that have been received and counted. Put each one on a shelf and record where. Damaged pieces are not put away.' }))));
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'Waiting for a location' }), h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), B.countBox, B.listBox));
  }

  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return Promise.all([App.api('put.list', { search: B.search }), PW.locations(), App.products().catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (res) {
      var d = res[0];
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.rows = d.rows;
      PW.badge('putaway', d.rows.length, 'Lines waiting for a location');
      B.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' line' : ' lines') + ' to put away';
      render();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.listBox, 'the put-away list', e); } });
  }

  function rowEl(r) {
    var held = {}; r.where.forEach(function (w) { held[w.loc] = w.qty; });
    var loc = App.combo(PW.locationPicker(held, { placeholder: 'Location, e.g. FL1-ZA' }));
    var first = (PW.locationList || []).filter(function (l) { return l.code === (r.planned || (r.where[0] && r.where[0].loc)); })[0];
    if (first) loc.set(first);
    var pcs = h('input', { class: 'inp sm pw-n', type: 'number', min: 0, step: 'any', value: r.goodPcs, 'aria-label': 'Pieces to put away' });
    var stk = PW.check('Stickered', r.stickered);
    var pick = null;
    if (!r.itemCode) pick = App.combo(App.productPicker({ placeholder: 'Pick the item first', allowFree: false, none: 'Not in Products - add it on the Stock tab (New item)' }));
    var btn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm primary', text: 'Put away' });
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
      App.run(btn, function () {
        var l = loc.get(), item = pick ? pick.get() : null, n = App.num(pcs.value);
        if (pick && (!item || item.free)) { pick.focus(); throw new Error('Pick the item for this line first.'); }
        if (!l || l.free) { loc.focus(); throw new Error('Pick the location from the list.'); }
        if (!(n > 0)) { pcs.focus(); throw new Error('Enter how many pieces to put away.'); }
        return App.api('put.do', { lineId: r.id, location: l.code, pcs: n, itemCode: item ? item.code : '', stickered: stk.get() }).then(function (res) {
          App.toast(App.fmtQty(res.putAway) + ' pcs of ' + r.name + ' put away at ' + res.location + (res.split ? ' - ' + App.fmtQty(res.left) + ' pcs still to put away' : ''), 'ok');
          return load();
        });
      });
    });
    return h('tr', null,
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Delivery' }, h('b', { text: r.grnNo }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: r.supplier }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'Received ' + App.fmtShort(r.receivedDate) })),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Item' }, r.name || 'New item', r.itemCode ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: r.itemCode }) : null, pick ? pick.el : null,
        r.where.length ? h('div', { class: 'pw-locs', style: 'margin-top:6px' }, r.where.map(function (w) { return h('span', { class: 'pw-chip' }, w.loc, h('i', { text: App.fmtQty(w.qty) })); })) : null),
      h('td', { class: 'num', 'data-l': 'To put away' }, App.fmtQty(r.goodPcs) + ' pcs', h('span', { class: 'sub', text: r.per > 1 ? App.fmtQty(r.goodPcs / r.per) + ' sets of ' + App.fmtQty(r.per) : '' }),
        r.damaged ? h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: App.fmtQty(r.damaged) + ' damaged' }) : null),
      h('td', { 'data-l': 'Location' }, loc.el), h('td', { 'data-l': 'Pieces' }, pcs), h('td', { 'data-l': 'Sticker' }, stk.el),
      h('td', { class: 'acts' }, btn));
  }
  function render() {
    App.clear(B.listBox);
    if (!B.rows.length) { B.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('div', { class: 'empty', text: 'Nothing is waiting to be put away.' }))); return; }
    var head = h('tr', null, h('th', { text: 'Delivery' }), h('th', { text: 'Item' }), h('th', { class: 'num', text: 'To put away' }), h('th', { text: 'Location' }), h('th', { text: 'Pieces' }), h('th', { text: 'Sticker' }), h('th', null, h('span', { class: 'sr', text: 'Actions' })));
    var body = h('tbody');
    B.rows.forEach(function (r) { body.appendChild(rowEl(r)); });
    B.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('table', { class: 't', style: 'min-width:1000px' }, h('thead', null, head), body)));
  }

  App.register('putaway', 'Put-away', { init: init, show: function () { App.refreshLookups(); load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF PutAwayBoard =====
