# FILE 16 of 25 :  DispatchBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* DispatchBoard.html - Warehouse: an order has reached us. Pick it, pack it, book the transport, dispatch it.
   The order's stage is the sheet's own: To pick -> To pack -> Book transport -> Ready to dispatch -> Dispatched.
   Dispatching writes a minus line to the stock ledger (Stock Movement) for each shipped line and can be done in parts. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('warehouse')) return;
  var STAGES = ['To pick', 'To pack', 'Book transport', 'Ready to dispatch'];
  var STEP = { 'To pick': 1, 'To pack': 2, 'Book transport': 3, 'Ready to dispatch': 4, 'Dispatched': 5 };
  var D = { filter: 'Open', search: '', stage: '', seq: 0, rows: [], stages: {}, sel: '', detail: null, ui: null, listBox: null, countBox: null, strip: null, md: null, panel: null, panelBox: null };

  function init(root) {
    var search = PW.search('Search order number or customer…', 'Search orders', function (v) { D.search = v; load(); });
    D.strip = h('div', { class: 'stg', role: 'group', 'aria-label': 'Filter orders by stage' });
    D.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); D.listBox = h('div');
    D.panelBox = h('div'); D.panel = h('aside', { class: 'dp', 'aria-label': 'Order details', hidden: true }, D.panelBox);
    D.md = h('div', { class: 'md' }, h('div', null, D.listBox), D.panel);
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Dispatch' }),
      h('p', { text: 'Orders from Sales, in the order they must move: pick, pack, book the transport, then dispatch.' }))));
    root.appendChild(D.strip);
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar', style: 'margin-bottom:6px' }, h('h2', { text: 'Orders' }), h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), D.countBox, D.md));
  }

  // ---------- list ----------
  function load() {
    var seq = ++D.seq;
    D.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return Promise.all([App.api('disp.list', { filter: D.filter, search: D.search }), PW.locations(), App.products().catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (res) {
      var d = res[0];
      if (seq !== D.seq) return;
      D.rows = d.rows; if (D.filter === 'Open' && !D.search) D.stages = d.stages;
      var open = 0; Object.keys(D.stages).forEach(function (k) { open += D.stages[k]; });
      PW.badge('dispatch', open, 'Orders to pick, pack, book or dispatch');
      renderStrip(); renderList();
      if (D.sel) openOrder(D.sel, true);
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === D.seq) { D.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(D.listBox, 'orders', e); } });
  }
  function renderStrip() {
    var total = 0; Object.keys(D.stages).forEach(function (k) { total += D.stages[k]; });
    App.clear(D.strip);
    function card(label, n, on, click, big) {
      var b = h('button', { type: 'button', class: on ? 'on' : '', 'aria-pressed': on ? 'true' : 'false' },
        h('div', { class: 'bar2' }, h('i', { style: 'width:' + (total && n !== null ? Math.round(n / total * 100) : 0) + '%' })), h('small', { text: label }), h('b', { text: big || String(n) }), h('span', { text: ' ' }));
      b.addEventListener('click', click); D.strip.appendChild(b);
    }
    var hist = D.filter === 'Dispatched';
    card('All open', total, !hist && !D.stage, function () { D.filter = 'Open'; D.stage = ''; load(); });
    STAGES.forEach(function (s) { card(s, D.stages[s] || 0, !hist && D.stage === s, function () { var was = D.filter !== 'Open'; D.filter = 'Open'; D.stage = s; if (was) load(); else { renderStrip(); renderList(); } }); });
    card('Dispatched', null, hist, function () { D.filter = 'Dispatched'; D.stage = ''; load(); }, 'History');
  }
  function shown() { return D.rows.filter(function (o) { return !D.stage || o.stage === D.stage; }); }
  function stageBadge(s) { return App.badge(s, s === 'Dispatched' ? 'green' : (s === 'Ready to dispatch' ? 'blue' : (s === 'Cancelled' ? 'red' : 'amber'))); }
  function renderList() {
    var rows = shown();
    D.countBox.textContent = rows.length + (rows.length === 1 ? ' order' : ' orders') + (D.stage ? ' at ' + D.stage : '');
    App.clear(D.listBox);
    if (!rows.length) { D.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('div', { class: 'empty', text: D.filter === 'Open' ? 'No orders are waiting for the warehouse.' : 'No orders found.' }))); return; }
    var head = h('tr', null, h('th', { text: 'Order' }), h('th', { text: 'Customer' }), h('th', { text: 'Progress' }), h('th', { text: 'Transport' }), h('th', null, h('span', { class: 'sr', text: 'Open' })));
    var body = h('tbody');
    rows.forEach(function (o) {
      var n = STEP[o.stage] || 1, bars = [];
      for (var i = 1; i <= 4; i++) bars.push(h('i', { class: i <= n ? 'on' : '' }));
      var no = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'ordno', text: o.no, 'aria-label': 'Open ' + o.no });
      no.addEventListener('click', function () { D.sel = o.id; openOrder(o.id); });
      var tr = h('tr', { class: 'click' + (D.sel === o.id ? ' sel' : ''), 'data-id': o.id },
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Order' }, no, h('span', { class: 'sub', text: App.fmtDate(o.date) })),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Customer' }, o.customer || '–', h('span', { class: 'sub', text: o.lines + (o.lines === 1 ? ' item' : ' items') + (o.delivery ? ' · ' + o.delivery : '') })),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Progress' }, o.stage === 'Dispatched' || o.stage === 'Cancelled' ? stageBadge(o.stage) : [h('div', { class: 'stp', 'aria-hidden': 'true' }, bars), h('span', { class: 'stl', text: o.stage })],
          o.lines ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: o.picked + '/' + o.lines + ' picked · ' + o.packed + '/' + o.lines + ' packed' }) : null),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Transport' }, o.booked ? [o.transporter || 'Booked', o.lr ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'LR ' + o.lr }) : null] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: 'not booked' })),
        h('td', { class: 'acts' }, h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm' + (o.stage === 'Dispatched' ? '' : ' primary'), text: o.stage === 'Dispatched' ? 'View' : 'Open', onclick: function () { D.sel = o.id; openOrder(o.id); } })));
      tr.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('button,a')) return; D.sel = o.id; openOrder(o.id); });
      body.appendChild(tr);
    });
    D.listBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('table', { class: 't' }, h('thead', null, head), body)));
  }

  // ---------- the order panel ----------
  function openOrder(id, quiet) {
    D.sel = id;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(D.listBox.querySelectorAll('tr.click'), function (tr) { tr.classList.toggle('sel', tr.getAttribute('data-id') === id); });
    D.panel.hidden = false; D.md.classList.add('has-panel');
    if (!quiet) App.clear(D.panelBox).appendChild(h('div', { class: 'dp-b', text: 'Loading…' }));
    return App.api('disp.get', { orderId: id }).then(function (d) { if (D.sel === id) renderPanel(d); }).catch(function (e) { App.toast(e.message, 'err'); });
  }
  function closePanel() { D.sel = ''; D.panel.hidden = true; D.md.classList.remove('has-panel'); renderList(); }
  function num(v) { var n = App.num(v); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; }

  function renderPanel(d) {
    D.detail = d; D.ui = { lines: {} };
    var closed = d.stage === 'Dispatched' || d.stage === 'Cancelled', n = STEP[d.stage] || 1;
    var steps = h('div', { class: 'pw-steps' }, ['Pick', 'Pack', 'Book transport', 'Dispatch'].map(function (s, i) {
      return h('span', { class: closed && d.stage === 'Dispatched' ? 'done' : (i + 1 < n ? 'done' : (i + 1 === n ? 'now' : '')), text: s });
    }));
    var lines = h('div', { class: 'pw-lines' }, d.lines.map(function (l) { return lineCard(l, d); }));
    var tr = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 80, value: d.transporter || '', placeholder: 'Transporter / courier', disabled: closed });
    var lr = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 60, value: d.lr || '', placeholder: 'LR / docket no. (when you have it)', disabled: closed });
    var dt = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: App.today(), disabled: closed });
    D.ui.tr = tr; D.ui.lr = lr; D.ui.dt = dt;
    var transport = h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'lbl', style: 'margin-bottom:8px', text: 'Transport' }),
      h('div', { class: 'grid', style: 'grid-template-columns:1fr' }, App.field('Transporter', tr), App.field('LR no.', lr), d.booked && !closed ? App.field('Dispatch date', dt) : null),
      d.booked ? h('div', { class: 'hint', text: 'Transport is booked. Tick the sets to send and press Dispatch.' }) : null);
    var foot = h('div', { class: 'dp-f' });
    if (!closed) {
      var saveBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: 'Save pick & pack' });
      saveBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(saveBtn, function () { return saveFlags().then(function (r) { toastWarn(r); App.toast('Saved', 'ok'); return refresh(); }); }); });
      foot.appendChild(saveBtn);
      if (!d.booked) {
        var bookBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm primary', text: 'Book transport' });
        bookBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(bookBtn, book); });
        foot.appendChild(bookBtn);
      } else {
        var shipBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm primary', text: 'Dispatch' });
        shipBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(shipBtn, dispatch); });
        foot.appendChild(shipBtn);
        if (!d.lines.some(function (l) { return l.dispatchedSets > 0; })) {
          var unBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: 'Change booking' });
          unBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(unBtn, unbook); });
          foot.appendChild(unBtn);
        }
      }
    }
    App.clear(D.panelBox).appendChild(h('div', null,
      h('div', { class: 'dp-h' }, h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'kk', text: 'Order' }), h('h3', { text: d.no }), h('div', { class: 'pw-note', text: d.customer + (d.delivery ? ' · ' + d.delivery : '') })),
        h('div', { style: 'display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center' }, stageBadge(d.stage), h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'x', 'aria-label': 'Close details', onclick: closePanel }, App.icon('close', 18)))),
      h('div', { class: 'dp-b' }, steps, lines, closed ? null : transport,
        closed && d.stage === 'Dispatched' ? h('div', { class: 'note', text: 'Dispatched' + (d.dispatchDate ? ' on ' + App.fmtDate(d.dispatchDate) : '') + (d.transporter ? ' with ' + d.transporter : '') + (d.lr ? ' · LR ' + d.lr : '') + '.' }) : null),
      foot));
    if (window.innerWidth <= 1180 && D.panel.scrollIntoView) D.panel.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: 'smooth' });
  }

  function lineCard(l, d) {
    var u = D.ui.lines[l.id] = { l: l }, done = l.dispatched || (l.sets > 0 && l.open <= 0), booked = d.booked;
    var opts = [{ v: '', label: '- pick from -' }], seen = {};
    l.stock.forEach(function (s) { opts.push({ v: s.loc, label: s.loc + ' · ' + App.fmtQty(s.qty) + ' pcs' }); seen[s.loc] = true; });
    if (l.pickFrom && !seen[l.pickFrom]) opts.push({ v: l.pickFrom, label: l.pickFrom + ' · none recorded' });
    if (!l.stock.length) (PW.locationList || []).forEach(function (x) { if (!seen[x.code] && x.code !== l.pickFrom) opts.push({ v: x.code, label: x.code }); });
    u.from = PW.select(opts, l.pickFrom, 'inp sm');
    u.picked = PW.check('Picked', l.picked); u.packed = PW.check('Packed', l.packed);
    u.from.disabled = done; u.picked.input.disabled = done; u.packed.input.disabled = done;
    u.picked.input.addEventListener('change', function () { if (!u.picked.get()) u.packed.set(false); });
    u.packed.input.addEventListener('change', function () { if (u.packed.get()) u.picked.set(true); });
    u.ship = h('input', { class: 'inp sm', type: 'number', min: 0, step: 'any', value: l.open, 'aria-label': 'Sets to dispatch now' });
    var pcsText = App.fmtQty(l.sets) + ' sets × ' + App.fmtQty(l.pcs) + ' = ' + App.fmtQty(l.sets * l.pcs) + ' pcs';
    return h('div', { class: 'line' },
      h('div', { class: 'pw-head' }, h('div', null, h('b', { text: l.name || 'Item' }), h('div', { class: 'pw-exp', text: (l.itemCode ? l.itemCode + ' · ' : 'No item code · ') + pcsText + (l.dispatchedSets > 0 && !done ? ' · ' + App.fmtQty(l.dispatchedSets) + ' sets already sent' : '') })),
        done ? h('span', { class: 'pw-lock', text: 'Dispatched' }) : null),
      done ? null : h('div', { class: 'line-nums' }, h('div', { style: 'grid-column:span 2' }, h('label', { text: 'Pick from' }), u.from),
        h('div', { class: 'pw-checks', style: 'grid-column:span 2;padding-top:6px' }, u.picked.el, u.packed.el)),
      done || !booked ? null : h('div', { class: 'pw-ship' }, h('div', null, h('label', { text: 'Sets to dispatch now (of ' + App.fmtQty(l.open) + ' open)' }), u.ship)),
      !done && l.pickFrom && l.itemCode && l.atPick < l.open * l.pcs ? h('div', { class: 'hint warn', text: 'Only ' + App.fmtQty(l.atPick) + ' pcs recorded at ' + l.pickFrom + ' (needs ' + App.fmtQty(l.open * l.pcs) + ').' }) : null);
  }

  function flags() {
    return D.detail.lines.filter(function (l) { return !(l.dispatched || (l.sets > 0 && l.open <= 0)); }).map(function (l) {
      var u = D.ui.lines[l.id];
      return { id: l.id, pickFrom: u.from.value, picked: u.picked.get(), packed: u.packed.get() };
    });
  }
  function saveFlags() { return App.api('disp.save', { orderId: D.detail.id, lines: flags() }); }
  function toastWarn(r) { (r && r.warnings || []).forEach(function (w) { App.toast(w, 'warn'); }); }
  function refresh() { return Promise.all([openOrder(D.detail.id, true), load()]); }

  function book() {
    var tr = D.ui.tr.value.trim();
    if (!tr) { D.ui.tr.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the transporter you booked.'); }
    return saveFlags().then(function (r) {
      toastWarn(r);
      return App.api('disp.book', { orderId: D.detail.id, booked: true, transporter: tr, lrNo: D.ui.lr.value });
    }).then(function () { App.toast('Transport booked with ' + tr, 'ok'); return refresh(); });
  }
  function unbook() {
    return App.confirm({ title: 'Change the booking', message: 'Un-book the transport for ' + D.detail.no + '? You can book it again.', ok: 'Un-book' }).then(function (yes) {
      if (!yes) return;
      return App.api('disp.book', { orderId: D.detail.id, booked: false }).then(function () { App.toast('Booking removed', 'ok'); return refresh(); });
    });
  }
  function dispatch() {
    var d = D.detail, send = [];
    d.lines.forEach(function (l) {
      var done = l.dispatched || (l.sets > 0 && l.open <= 0); if (done) return;
      var n = num(D.ui.lines[l.id].ship.value);
      if (n > 0) send.push({ id: l.id, sets: n, name: l.name });
    });
    if (!send.length) throw new Error('Enter the sets to dispatch on at least one line.');
    return saveFlags().then(function (r) {
      toastWarn(r);
      return App.confirm({ title: 'Dispatch', message: 'Dispatch ' + send.length + (send.length === 1 ? ' line' : ' lines') + ' of ' + d.no + ' to ' + d.customer + '? The stock is reduced at the pick locations.', ok: 'Dispatch' });
    }).then(function (yes) {
      if (!yes) return;
      return App.api('disp.dispatch', { orderId: d.id, lines: send.map(function (s) { return { id: s.id, sets: s.sets }; }), lrNo: D.ui.lr.value, transporter: D.ui.tr.value, date: D.ui.dt.value }).then(function (res) {
        App.toast(res.complete ? d.no + ' fully dispatched' : 'Part of ' + d.no + ' dispatched - the rest is still open', 'ok');
        toastWarn(res); return refresh();
      });
    });
  }

  App.register('dispatch', 'Dispatch', { init: init, show: function () { App.refreshLookups(); load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF DispatchBoard =====

# FILE 17 of 25 :  StockBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (unchanged since the earlier version)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* StockBoard.html - Warehouse: what is on the shelves. READ-ONLY: Stock Movement is only ever written by put-away,
   dispatch and an approved audit count. "Books" is Tally's closing stock; "Warehouse" is the sum of the ledger. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('warehouse')) return;
  var LABELS = ['In warehouse', 'Books ≠ warehouse', 'Short for orders', 'All items'];
  var B = { filter: 'In warehouse', search: '', seq: 0, rows: [], counts: {}, nextCode: '', chips: null, listBox: null, countBox: null, drawer: null, f: {} };

  function init(root) {
    B.chips = App.seg({
      cls: 'chips-s', label: 'Filter stock',
      options: [{ v: 'In warehouse', label: LABELS[0] }, { v: 'Mismatch', label: LABELS[1] }, { v: 'Short', label: LABELS[2] }, { v: 'All', label: LABELS[3] }],
      value: B.filter, onChange: function (v) { B.filter = v; load(); }
    });
    var search = PW.search('Search item, code, group, location…', 'Search stock', function (v) { B.search = v; load(); });
    B.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); B.listBox = h('div');
    var newBtn = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn' }, App.icon('plus', 16), 'New item');
    newBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { B.drawer.open(); });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Stock' }),
      h('p', { text: 'What is on the shelves, and where. This list is read-only: stock changes only through put-away, dispatch and approved audit counts.' })), h('div', { class: 'acts' }, newBtn)));
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'Items' }), B.chips.el, h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), B.countBox, B.listBox));
    buildDrawer();
  }

  function buildDrawer() {
    var f = B.f;
    f.code = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 30 }); f.name = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 120, placeholder: 'As it appears in Tally' });
    f.pcs = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'number', min: 1, step: 'any', value: 1 }); f.group = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 60, placeholder: 'Uncategorised' });
    f.gst = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'number', min: 0, max: 100, step: 'any', placeholder: 'e.g. 18' });
    var save = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Add item' });
    save.addEventListener('click', function () {
      App.run(save, function () {
        if (!f.code.value.trim()) { f.code.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the item code.'); }
        if (!f.name.value.trim()) { f.name.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the item name.'); }
        return App.api('prod.create', { code: f.code.value, name: f.name.value, pcs: f.pcs.value, group: f.group.value, gst: f.gst.value }).then(function () {
          App.toast('Item ' + f.code.value + ' added to Products', 'ok');
          ['code', 'name', 'group', 'gst'].forEach(function (k) { f[k].value = ''; }); f.pcs.value = 1; B.drawer.close();
          return App.products(true).then(load);
        });
      });
    });
    B.drawer = App.drawer({
      title: 'New item', sub: 'For goods that are not in Products yet, so they can be put away. Prices and the catalog name are filled in by Sales / Admin.',
      body: h('div', null, App.field('Item code', f.code, { required: true, hint: h('div', { class: 'hint', text: 'Next free number: ' }) }), App.field('Item name', f.name, { required: true }),
        h('div', { class: 'grid' }, App.field('Pcs per set', f.pcs), App.field('GST %', f.gst)), App.field('Stock group', f.group)),
      footer: [save, h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Cancel', onclick: function () { B.drawer.close(); } })],
      onopen: function () { if (!f.code.value) f.code.value = B.nextCode; setTimeout(function () { f.name.focus(); }, 30); }
    });
  }

  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return Promise.all([App.api('stock.list', { filter: B.filter, search: B.search }), App.products().catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (res) {
      var d = res[0];
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.rows = d.rows; B.counts = d.counts; B.nextCode = d.nextCode;
      PW.chipCounts(B.chips, LABELS, [d.counts.inWh, d.counts.mismatch, d.counts.short, 0]);
      B.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' item' : ' items') + (d.shown > d.rows.length ? ' - showing the first ' + d.rows.length + ', search to narrow down' : '');
      render();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.listBox, 'stock', e); } });
  }
  function history(r) {
    return App.api('stock.history', { code: r.code }).then(function (d) {
      var cols = [
        { h: 'When', f: function (m) { return m.at; } }, { h: 'Type', f: function (m) { return m.type; } },
        { h: 'Location', f: function (m) { return m.loc; } },
        { h: 'Pieces', cls: 'num', f: function (m) { return h('span', { class: m.qty < 0 ? 'pw-neg' : 'pw-pos', text: (m.qty > 0 ? '+' : '') + App.fmtQty(m.qty) }); } },
        { h: 'By', f: function (m) { return m.by; } }
      ];
      App.modal({ title: d.name + ' · ' + d.code, wide: true, body: h('div', { class: 'pw-ledger' }, App.table(cols, d.rows, 'Nothing has been recorded for this item yet.'),
        d.total > d.rows.length ? h('p', { class: 'pw-note', text: 'Showing the latest ' + d.rows.length + ' of ' + d.total + ' movements.' }) : null), buttons: [{ label: 'Close' }] });
    });
  }
  function render() {
    var cols = [
      { h: 'Item', f: function (r) { return [h('b', { text: r.name || r.code }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: r.code + (r.group ? ' · ' + r.group : '') })]; } },
      { h: 'Pcs / set', cls: 'num', f: function (r) { return String(r.pcs); } },
      { h: 'Books (Tally)', cls: 'num', f: function (r) { return App.fmtQty(r.books); } },
      { h: 'Warehouse', cls: 'num', f: function (r) { var diff = Math.abs(r.books - r.wh) > 0.5 && (r.books > 0 || r.wh !== 0); return h('span', { class: diff ? 'late' : '', text: App.fmtQty(r.wh) }); } },
      { h: 'Reserved', cls: 'num', f: function (r) { return r.reserved ? App.fmtQty(r.reserved) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'On order', cls: 'num', f: function (r) { return r.onOrder ? App.fmtQty(r.onOrder) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Available', cls: 'num', f: function (r) { return r.avail === '' ? '' : h('span', { class: r.avail < 0 ? 'late' : '', text: App.fmtQty(r.avail) }); } },
      { h: 'Where', f: function (r) { return r.locs.length ? h('div', { class: 'pw-locs' }, r.locs.map(function (l) { return h('span', { class: 'pw-chip' }, l.loc, h('i', { text: App.fmtQty(l.qty) })); })) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: 'not located' }); } },
      { h: '', cls: 'acts', f: function (r) { return h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm link', text: 'History', onclick: function (e) { App.run(e.currentTarget, function () { return history(r); }); } }); } }
    ];
    App.clear(B.listBox).appendChild(App.table(cols, B.rows, B.filter === 'In warehouse' ? 'Nothing has been put away yet.' : 'No items match.'));
    var hint = B.drawer.el.querySelector('.hint'); if (hint) hint.textContent = 'Next free number: ' + B.nextCode;
  }

  App.register('stock', 'Stock', { init: init, show: function () { App.refreshLookups(); load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF StockBoard =====

# FILE 18 of 25 :  AuditBoard    [HTML file]   REPLACES AuditBoard.html from the earlier version (now also loads on the Accounts page) - or NEW
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* AuditBoard.html - stock audit (shared: the Warehouse page and the Accounts page). Warehouse: count what is on a shelf when you are free (pieces) and save it.
   Accounts (and Admin): review each count. Approving makes the ledger match the count (an "Audit adj" line in Stock Movement);
   rejecting changes nothing. System quantity = what the ledger says is at that location. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('warehouse') && !PW.active('accounts')) return;
  var LABELS = ['Pending', 'Approved', 'Rejected', 'All'];
  var B = { filter: 'Pending', search: '', seq: 0, rows: [], counts: {}, chips: null, listBox: null, countBox: null, f: {} };
  function role() { return App.boot && App.boot.user ? App.boot.user.role : ''; }
  function canCount() { return role() === 'Warehouse' || role() === 'Admin'; }
  function canReview() { return role() === 'Accounts' || role() === 'Admin'; }

  function init(root) {
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Stock audit' }),
      h('p', { text: canCount() ? 'When you have a free moment: pick a shelf, count what is really there, and save. Accounts reviews every count.' : 'Review the counts the warehouse has made. Approving fixes the stock record.' }))));
    if (canCount()) root.appendChild(countCard());
    B.chips = App.seg({
      cls: 'chips-s', label: 'Filter counts', options: [{ v: 'Pending', label: LABELS[0] }, { v: 'Approved', label: LABELS[1] }, { v: 'Rejected', label: LABELS[2] }, { v: 'All', label: LABELS[3] }],
      value: B.filter, onChange: function (v) { B.filter = v; load(); }
    });
    var search = PW.search('Search item, location, counted by…', 'Search counts', function (v) { B.search = v; load(); });
    B.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); B.listBox = h('div');
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'Counts' }), B.chips.el, h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), B.countBox, B.listBox));
  }

  function countCard() {
    var f = B.f;
    f.item = App.combo(App.productPicker({ placeholder: 'Item name or code - or type what you found' }));
    f.loc = App.combo(PW.locationPicker(null, { placeholder: 'Shelf / location' }));
    f.qty = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'number', min: 0, step: 'any', inputmode: 'decimal', placeholder: 'Pieces counted' });
    f.file = h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'file', accept: 'image/*', capture: 'environment' });
    var save = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Save count' });
    save.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(save, saveCount); });
    f.qty.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); save.click(); } });
    PW.locations().catch(function () { return null; });
    return h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'padding:20px 24px 22px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead', style: 'margin-bottom:14px' }, h('h2', { text: 'New count' }),
      h('span', { class: 'mute', style: 'font-size:13px', text: 'Count in pieces. Enter 0 if the shelf is empty.' })),
      h('div', { class: 'quick', style: 'grid-template-columns:minmax(240px,2fr) minmax(160px,1fr) 140px minmax(160px,1fr) auto' },
        App.field('Item', f.item.el, { required: true }), App.field('Location', f.loc.el, { required: true }), App.field('Counted (pcs)', f.qty, { required: true }), App.field('Photo (optional)', f.file), save));
  }
  function saveCount() {
    var f = B.f, item = f.item.get(), loc = f.loc.get(), qty = App.num(f.qty.value);
    if (!item) { f.item.focus(); throw new Error('Pick the item, or type what you found.'); }
    if (!loc || loc.free) { f.loc.focus(); throw new Error('Pick the location from the list.'); }
    if (isNaN(qty) || qty < 0) { f.qty.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the counted pieces (0 if empty).'); }
    var file = f.file.files && f.file.files[0];
    return (file ? App.readPhoto(file) : Promise.resolve(null)).then(function (photo) {
      return App.api('audit.create', { itemCode: item.free ? '' : item.code, desc: item.free ? item.name : '', location: loc.code, qty: qty, photo: photo });
    }).then(function () {
      App.toast('Count saved - waiting for review', 'ok');
      f.item.clear(); f.qty.value = ''; f.file.value = ''; f.item.focus();
      B.filter = 'Pending'; B.chips.set('Pending'); return load();
    });
  }

  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return Promise.all([App.api('audit.list', { filter: B.filter, search: B.search }), App.products().catch(function () { return null; })]).then(function (res) {
      var d = res[0];
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.rows = d.rows; B.counts = d.counts;
      PW.badge('audit', d.counts.pending, 'Counts waiting for review');
      PW.chipCounts(B.chips, LABELS, [d.counts.pending, 0, 0, 0]);
      B.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' count' : ' counts');
      render();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.listBox, 'counts', e); } });
  }

  function review(c, decision) {
    var verb = decision === 'Approved' ? 'Approve' : 'Reject';
    var itemPick = null, body;
    if (decision === 'Approved' && !c.itemCode) itemPick = App.combo(App.productPicker({ placeholder: 'Pick the item this count is for', allowFree: false }));
    var msg = decision === 'Approved'
      ? (c.system === null ? 'Approve this count?' : 'Approve this count? The stock record at ' + c.loc + ' goes from ' + App.fmtQty(c.system) + ' to ' + App.fmtQty(c.counted) + ' pcs (' + (c.variance > 0 ? '+' : '') + App.fmtQty(c.variance) + ').')
      : 'Reject this count? The stock record stays as it is.';
    body = h('div', null, h('p', { text: c.name + ' at ' + c.loc + ': counted ' + App.fmtQty(c.counted) + ' pcs.' }), h('p', { text: msg }), itemPick ? App.field('Item', itemPick.el, { required: true }) : null);
    App.modal({
      title: verb + ' count', body: body,
      buttons: [{ label: 'Cancel' }, { label: verb, kind: decision === 'Approved' ? 'primary' : 'danger', onclick: function (api, btn) {
        App.run(btn, function () {
          var it = itemPick ? itemPick.get() : null;
          if (itemPick && (!it || it.free)) { itemPick.focus(); throw new Error('Pick the item first.'); }
          return App.api('audit.review', { id: c.id, decision: decision, itemCode: it ? it.code : '' }).then(function (r) {
            api.close();
            App.toast(decision === 'Approved' ? (r.adjusted ? 'Approved - stock adjusted by ' + (r.adjusted > 0 ? '+' : '') + App.fmtQty(r.adjusted) + ' pcs' : 'Approved - the stock already matched') : 'Rejected', 'ok');
            return load();
          });
        });
      } }]
    });
  }

  function render() {
    var cols = [
      { h: 'Counted', f: function (c) { return [c.at.slice(0, 10) ? App.fmtDate(c.at.slice(0, 10)) : '', h('span', { class: 'sub', text: (c.at.slice(11) ? c.at.slice(11) + ' · ' : '') + c.by })]; } },
      { h: 'Item', f: function (c) { return [h('b', { text: c.name || 'Unnamed item' }), c.itemCode ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: c.itemCode }) : h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: 'No item code yet' })]; } },
      { h: 'Location', f: function (c) { return c.loc; } },
      { h: 'Counted', cls: 'num', f: function (c) { return App.fmtQty(c.counted); } },
      { h: 'System', cls: 'num', f: function (c) { return c.system === null ? h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }) : App.fmtQty(c.system); } },
      { h: 'Difference', cls: 'num', f: function (c) { return c.variance === null ? h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }) : h('span', { class: c.variance === 0 ? 'pw-pos' : (c.variance < 0 ? 'pw-neg' : 'late'), text: c.variance === 0 ? 'matches' : (c.variance > 0 ? '+' : '') + App.fmtQty(c.variance) }); } },
      { h: 'Photo', f: function (c) { return c.photo ? h('a', { href: c.photo, target: '_blank', rel: 'noopener', text: 'View' }) : (c.hasPhoto ? 'On file' : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' })); } },
      { h: 'Status', f: function (c) { return [App.badge(c.status, c.status === 'Approved' ? 'green' : (c.status === 'Rejected' ? 'red' : 'amber')), c.reviewedBy ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: c.reviewedBy + (c.reviewedOn ? ' · ' + App.fmtShort(c.reviewedOn) : '') }) : null]; } },
      { h: '', cls: 'acts', f: function (c) {
          if (c.status !== 'Pending' || !canReview()) return '';
          return [h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm primary', text: 'Approve', onclick: function () { review(c, 'Approved'); } }), ' ',
                  h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm danger', text: 'Reject', onclick: function () { review(c, 'Rejected'); } })];
        } }
    ];
    App.clear(B.listBox).appendChild(App.table(cols, B.rows, B.filter === 'Pending' ? 'No counts are waiting for review.' : 'No counts found.'));
  }

  App.register('audit', 'Stock audit', { init: init, show: function () { App.refreshLookups(); PW.locations().catch(function () { return null; }); load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF AuditBoard =====

# FILE 19 of 25 :  OrderReceiptsBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (Accounts)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* OrderReceiptsBoard.html - Accounts: bill each sales order (after the invoice is made in Tally) and record every rupee received.
   An order is worth its Invoice Amount once billed, until then its value including GST. A part payment is simply another receipt.
   "Payment Cleared On" in the sheet is kept right automatically: it holds the date of the last receipt once the invoice is fully paid. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('accounts')) return;
  var LABELS = ['To invoice', 'To collect', 'Paid', 'All'];
  var B = { filter: 'To invoice', search: '', seq: 0, rows: [], d: null, listBox: null, countBox: null, statBox: null, chips: null, drawer: null, body: null, O: null };

  function init(root) {
    B.chips = App.seg({
      cls: 'chips-s', label: 'Filter orders', value: B.filter,
      options: LABELS.map(function (l) { return { v: l, label: l }; }), onChange: function (v) { B.filter = v; load(); }
    });
    var search = PW.search('Search order, customer, voucher…', 'Search orders', function (v) { B.search = v; load(); });
    B.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); B.listBox = h('div'); B.statBox = h('div', { class: 'stats', style: 'margin:18px 0' });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Order receipts' }),
      h('p', { text: 'Make the invoice in Tally, record it here, then record every payment that comes in. Overdue payments are listed first.' }))));
    root.appendChild(B.statBox);
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'Orders' }), B.chips.el, h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), B.countBox, B.listBox));
    B.body = h('div');
    B.drawer = App.drawer({ title: 'Order', wide: true, body: B.body, footer: [h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Close', onclick: function () { B.drawer.close(); } })] });
  }

  // ---------- list ----------
  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return App.api('ord.list', { filter: B.filter, search: B.search }).then(function (d) {
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.rows = d.rows; B.d = d;
      PW.badge('orderpay', d.counts.toInvoice + d.sum.overdueCount, 'Orders to invoice and overdue payments');
      PW.chipCounts(B.chips, LABELS, [d.counts.toInvoice, d.counts.toCollect, 0, 0]);
      B.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' order' : ' orders') + (d.shown > d.rows.length ? ' (showing ' + d.rows.length + ': search to narrow it down)' : '');
      stats(d); renderList();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.listBox, 'orders', e); } });
  }
  function stats(d) {
    App.clear(B.statBox);
    [PW.stat('Orders to invoice', d.counts.toInvoice, PW.rs(d.sum.toInvoiceValue) + ' not billed yet'),
     PW.stat('To collect', PW.rs(d.sum.outstanding), d.counts.toCollect + (d.counts.toCollect === 1 ? ' invoice open' : ' invoices open')),
     PW.stat('Overdue', PW.rs(d.sum.overdue), d.sum.overdueCount + (d.sum.overdueCount === 1 ? ' invoice' : ' invoices'), d.sum.overdueCount ? 'c' : 'g'),
     PW.stat('Received this month', PW.rs(d.sum.receivedMonth), '', 'g')].forEach(function (n) { B.statBox.appendChild(n); });
  }
  function stateBadge(o) {
    var k = { 'To invoice': 'amber', 'Awaiting payment': 'blue', 'Overdue': 'red', 'Paid': 'green', 'Cancelled': '' }[o.state];
    return App.badge(o.state, k);
  }
  function renderList() {
    var cols = [
      { h: 'Order', f: function (o) {
          var b = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'ordno', text: o.no, 'aria-label': 'Open ' + o.no });
          b.addEventListener('click', function () { openOrder(o.id); });
          return [b, h('span', { class: 'sub', text: App.fmtDate(o.date) + (o.dispatched ? ' · dispatched' : (o.stage ? ' · ' + o.stage : '')) })]; } },
      { h: 'Customer', f: function (o) { return [o.customer || '–', o.terms ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: o.terms }) : null]; } },
      { h: 'Value', cls: 'num', f: function (o) { return o.invoiced ? [h('span', { class: 'pw-money', text: PW.rs(o.invoiceAmount) }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'invoiced' })] : [h('span', { class: 'pw-money', text: PW.rs(o.value) }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'order value' })]; } },
      { h: 'Invoice', f: function (o) { return o.invoiced ? [o.voucher || '–', h('span', { class: 'sub', text: App.fmtDate(o.invoiceDate) })] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Due', f: function (o) { return o.due ? [App.fmtDate(o.due), o.state === 'Overdue' ? h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: o.overdueDays + (o.overdueDays === 1 ? ' day late' : ' days late') }) : null] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Received', cls: 'num', f: function (o) { return o.received ? h('span', { class: 'pw-money pw-in', text: PW.rs(o.received) }) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Balance', cls: 'num', f: function (o) { return o.value > 0 || o.invoiced ? h('span', { class: 'pw-money' + (o.state === 'Overdue' ? ' late' : ''), text: PW.rs(o.balance) }) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Status', f: function (o) { return [stateBadge(o), o.refundDue ? h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: 'refund due' }) : null]; } },
      { h: '', cls: 'acts', f: function (o) {
          var primary = o.state === 'To invoice' ? 'Invoice' : (o.state === 'Awaiting payment' || o.state === 'Overdue' ? 'Receive' : 'View');
          return h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm' + (primary === 'View' ? '' : ' primary'), text: primary, onclick: function () { openOrder(o.id); } }); } }
    ];
    App.clear(B.listBox).appendChild(App.table(cols, B.rows, B.filter === 'To invoice' ? 'Every order has been invoiced.' : (B.filter === 'To collect' ? 'Nothing is waiting to be collected.' : 'No orders found.')));
  }

  // ---------- one order (drawer) ----------
  function openOrder(id) {
    return App.api('ord.get', { id: id }).then(function (o) { B.O = o; render(); B.drawer.open(); }).catch(function (e) { App.toast(e.message, 'err'); });
  }
  function refresh() { return Promise.all([App.api('ord.get', { id: B.O.id }).then(function (o) { B.O = o; render(); }), load()]); }
  function render() {
    var o = B.O, cancelled = o.status === 'Cancelled';
    PW.drawerTitle(B.drawer, o.no, o.customer + (o.terms ? ' · ' + o.terms : ''));
    App.clear(B.body);
    B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'pw-sum' },
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Order value (incl. GST)' }), h('b', { text: PW.rs(o.value, 2) })),
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Invoiced' }), h('b', { text: o.invoiced ? PW.rs(o.invoiceAmount, 2) : 'Not yet' })),
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Received' }), h('b', { text: PW.rs(o.received, 2) })),
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Balance' }), h('b', { class: o.state === 'Overdue' ? 'late' : '', text: PW.rs(o.balance, 2) }))));
    if (cancelled) B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'note', text: o.refundDue ? 'This order is cancelled and ' + PW.rs(o.received, 2) + ' was received. Record the refund below.' : 'This order is cancelled.' }));

    // 1. lines
    var lines = App.table([
      { h: 'Item', f: function (l) { return [l.name, h('span', { class: 'sub', text: l.itemCode })]; } },
      { h: 'Quantity', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return [App.fmtQty(l.sets) + ' sets', h('span', { class: 'sub', text: '× ' + App.fmtQty(l.pcs) + ' pcs' })]; } },
      { h: 'Rate / pc', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return [App.inr(l.price, 2), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: '+ ' + App.fmtQty(App.r2(l.gst * 100)) + '% GST' })]; } },
      { h: 'Amount', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return App.inr(l.amount, 2); } }
    ], o.lineList, '');
    B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '1' }), h('h2', { text: 'What was ordered' }))),
      o.lineList.length ? lines : h('div', { class: 'empty', text: 'This order has no lines yet.' })));

    // 2. billing
    var f = {
      voucher: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 40, value: o.voucher, placeholder: 'Tally voucher number' }),
      date: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: o.invoiceDate || App.today(), max: App.today() }),
      amount: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'number', min: 0, step: '0.01', inputmode: 'decimal', value: o.invoiced ? o.invoiceAmount : (o.value || '') }),
      due: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: o.due || o.suggestedDue || '' })
    };
    var dueTouched = !!o.due;
    f.due.addEventListener('input', function () { dueTouched = true; });
    f.date.addEventListener('change', function () { if (!dueTouched) f.due.value = PW.dueFrom(o.terms, f.date.value); });
    var save = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: o.invoiced ? 'Save changes' : 'Save invoice' });
    save.addEventListener('click', function () {
      App.run(save, function () {
        if (!f.voucher.value.trim()) { f.voucher.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the Tally voucher number.'); }
        var amt = App.num(f.amount.value);
        if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { f.amount.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the invoice amount.'); }
        return App.api('ord.bill', { id: o.id, voucherNo: f.voucher.value, invoiceDate: f.date.value, invoiceAmount: amt, dueDate: f.due.value }).then(function () {
          App.toast('Invoice saved for ' + o.no, 'ok'); return refresh();
        });
      });
    });
    B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '2' }), h('h2', { text: 'Invoice' })),
      o.invoiced ? App.badge('Invoiced ' + App.fmtDate(o.invoiceDate), 'green') : App.badge('Not invoiced', 'amber')),
      cancelled ? h('div', { class: 'empty', text: 'A cancelled order is not invoiced.' }) : [
        h('div', { class: 'grid' }, App.field('Tally voucher no.', f.voucher, { required: true }), App.field('Invoice date', f.date, { required: true }),
          App.field('Invoice amount (Rs, incl. GST)', f.amount, { required: true, hint: h('span', { class: 'pw-note', text: 'Order value is ' + PW.rs(o.value, 2) }) }),
          App.field('Payment due on', f.due, { hint: h('span', { class: 'pw-note', text: o.terms ? 'Terms: ' + o.terms : 'No payment terms on the order' }) })),
        h('div', { class: 'actions' }, save)]));

    // 3. receipts
    var recv = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: cancelled ? 'Record refund' : 'Receive money' });
    recv.addEventListener('click', function () { PW.payDialog({ kind: 'Order', id: o.id, no: o.no, party: o.customer, balance: o.balance, onDone: refresh }); });
    var pays = o.paymentList.map(function (p) {
      return h('tr', null, h('td', { 'data-l': 'Date', text: App.fmtDate(p.date) }),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Amount', class: 'num' }, h('span', { class: p.direction === 'Received' ? 'pw-in' : 'pw-out', text: (p.direction === 'Received' ? '' : '− ') + PW.rs(p.amount, 2) }), p.direction === 'Paid' ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'refund' }) : null),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'How', text: p.mode }), h('td', { 'data-l': 'Reference' }, p.referenceNo || h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }), p.notes ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: p.notes }) : null),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'By', text: p.by }),
        h('td', { class: 'acts' }, h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: 'Correct', onclick: function () { PW.payDialog({ kind: 'Order', id: o.id, no: o.no, party: o.customer, balance: null, pay: p, onDone: refresh }); } })));
    });
    B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '3' }), h('h2', { text: 'Money received' })), recv),
      pays.length ? h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('table', { class: 't' }, h('thead', null, h('tr', null, h('th', { text: 'Date' }), h('th', { class: 'num', text: 'Amount' }), h('th', { text: 'How' }), h('th', { text: 'Reference' }), h('th', { text: 'Entered by' }), h('th', null, h('span', { class: 'sr', text: 'Actions' })))), h('tbody', null, pays)))
        : h('div', { class: 'empty', text: 'Nothing received yet.' }),
      o.clearedOn ? h('p', { class: 'pw-note', style: 'margin:12px 0 0', text: 'Fully paid: payment cleared on ' + App.fmtDate(o.clearedOn) + '.' }) : null));
  }

  App.register('orderpay', 'Order receipts', { init: init, show: function () { load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF OrderReceiptsBoard =====

# FILE 20 of 25 :  PurchaseReceiptsBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (Accounts)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----

<script>
/* PurchaseReceiptsBoard.html - Accounts: the supplier side. Each delivery (GRN) gets its bill (Bill Amount + Tally purchase voucher),
   then every payment to the supplier is recorded against it. A part payment is simply another payment. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('accounts')) return;
  var LABELS = ['To bill', 'To pay', 'Paid', 'Not received', 'All'];
  var B = { filter: 'To bill', search: '', seq: 0, rows: [], d: null, listBox: null, countBox: null, statBox: null, chips: null, drawer: null, body: null, G: null };

  function init(root) {
    B.chips = App.seg({
      cls: 'chips-s', label: 'Filter deliveries', value: B.filter,
      options: LABELS.map(function (l) { return { v: l, label: l }; }), onChange: function (v) { B.filter = v; load(); }
    });
    var search = PW.search('Search GRN, PO, supplier, invoice, voucher…', 'Search deliveries', function (v) { B.search = v; load(); });
    B.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); B.listBox = h('div'); B.statBox = h('div', { class: 'stats', style: 'margin:18px 0' });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Purchase receipts' }),
      h('p', { text: 'When goods arrive, enter the supplier’s bill from Tally. Then record each payment you make against it.' }))));
    root.appendChild(B.statBox);
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'Deliveries' }), B.chips.el, h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), B.countBox, B.listBox));
    B.body = h('div');
    B.drawer = App.drawer({ title: 'Delivery', wide: true, body: B.body, footer: [h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Close', onclick: function () { B.drawer.close(); } })] });
  }

  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return App.api('bill.list', { filter: B.filter, search: B.search }).then(function (d) {
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.rows = d.rows; B.d = d;
      PW.badge('billpay', d.counts.toBill + d.sum.overdueCount, 'Deliveries to bill and overdue supplier bills');
      PW.chipCounts(B.chips, LABELS, [d.counts.toBill, d.counts.toPay, 0, 0, 0]);
      B.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' delivery' : ' deliveries') + (d.shown > d.rows.length ? ' (showing ' + d.rows.length + ': search to narrow it down)' : '');
      App.clear(B.statBox);
      [PW.stat('Received, not billed', d.counts.toBill, 'deliveries waiting for the supplier’s bill'),
       PW.stat('To pay', PW.rs(d.sum.payable), d.counts.toPay + (d.counts.toPay === 1 ? ' bill open' : ' bills open')),
       PW.stat('Overdue', PW.rs(d.sum.overdue), d.sum.overdueCount + (d.sum.overdueCount === 1 ? ' bill' : ' bills'), d.sum.overdueCount ? 'c' : 'g'),
       PW.stat('Paid this month', PW.rs(d.sum.paidMonth), '', 'g')].forEach(function (n) { B.statBox.appendChild(n); });
      renderList();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.listBox, 'deliveries', e); } });
  }
  function badge(b) {
    return App.badge(b.state, { 'To bill': 'amber', 'To pay': 'blue', 'Overdue': 'red', 'Paid': 'green', 'Not received': '' }[b.state]);
  }
  function renderList() {
    var cols = [
      { h: 'Delivery', f: function (b) {
          var o = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'ordno', text: b.no, 'aria-label': 'Open ' + b.no });
          o.addEventListener('click', function () { openBill(b.id); });
          return [o, h('span', { class: 'sub', text: b.poNo ? 'PO ' + b.poNo : 'No PO' })]; } },
      { h: 'Supplier', f: function (b) { return [b.supplier || '–', b.terms ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: b.terms }) : null]; } },
      { h: 'Received', f: function (b) { return b.receivedDate ? App.fmtDate(b.receivedDate) : (b.received ? 'Yes' : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: 'Not yet' })); } },
      { h: 'Supplier invoice', f: function (b) { return b.invoiceNo ? [b.invoiceNo, b.invoiceDate ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: App.fmtDate(b.invoiceDate) }) : null] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Bill', cls: 'num', f: function (b) { return b.billAmount !== null ? [h('span', { class: 'pw-money', text: PW.rs(b.billAmount) }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: b.voucher })] : [h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }), b.recValue || b.poValue ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: (b.received && b.recValue ? 'received ' + PW.rs(b.recValue) : 'PO ' + PW.rs(b.poValue)) }) : null]; } },
      { h: 'Paid', cls: 'num', f: function (b) { return b.paid ? h('span', { class: 'pw-money pw-out', text: PW.rs(b.paid) }) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Balance', cls: 'num', f: function (b) { return b.balance === null ? h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }) : h('span', { class: 'pw-money' + (b.state === 'Overdue' ? ' late' : ''), text: PW.rs(b.balance) }); } },
      { h: 'Due', f: function (b) { return b.due && b.state !== 'Paid' ? [App.fmtDate(b.due), b.state === 'Overdue' ? h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: b.overdueDays + (b.overdueDays === 1 ? ' day late' : ' days late') }) : null] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Status', f: function (b) { return badge(b); } },
      { h: '', cls: 'acts', f: function (b) {
          var primary = b.state === 'To bill' ? 'Add bill' : (b.state === 'To pay' || b.state === 'Overdue' ? 'Pay' : 'View');
          return h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm' + (primary === 'View' ? '' : ' primary'), text: primary, onclick: function () { openBill(b.id); } }); } }
    ];
    App.clear(B.listBox).appendChild(App.table(cols, B.rows, B.filter === 'To bill' ? 'Every received delivery has its bill.' : (B.filter === 'To pay' ? 'No supplier bill is waiting for payment.' : 'No deliveries found.')));
  }

  // ---------- one delivery (drawer) ----------
  function openBill(id) {
    return App.api('bill.get', { id: id }).then(function (g) { B.G = g; render(); B.drawer.open(); }).catch(function (e) { App.toast(e.message, 'err'); });
  }
  function refresh() { return Promise.all([App.api('bill.get', { id: B.G.id }).then(function (g) { B.G = g; render(); }), load()]); }
  function render() {
    var g = B.G, billed = g.billAmount !== null;
    PW.drawerTitle(B.drawer, g.no + (g.poNo ? ' · PO ' + g.poNo : ''), g.supplier + (g.terms ? ' · ' + g.terms : ''));
    App.clear(B.body);
    B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'pw-sum' },
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Value of the order' }), h('b', { text: PW.rs(g.poValue, 2) })),
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Value of goods received' }), h('b', { text: g.received ? PW.rs(g.recValue, 2) : 'Not received' })),
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Supplier bill' }), h('b', { text: billed ? PW.rs(g.billAmount, 2) : 'Not entered' })),
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Paid' }), h('b', { text: PW.rs(g.paid, 2) })),
      h('div', null, h('span', { text: 'Balance' }), h('b', { class: g.state === 'Overdue' ? 'late' : '', text: billed ? PW.rs(g.balance, 2) : '–' }))));
    if (g.damaged > 0) B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'note', text: g.damaged + ' pieces arrived damaged. Check whether the supplier’s bill gives credit for them.' }));
    if (!g.received && !billed) B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'note', text: 'The goods have not arrived yet. You can still pay an advance below.' }));

    var lines = App.table([
      { h: 'Item', f: function (l) { return [l.name, h('span', { class: 'sub', text: l.itemCode })]; } },
      { h: 'Ordered / received', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return [App.fmtQty(l.expSets) + ' / ' + (l.recSets === null ? '–' : App.fmtQty(l.recSets)) + ' sets', h('span', { class: 'sub', text: '× ' + App.fmtQty(l.per) + ' pcs' + (l.damaged ? ' · ' + l.damaged + ' damaged' : '') })]; } },
      { h: 'Rate / pc', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return l.rate ? [App.inr(l.rate, 2), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: '+ ' + App.fmtQty(App.r2(l.gst * 100)) + '% GST' })] : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } },
      { h: 'Value', cls: 'num', f: function (l) { return l.amount ? App.inr(l.amount, 2) : h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }); } }
    ], g.lineList, '');
    B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '1' }), h('h2', { text: 'What arrived' }))),
      g.lineList.length ? lines : h('div', { class: 'empty', text: 'No lines on this delivery.' })));

    var f = {
      amount: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'number', min: 0, step: '0.01', inputmode: 'decimal', value: billed ? g.billAmount : (g.recValue || g.poValue || '') }),
      voucher: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 40, value: g.voucher, placeholder: 'Tally purchase voucher number' }),
      invNo: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'text', maxlength: 60, value: g.invoiceNo, placeholder: 'Number on the supplier’s invoice' }),
      invDate: h('input', { class: 'inp', type: 'date', value: g.invoiceDate })
    };
    var save = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: billed ? 'Save changes' : 'Save bill' });
    save.addEventListener('click', function () {
      App.run(save, function () {
        var amt = App.num(f.amount.value);
        if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { f.amount.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the bill amount.'); }
        if (!f.voucher.value.trim()) { f.voucher.focus(); throw new Error('Enter the Tally purchase voucher number.'); }
        return App.api('bill.save', { id: g.id, billAmount: amt, voucherNo: f.voucher.value, invoiceNo: f.invNo.value, invoiceDate: f.invDate.value }).then(function () {
          App.toast('Bill saved for ' + g.no, 'ok'); return refresh();
        });
      });
    });
    B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '2' }), h('h2', { text: 'Supplier bill' })),
      billed ? App.badge('Billed', 'green') : App.badge('Not billed', 'amber')),
      h('div', { class: 'grid' }, App.field('Bill amount (Rs, incl. GST)', f.amount, { required: true, hint: h('span', { class: 'pw-note', text: g.received && g.recValue ? 'Goods received are worth ' + PW.rs(g.recValue, 2) : 'Order value is ' + PW.rs(g.poValue, 2) }) }),
        App.field('Tally purchase voucher no.', f.voucher, { required: true }), App.field('Supplier invoice no.', f.invNo), App.field('Supplier invoice date', f.invDate)),
      h('div', { class: 'actions' }, save)));

    var pay = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn primary', text: 'Pay supplier' });
    pay.addEventListener('click', function () { PW.payDialog({ kind: 'GRN', id: g.id, no: g.no, party: g.supplier, balance: billed ? g.balance : null, onDone: refresh }); });
    var pays = g.paymentList.map(function (p) {
      return h('tr', null, h('td', { 'data-l': 'Date', text: App.fmtDate(p.date) }),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'Amount', class: 'num' }, h('span', { class: p.direction === 'Paid' ? 'pw-out' : 'pw-in', text: (p.direction === 'Paid' ? '' : '+ ') + PW.rs(p.amount, 2) }), p.direction === 'Received' ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: 'refund' }) : null),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'How', text: p.mode }), h('td', { 'data-l': 'Reference' }, p.referenceNo || h('span', { class: 'dash', text: '–' }), p.notes ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: p.notes }) : null),
        h('td', { 'data-l': 'By', text: p.by }),
        h('td', { class: 'acts' }, h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: 'Correct', onclick: function () { PW.payDialog({ kind: 'GRN', id: g.id, no: g.no, party: g.supplier, balance: null, pay: p, onDone: refresh }); } })));
    });
    B.body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card', style: 'margin-top:16px' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', { class: 'step' }, h('i', { text: '3' }), h('h2', { text: 'Payments to the supplier' })), pay),
      pays.length ? h('div', { class: 'tablewrap' }, h('table', { class: 't' }, h('thead', null, h('tr', null, h('th', { text: 'Date' }), h('th', { class: 'num', text: 'Amount' }), h('th', { text: 'How' }), h('th', { text: 'Reference' }), h('th', { text: 'Entered by' }), h('th', null, h('span', { class: 'sr', text: 'Actions' })))), h('tbody', null, pays)))
        : h('div', { class: 'empty', text: 'Nothing paid yet.' })));
  }

  App.register('billpay', 'Purchase receipts', { init: init, show: function () { load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF PurchaseReceiptsBoard =====

# FILE 21 of 25 :  PaymentsBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (Accounts)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* PaymentsBoard.html - Accounts: every rupee in and out, newest first. Receipts come from the Order receipts tab and payments from the
   Purchase receipts tab; this page is for looking things up, correcting a wrong entry and taking a copy into a spreadsheet.
   Only an Admin can delete a payment. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('accounts')) return;
  var LABELS = ['All', 'Received', 'Paid'];
  var B = { dir: 'All', search: '', from: '', to: '', seq: 0, rows: [], d: null, listBox: null, countBox: null, statBox: null, chips: null, fromBox: null, toBox: null };
  function isAdmin() { return App.boot && App.boot.user && App.boot.user.role === 'Admin'; }

  function init(root) {
    B.chips = App.seg({ cls: 'chips-s', label: 'Filter payments', value: B.dir, options: LABELS.map(function (l) { return { v: l, label: l === 'Received' ? 'Money in' : (l === 'Paid' ? 'Money out' : 'All') }; }), onChange: function (v) { B.dir = v; load(); } });
    var search = PW.search('Search order, customer, supplier, reference…', 'Search payments', function (v) { B.search = v; load(); });
    B.fromBox = h('input', { class: 'inp pw-date', type: 'date', 'aria-label': 'From date' }); B.toBox = h('input', { class: 'inp pw-date', type: 'date', 'aria-label': 'To date' });
    B.fromBox.addEventListener('change', function () { B.from = B.fromBox.value; load(); }); B.toBox.addEventListener('change', function () { B.to = B.toBox.value; load(); });
    var month = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'This month', onclick: function () {
      var t = App.today(); B.from = t.slice(0, 8) + '01'; B.to = t; B.fromBox.value = B.from; B.toBox.value = B.to; load(); } });
    var all = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'All dates', onclick: function () { B.from = B.to = ''; B.fromBox.value = B.toBox.value = ''; load(); } });
    var dl = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Download', onclick: download });
    B.countBox = h('div', { class: 'count' }); B.listBox = h('div'); B.statBox = h('div', { class: 'stats', style: 'margin:18px 0' });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Payments' }),
      h('p', { text: 'All money received from customers and paid to suppliers. Correct a wrong amount, date or reference here.' }))));
    root.appendChild(B.statBox);
    root.appendChild(h('div', null, h('div', { class: 'toolbar' }, h('h2', { text: 'Ledger' }), B.chips.el, h('div', { class: 'sx grow' }, App.icon('search'), search)),
      h('div', { class: 'toolbar', style: 'margin-top:10px' }, h('span', { class: 'pw-note', text: 'From' }), B.fromBox, h('span', { class: 'pw-note', text: 'to' }), B.toBox, month, all, h('span', { class: 'grow' }), dl,
        h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load })), B.countBox, B.listBox));
  }
  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.countBox.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return App.api('pay.list', { direction: B.dir, search: B.search, from: B.from, to: B.to }).then(function (d) {
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.rows = d.rows; B.d = d;
      B.countBox.textContent = d.shown + (d.shown === 1 ? ' payment' : ' payments') + (d.shown > d.rows.length ? ' (showing the latest ' + d.rows.length + ': narrow the dates)' : '');
      App.clear(B.statBox);
      [PW.stat('Money in', PW.rs(d.totals.received, 2), 'from customers', 'g'), PW.stat('Money out', PW.rs(d.totals.paid, 2), 'to suppliers'),
       PW.stat('Net', (d.totals.net < 0 ? '− ' : '') + PW.rs(Math.abs(d.totals.net), 2), d.totals.net < 0 ? 'more went out than came in' : 'in minus out', d.totals.net < 0 ? 'c' : '')].forEach(function (n) { B.statBox.appendChild(n); });
      render();
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.countBox.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.listBox, 'payments', e); } });
  }
  function download() {
    if (!B.rows.length) { App.toast('Nothing to download.', 'err'); return; }
    PW.csv('payments-' + App.today() + '.csv', [
      { h: 'Date', f: function (p) { return p.date; } }, { h: 'Direction', f: function (p) { return p.direction; } }, { h: 'For', f: function (p) { return p.refNo; } },
      { h: 'Party', f: function (p) { return p.party; } }, { h: 'Amount', f: function (p) { return p.amount; } }, { h: 'Mode', f: function (p) { return p.mode; } },
      { h: 'Reference', f: function (p) { return p.referenceNo; } }, { h: 'Entered by', f: function (p) { return p.by; } }, { h: 'Notes', f: function (p) { return p.notes; } }], B.rows);
  }
  function correct(p) {
    PW.payDialog({ kind: p.refType, id: p.refId, no: p.refNo || p.refId, party: p.party, balance: null, pay: p, onDone: load });
  }
  function remove(p) {
    App.confirm({ title: 'Delete payment', message: 'Delete the ' + PW.rs(p.amount, 2) + ' ' + p.direction.toLowerCase() + ' on ' + App.fmtDate(p.date) + ' for ' + (p.refNo || p.refId) + '? This cannot be undone.', ok: 'Delete', danger: true }).then(function (yes) {
      if (!yes) return null;
      return App.api('pay.delete', { id: p.id }).then(function () { App.toast('Payment deleted', 'ok'); return load(); }).catch(function (e) { App.toast(e.message, 'err'); });
    });
  }
  function render() {
    var cols = [
      { h: 'Date', f: function (p) { return App.fmtDate(p.date); } },
      { h: 'In / out', f: function (p) { return App.badge(p.direction === 'Received' ? 'Money in' : 'Money out', p.direction === 'Received' ? 'green' : 'amber'); } },
      { h: 'For', f: function (p) { return [p.refNo || h('span', { class: 'dash', text: 'Unknown ' + p.refType }), h('span', { class: 'sub', text: (p.refType === 'Order' ? 'Order' : 'Delivery') + (p.party ? ' · ' + p.party : '') })]; } },
      { h: 'Amount', cls: 'num', f: function (p) { return h('span', { class: 'pw-money ' + (p.direction === 'Received' ? 'pw-in' : 'pw-out'), text: PW.rs(p.amount, 2) }); } },
      { h: 'How', f: function (p) { return [p.mode, p.referenceNo ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: p.referenceNo }) : null]; } },
      { h: 'Entered by', f: function (p) { return [p.by, p.notes ? h('span', { class: 'sub', text: p.notes }) : null]; } },
      { h: '', cls: 'acts', f: function (p) {
          var out = [h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm', text: 'Correct', onclick: function () { correct(p); } })];
          if (isAdmin()) out.push(' ', h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn sm danger', text: 'Delete', onclick: function () { remove(p); } }));
          return out; } }
    ];
    App.clear(B.listBox).appendChild(App.table(cols, B.rows, 'No payments match.'));
  }
  App.register('payments', 'Payments', { init: init, show: function () { load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF PaymentsBoard =====

# FILE 22 of 25 :  OverviewBoard    [HTML file]   NEW (Admin)
# first line of this file is:  <script>
# ----- paste everything BELOW this line, up to the END marker -----
##############################################################################
<script>
/* OverviewBoard.html - Admin: where every department stands, what looks wrong, and a check that the sheet still has the tabs and
   columns the app needs. Read-only: fix things in the department that owns them (the links open that department). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var App = window.App, h = App.h, PW = window.PW;
  if (!PW.active('admin')) return;
  var B = { seq: 0, cardBox: null, exBox: null, chkBox: null, stamp: null };
  var DEPTS = ['Procurement', 'Warehouse', 'Accounts', 'Admin'];

  function init(root) {
    B.cardBox = h('div'); B.exBox = h('div'); B.chkBox = h('div'); B.stamp = h('div', { class: 'count' });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ph' }, h('div', null, h('h1', { text: 'Overview' }),
      h('p', { text: 'A look across the whole business: what is waiting in each department, and anything that looks wrong.' })),
      h('div', { class: 'acts' }, h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Refresh', onclick: load }))));
    root.appendChild(B.stamp);
    root.appendChild(B.cardBox);
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'pw-grp', text: 'Needs attention' }));
    root.appendChild(B.exBox);
    var check = h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', text: 'Run sheet check' });
    check.addEventListener('click', function () { App.run(check, runCheck); });
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'pw-grp', text: 'Sheet check' }));
    root.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card' }, h('div', { class: 'chead' }, h('div', null, h('h2', { text: 'Tabs and columns' }), h('p', { class: 'pw-note', style: 'margin:4px 0 0', text: 'Checks that every tab the app uses exists and has the columns it needs.' })), check), B.chkBox));
  }
  function load() {
    var seq = ++B.seq;
    B.stamp.textContent = 'Loading…';
    return App.api('adm.overview').then(function (d) {
      if (seq !== B.seq) return;
      B.stamp.textContent = 'As of ' + App.fmtDate(d.today) + '. Several tabs are read to build this page, so it takes a few seconds.';
      var high = d.exceptions.filter(function (e) { return e.sev === 'high'; }).length;
      PW.badge('overview', high, 'Things that look wrong');
      cards(d); exceptions(d);
    }).catch(function (e) { if (seq === B.seq) { B.stamp.textContent = ''; PW.loadError(B.exBox, 'the overview', e); } });
  }
  function cards(d) {
    App.clear(B.cardBox);
    DEPTS.forEach(function (dep) {
      var list = d.cards.filter(function (c) { return c.dept === dep; });
      if (!list.length) return;
      var url = PW.deptUrl(dep), grid = h('div', { class: 'stats' });
      list.forEach(function (c) { grid.appendChild(PW.stat(c.title, c.money ? PW.rs(c.value) : c.value, c.sub)); });
      B.cardBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'pw-grp' }, dep, url ? [' ', h('a', { href: url, target: '_top', style: 'text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-weight:600', text: 'open →' })] : null));
      B.cardBox.appendChild(grid);
    });
  }
  function exceptions(d) {
    App.clear(B.exBox);
    if (!d.exceptions.length) { B.exBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'card' }, h('div', { class: 'empty', text: 'Nothing looks wrong right now.' }))); return; }
    d.exceptions.forEach(function (e) {
      var url = PW.deptUrl(e.dept);
      B.exBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'pw-ex ' + e.sev },
        h('h3', null, e.title, ' ', App.badge(e.dept, e.sev === 'high' ? 'red' : (e.sev === 'med' ? 'amber' : 'blue')), url ? h('a', { href: url, target: '_top', style: 'font-weight:600;font-size:13px', text: 'open ' + e.dept + ' →' }) : null),
        h('ul', null, e.items.map(function (t) { return h('li', { text: t }); })), e.more ? h('div', { class: 'more', text: '… and ' + e.more + ' more' }) : null));
    });
  }
  function runCheck() {
    return App.api('adm.check').then(function (d) {
      var bad = d.tables.filter(function (t) { return !t.ok; }).length;
      App.clear(B.chkBox);
      B.chkBox.appendChild(h('div', { class: bad ? 'note err' : 'note', style: bad ? '' : 'background:var(--ok-l);color:var(--ok)', text: bad ? bad + (bad === 1 ? ' tab has a problem.' : ' tabs have problems.') : 'All ' + d.tables.length + ' tabs look right.' }));
      B.chkBox.appendChild(App.table([
        { h: 'Tab', f: function (t) { return t.sheet; } },
        { h: 'Rows', cls: 'num', f: function (t) { return String(t.rows); } },
        { h: 'Result', f: function (t) { return t.ok ? App.badge('OK', 'green') : [App.badge('Problem', 'red'), h('span', { class: 'sub late', text: t.error || ('Missing columns: ' + t.missing.join(', ')) })]; } }
      ], d.tables, ''));
    });
  }
  App.register('overview', 'Overview', { init: init, show: function () { load(); } });
})();
</script>
# ===== END OF OverviewBoard =====
